### [1] [Exact branch-transfer criterion for common-mode Thomson heat cancellation in thermoelectric couples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27519)
*Peng Kang,Da Wan,Shulin Bai,Wei Yin,Peng Wang,Chenglong Wen,Zhen Li,Yu Liu,Lei Zheng,Li-Dong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究热电制冷器中 p 型和 n 型腿（leg）的配对标准。作者将热电腿系数分解为差动热电功率（differential thermopower）和共同热电功率（common thermopower），证明在连通稳态标量热电网络中，温度无关的共移（co-shift）是精确的终端零点，而温度依赖的扰动才产生物理效应，由此给出新的材料配对与器件测试判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，热电 p 型和 n 型腿通过匹配其塞贝克系数（Seebeck coefficient）幅值来配对，但制冷器实际响应的是通过其完整电热网络传输的热。这种简化的配对方式忽略了网络拓扑与共同模式（common-mode）效应，需要更精确的分析框架。

Method: 将腿系数分解为差动热电功率 α=S_p−S_n 与共同热电功率 M=(S_p+S_n)/2；在连通稳态标量热电网络中，证明温度无关的共移是精确终端零零（exact terminal null），温度依赖的扰动则是物理的。通过一阶冷端响应公式及 Γ_m=T·dm/dT 算子，推导共同模式汤普森热（common-mode Thomson heat）的消除条件，并用常数性质线性共同模式极限和一维、二维计算验证。

Result: 证明每条连续 Γ_m 被消除当且仅当 p 与 n 分支定向集合测度相等；在常数性质线性共同模式极限下，匹配 R_i/K_i^leg 即充分条件且不要求腿完全相同。数值计算：七对模型给出 7.87 mW 和 −2.80 mV 的增量；分裂热垫给出精确阵列定律，串联元件给出 IΔV_Σ=−ΔQ_{c,Σ}。

Conclusion: 分支传递（branch transfer）与端点拓扑（endpoint topology）为共同模式汤普森热提供了与以往不同的材料配对和器件测试判据。常用匹配 Seebeck 幅值的做法并非充分条件，需考虑完整电热网络的共同模式效应。

Abstract: Thermoelectric p- and n-type legs are commonly paired by matching their Seebeck magnitudes, although a cooler responds to heat transported through its complete electrical and thermal network. We decompose the leg coefficients into differential thermopower $α=S_p-S_n$ and common thermopower $M=(S_p+S_n)/2$. In a connected steady-state scalar thermoelectric network, a temperature-independent co-shift applied to every electrically active segment is an exact terminal null. A temperature-dependent perturbation of the legs relative to fixed leads is instead physical. At fixed current and shared isothermal endpoints, its first-order cold-port response is the action of $Γ_m=T\,dm/dT$ on the difference between the p- and n-branch oriented collection measures. We prove that every continuous $Γ_m$ cancels if and only if these measures are equal. In the constant-property, linear-common-mode limit, matching $R_i/K_i^{\rm leg}$ is sufficient and does not require identical legs. One- and two-dimensional calculations confirm the analytic reductions within their stated domains. For split thermal pads, the analysis gives the exact array law $ΔQ_{c,Σ}=\sum_j C_jI_jΔT_{c,j}$ and, for series elements with isothermal hot pairs, $IΔV_Σ=-ΔQ_{c,Σ}$. A representative seven-pair model gives corresponding increments of 7.87 mW and $-2.80$ mV. Branch transfer and endpoint topology therefore provide distinct material-pairing and device-test criteria for common-mode Thomson heat.

</details>


### [2] [Ab initio Modeling of MoS2/Oxide Device Interfaces with Machine Learned Electronic Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27533)
*Manasa Kaniselvan,Mauro Dossena,Denghui Lu,Alexander Maeder,Nicolas Vetsch,Alexandros Nikolaos Ziogas,Mathieu Luisier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的从头算（ab initio）方法模拟半导体器件，将可扩展的机器学习电子结构模型与先进量子输运（quantum transport, QT）求解器相结合，实现对超过20,000个原子器件的哈密顿矩阵计算，较密度泛函理论（DFT）提速10,000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密度泛函理论计算半导体器件哈密顿矩阵的计算成本极高，限制了量子输运模拟在大规模器件上的应用。因此需要开发一种既能保持高预测精度又能大幅提升计算效率的从头算模拟框架。

Method: 将可扩展的机器学习（machine-learned, ML）电子结构模型与先进的量子输运求解器集成，形成统一的从头算模拟框架，用于直接生成包含超过20,000个原子器件的哈密顿矩阵。

Result: 该框架较密度泛函理论实现了10,000倍计算加速，同时保持高预测精度。利用该框架研究了MoS2/氧化物样品和单层MoS2场效应晶体管（field-effect transistor），其中HfO2或Al2O3氧化物层被显式纳入量子输运域。研究发现，靠近半导体-氧化物界面的欠配位金属原子（Hf或Al）显著影响电子电流的大小及其在MoS2中的传播。

Conclusion: 该从头算框架为大规模半导体器件模拟提供了高效且准确的工具，并揭示了界面欠配位金属原子对电子输运性质的关键影响，为器件设计与界面工程提供了新的物理见解。

Abstract: We introduce a new ab initio approach to simulate semiconductor devices that integrates scalable machine-learned (ML) electronic structure models with an advanced quantum transport (QT) solver. The developed framework enables 10,000X speedups over density functional theory to produce the Hamiltonian matrix of devices made of >20,000 atoms, while offering high prediction accuracy. We use its unique features to investigate MoS2/oxide samples and single-layer MoS2 field-effect transistors, where the surrounding oxide layers, here, HfO2 or Al2O3, are explicitly included into the QT domain. In particular, we reveal that the presence of undercoordinated metal atoms (Hf or Al) close to the semiconductor-oxide interface significantly affects the magnitude of the electronic current and its propagation through MoS2.

</details>


### [3] [Compiling Chemical Knowledge into Executable Descriptors for Materials Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27587)
*Jaehwan Choi,Kunik Jang,Seongmin Kim,Shuan Chen,Kyungju Nam,Seung Hyo Noh,Donghwi Kim,Yousung Jung*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出CRISP，一个基于大语言模型（large language model）辅助的框架，将材料科学中的表征构建（representation construction）视为规则空间探索与编译问题。该方法无需结构、标签或数据划分信息，通过反复采样目标相关的化学规则并将其编译为可执行的标量描述符，在正-无标签（positive-unlabeled）无机晶体可合成性预测中超越了专家策划和通用结构表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料预测的准确性关键取决于科学知识的表征方式，但许多关键考量仅以自然语言启发式（natural-language heuristics）的形式存在，传统机器学习模型无法直接利用这类知识。现有方法依赖专家手工设计特征或通用结构描述符，缺乏从广泛化学知识中系统化构建可迁移表征的途径。

Method: CRISP框架以迭代方式采样目标相关的化学规则，无需访问结构、标签或数据划分信息；对相关概念进行合并整理，并将每条规则编译为可执行的标量描述符（scalar descriptor），供传统学习器（conventional learner）使用。整个流程不依赖数据集先验，因而是数据集盲（dataset-blind）且可审计的。

Result: 在正-无标签无机晶体可合成性预测任务中，CRISP在相同学习器条件下优于专家策划和通用结构表征，并超越了专门构建的合成性预测模型；其优势在结构尺寸变化和化学族分布偏移场景下最为显著。低频生成的规则仍贡献了互补的预测信息，表明生成频率不代表实用性。同一工作流在形成能和离子电导率预测上取得竞争力，但在剪切模量预测上暴露了任务依赖性局限。

Conclusion: CRISP建立了一条从广泛化学知识到可迁移计算表征的数据集盲且可审计的构建路径，证明大语言模型辅助的规则探索与编译能够产出超越专家设计的描述符，同时揭示了该方法的任务依赖性边界。

Abstract: Materials prediction depends critically on how scientific knowledge is represented, yet many governing considerations exist only as natural-language heuristics that conventional learners cannot use. We introduce CRISP, a large language model-assisted framework that treats representation construction as a rule-space exploration and compilation problem: it repeatedly samples target-relevant chemical rules without access to structures, labels or data splits, consolidates related concepts, and compiles each into an executable scalar descriptor supplied to a conventional learner. For positive-unlabeled inorganic-crystal synthesizability, CRISP outperformed expert-curated and generic structural representations under a shared learner and surpassed purpose-built synthesizability models, with its advantage most pronounced under structural-size and chemical-family shifts. Infrequently generated rules contributed complementary predictive information, showing that generation frequency does not determine utility. The same workflow yielded competitive representations for formation energy and ionic conductivity while revealing task-dependent limits for shear modulus, establishing a dataset-blind, auditable route from broad chemical knowledge to transferable computational representations.

</details>


### [4] [Atomistic Indicators of the Ductile-to-Brittle Transition in Polycrystalline Tungsten: Temperature and Rhenium Effects on Crack-Tip Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27589)
*Divyesh Mistry,Avik Mahata*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟，研究了体心立方钨（W）及其钨-铼（W-Re）合金在位移控制的Mode-I拉伸载荷下（300–1800 K，应变率5×10^8 s⁻¹）的断裂响应。结果表明，加热纯W会降低失稳应力和预失稳体积功密度，并给出约870 K的交叉转变标记T*_MD；W-10Re通过改变裂纹尖端塑性容纳路径而非简单强化来影响性能。作者还使用OpenDiS/pydis计算提供了无裂纹多晶W的中尺度位错演化的背景参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解体心立方钨的断裂响应如何由裂纹尖端失稳与位错介导的塑性容纳之间的竞争所控制，以及合金化（加入铼）如何影响这种竞争关系，是预测高应变率下钨材料韧性-脆性转变（ductile-to-brittle transition, DBT）行为的关键。

Method: 采用分子动力学模拟，对含边缘裂纹的多晶纯W和W-Re合金施加位移控制的Mode-I拉伸，温度范围为300–1800 K，应变率为5×10^8 s⁻¹；同时使用OpenDiS/pydis对无裂纹多晶W进行位错动力学计算以提供中尺度背景。

Result: 加热纯W降低了失稳应力、有效试样刚度和预失稳体积功密度，而失稳应变保持分散；基于预失稳功定义的无量纲功损耗度量给出了约为870 K的宽S形交叉标记T*_MD。W-10Re在大多数温度下表现出比纯W更大的预失稳变形和功积累，其最大应力响应强烈依赖于温度和成分。铼改变了保留位错特征和近尖端位错活动的时间分布，且上述机械与缺陷结构的耦合变化可作为高应变率DBT的原子尺度指示指标。

Conclusion: 铼的合金化改变了裂纹尖端的塑性容纳路径而非简单地提升强度；本研究的MD结果提供了高应变率条件下原子尺度的DBT指示指标，但不能直接预测实验DBTT；OpenDiS/pydis计算仅提供中尺度背景参考，不暗示MD与DDD之间的直接定量耦合。

Abstract: The fracture response of body-centered-cubic tungsten is governed by competition between crack-tip instability and dislocation-mediated plastic accommodation. Molecular dynamics (MD) simulations are used to examine edge-cracked polycrystalline W and W-Re under displacement-controlled Mode-I tension from 300-1800 K at a strain rate of 5 x 10^8 s^-1. Heating pure W reduces the instability stress, effective specimen stiffness, and pre-instability volumetric work density, while the instability strain remains comparatively scattered. A normalized work-loss metric derived from the pre-instability work gives a broad sigmoid crossover marker, T*_MD ~ 870 K, characteristic of the simulated geometry and high-rate loading conditions. Among the available W-Re compositions, W-10Re shows greater pre-instability deformation and work accumulation than pure W across most temperatures, while the maximum-stress response remains strongly temperature- and composition-dependent. Changes in retained dislocation character and near-tip activity indicate that Re modifies the crack-tip plastic-accommodation pathway rather than producing a simple strength increase. Common-state analysis further shows that Re alters the retained 1/2<111> line character and the temporal distribution of near-tip dislocation activity without uniformly increasing the local dislocation population. These coupled mechanical and defect-structure changes provide atomistic indicators of a high-rate ductile-to-brittle transition (DBT), but not a direct prediction of experimental DBTT. A separate OpenDiS/pydis calculation demonstrates source-like bow-out and heterogeneous dislocation-network development in crack-free polycrystalline W, providing mesoscale context without implying direct quantitative MD-DDD coupling.

</details>


### [5] [Physics-informed learning for the inverse problem in resonant ultrasound spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27590)
*Alejandro Cubillos Muñoz,Manuela Rivas,Julian Rincon*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文将超声共振谱（resonant ultrasound spectroscopy）中弹性常数反演问题形式化为约束逆等谱问题（constrained inverse-isospectral problem），在物理容许的弹性张量流形上引入降维变量，构建了一个物理信息学习流水线（physics-informed learning pipeline）：仅对降维后的谱特征与几何特征执行回归，而尺度恢复和最终弹性常数重构以解析方式施加。在立方对称基准和固定几何基准上分别报告了对应弹性常数的MAE和MAPE误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限超声共振谱数据反推弹性常数是一个非线性的、通常过定的（overdetermined）逆问题，直接求解在数值和物理一致性上都存在困难。作者旨在通过物理约束将原问题转化为更适合机器学习求解的回归形式，提高反演精度与可解释性。

Method: 将Rayleigh-Ritz逆问题表述为物理容许弹性张量集合上的约束逆等谱问题，并由此提取有效低维变量，包括长度与弹性尺度、长宽比坐标、无标度谱特征及保持稳定性的弹性比率。基于这些变量构建物理信息学习流水线，回归模型仅处理降维的谱与几何特征，而尺度恢复和弹性常数最终重构以解析方式完成。

Result: 在全立方对称（cubic）基准中，C11、C12和C44的MAE分别为20.37(35.15)、24.30(41.33)和2.13(3.66) GPa。在固定几何基准中，立方对称对应的MAPE分别为4.14%(3.87%)、8.31%(8.50%)和2.44%(2.86%)；各向同性情形下体模量与剪切模量的MAPE分别为4.0%(3.6%)和0.4%(0.3%)。

Conclusion: 该逆问题被转化为一个在适应几何、尺度、晶体对称性及胡克弹性热力学稳定性的变量上进行的约束回归问题，从而在不牺牲物理约束的解析可施加性的前提下，利用机器学习提升弹性常数反演性能。

Abstract: Inferring elastic constants from resonant ultrasound spectra is a nonlinear and typically overdetermined inverse problem based on finite spectral data. We formulate the Rayleigh-Ritz inverse problem as a constrained inverse-isospectral problem on the set of physically admissible elasticity tensors. This induces effective low-dimensional variables for the inverse map on the admissible elasticity manifold: length and elastic scales, aspect-ratio coordinates, scale-free spectral features, and stability-respecting elastic ratios. We use these variables to construct a physics-informed learning pipeline in which a regression model acts only on reduced spectral and geometric features, while scale recovery and final elastic-constant reconstruction are imposed analytically. For the full cubic benchmark, the reconstructed constants have MAE values of $20.37(35.15)$, $24.30(41.33)$, and $2.13(3.66)~\mathrm{GPa}$ for $C_{11}$, $C_{12}$, and $C_{44}$. In the fixed-geometry benchmark, the corresponding cubic MAPE values are $4.14(3.87)\%$, $8.31(8.50)\%$, and $2.44(2.86)\%$, while the isotropic values are $4.0(3.6)\%$ and $0.4(0.3)\%$ for the bulk and shear moduli. The inverse problem then becomes a constrained regression problem in variables adapted to the geometry, scaling, crystal symmetry, and thermodynamic stability of Hookean elasticity.

</details>


### [6] [LDA-1/2 for Molecular Systems: A Real-Space Finite-Element Benchmark on the GW100 Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27643)
*Dongming Li,Niamh Matthews,Qingchuan Sang,Eric Polizzi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在NESSIE电子结构框架内实现了LDA-1/2方法的全电子有限元（finite-element）版本，并应用于GW100分子基准集。该实现通过构造中性态与半电离态的自能修正，避免了预计算的原子修正势，在GW100上相对CCSD(T)参考电离能取得0.472 eV的平均绝对误差和0.645 eV的均方根误差，精度与G0W0@PBE相当，显著优于传统LDA及此前LAPW实现的LDA-1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDA-1/2方法能有效修正半局域密度泛函理论（semilocal density functional theory）的电离能和带隙，但此前在分子体系中的应用较为有限。作者旨在提供一种系统可控的数值精度实现，并为分子体系的LDA-1/2建立精确的基准数据。

Method: 在NESSIE电子结构框架中实现全电子有限元的LDA-1/2方法；对每个分子显式构造中性态与半电离态以建立自能修正，不使用预计算的原子修正势；利用实空间有限元离散实现系统收敛控制；对GW100分子基准集进行数值验证，并执行收敛性测试。

Result: GW100基准集上，相对CCSD(T)参考电离能的平均绝对误差为0.472 eV，均方根误差为0.645 eV；精度显著优于传统LDA及此前LAPW实现的LDA-1/2，与G0W0@PBE相当。收敛测试表明，对所考察的代表性体系，三阶有限元即足以达到或接近化学精度（chemical accuracy）。修正后的哈密顿量还改善了一些较低价态（valence states），但远离最高占据分子轨道（HOMO）时改善变得不够系统。

Conclusion: 该工作为分子体系提供了精确的LDA-1/2基准数据，并为未来分子GW计算建立了严格的有限元基础。

Abstract: The LDA-1/2 method provides an efficient correction to semilocal density functional theory for improving ionization energies and band gaps, yet its application to molecular systems has remained limited. In this work, we present an all-electron finite-element implementation of LDA-1/2 within the NESSIE electronic-structure framework and apply it to the GW100 molecular benchmark set with systematically controllable numerical accuracy. The self-energy correction is constructed explicitly from neutral and half-ionized calculations for each molecule, avoiding the use of precomputed atomic correction potentials. The real-space finite-element formulation enables systematic convergence with respect to the discretization and provides a controlled assessment of LDA-1/2 performance. For the GW100 set, the present implementation yields a mean absolute error of 0.472 eV and a root-mean-square error of 0.645 eV relative to CCSD(T) reference ionization energies, substantially improving upon conventional LDA and the previously reported LAPW implementation of LDA-1/2, while achieving accuracy comparable to G0W0@PBE. Convergence tests show that third-order finite elements are sufficient to reach or approach chemical accuracy relative to higher-order calculations for the representative systems considered. The resulting corrected Hamiltonian also improves several lower lying valence states relative to LDA, although the improvement becomes less systematic away from the HOMO. This work provides accurate LDA-1/2 benchmark data for molecular systems and establishes a rigorous finite-element foundation for future molecular GW calculations.

</details>


### [7] [First-Principles DFT Study of Ferroelectric-to-Antiferroelectric Phase Transitions in LiTaO$_3$ under Electric Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27645)

  <summary>Details</summary>

When a defect transitions between two electronic states, the lattice distorts in response. Large displacements occur near the defect, inducing displacements on neighboring atoms, and so on, producing small displacements on atoms hundreds of Ångströms from the defect. Describing these displacements accurately is challenging for first-principles calculations due to limited supercell sizes. Here we demonstrate a procedure to efficiently obtain dilute-limit atomic displacements, using information obtained in typical modest supercells. In our approach, the atomic displacements are first obtained in a small supercell and converted into a force difference under a harmonic potential energy surface. The force difference and phonon modes at different ${\bf q}$-points are then unfolded into the Born-von Kármán supercell to obtain the dilute-limit atomic displacements. We critically analyze the convergence behavior of the force difference and study possible electron density differences that give rise to those forces, arguing that modest supercells are sufficient for internal transitions and bound-exciton transitions. Two example applications of our approach are given: (1) we calculate the luminescence spectrum of the NV center in diamond and the T center in Si and (2) we obtain dilute-limit configuration coordinate diagrams for these defects. In particular, we find that coupling to acoustic phonon modes reduces the accepting-mode frequency in the configuration coordinate diagram. Our work provides the missing ingredients necessary to address truly dilute-limit transitions at defects.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者提出一个尖锐界面理论（sharp theory），其核心假设为：裂纹仅在材料强度面被超过的区域成核与扩展，演化规律由（弹性能减去外载荷功所得的）势能与表面能量之和的最小化决定。该理论适用于任意弹性（线性或非线性）与任意材料对称性（各向同性或各向异性），但本文聚焦于各向同性弹性脆性材料的最基本情形，并通过九项覆盖断裂成核与扩展全部成熟实验知识的测试进行验证。

Result: 该理论被应用于两种材料——硬质材料（硅酸盐玻璃）与软质材料（合成橡胶）——并对照九项测试（即作者所称的"弹性脆性断裂的九重圈"，Nine Circles of Elastic Brittle Fracture）进行演示验证，结果覆盖了实验上已充分确定的断裂成核与扩展的完整知识范围。

Conclusion: 该宏观连续统断裂理论能够统一描述弹性脆性材料中裂纹的成核与扩展，且其适用范围涵盖不同刚度的材料类型；强度面准则与势能-表面能最小化原则共同构成裂纹演化的理论基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a macroscopic, or continuum, theory aimed at describing when, where, and why cracks nucleate and propagate in nominally elastic brittle materials under monotonic, quasi-static, but otherwise arbitrary mechanical loads. Motivated by recent insights, the proposed sharp theory posits that: \emph{cracks nucleate and propagate exclusively in regions where the strength surface of the material is exceeded, with their evolution dictated by the minimization of the sum of the potential --- the elastic energy minus the work done by the externally applied forces --- and surface energies.} While the theory applies to materials with any elasticity (linear or nonlinear) and any material symmetry (isotropic or anisotropic), attention is restricted here to the most basic case of isotropic elastic brittle materials. For demonstration purposes, the theory is confronted with a set of nine tests that span the entire range of well-settled experimental knowledge on fracture nucleation and propagation --- the so-called ``Nine Circles of Elastic Brittle Fracture'' --- on both a hard material (a silicate glass) and a soft material (a synthetic rubber).

</details>

These materials exhibit intrinsic ferromagnetism, with CrCl3 displaying overlapping topological features in both its magnon and phonon dispersions along the high-symmetry K direction, which induces magnon-phonon coupling. Incorporating 3d dopants into VCl3 successfully tunes the bandgap (1.32 eV for Ti and 2.74 eV for Cr) without destroying ferromagnetism, while 4f Ce-doping in CrCl3 introduces localized states below the Fermi level that yield strong nearest-neighbor exchange coupling. *Jian Luo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文综述了从高熵陶瓷（high-entropy ceramics, HECs）到成分复杂陶瓷（compositionally complex ceramics, CCCs）的研究进展，指出通过非等摩尔设计、长程与短程有序等手段，CCCs 可在降低构型熵的同时提供更多性能调控机会，超越高熵对应物。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The development of high-entropy ceramics (HECs) over the past decade has extended the high-entropy concept to a diverse range of oxides, borides, silicides, carbides, nitrides, fluorides, silicates, and other ceramic solid solutions, encompassing increasingly diverse crystal structures and bonding characteristics and exhibiting a broad spectrum of promising mechanical, thermal, and functional properties. Initial studies predominantly focused on five-component equimolar compositions, often assuming the formation of random solid solutions. More recently, 10-21 component ultrahigh-entropy ceramics have been developed as a subset of HECs, some of which exhibit intriguing abrupt phase transitions. In 2020, we proposed extending the exploration of HECs to the broader class of "compositionally complex ceramics" (CCCs), in which non-equimolar compositions and long- and short-range order reduce configurational entropy while providing additional opportunities to tailor and enhance materials properties, thereby outperforming their higher-entropy counterparts. Dual-phase CCCs have also been reported, with thermodynamic equilibria governing cation partitioning between the two phases and offering further opportunities to control and enhance properties through microstructural engineering. Subsequent studies have revealed grain-boundary phase-like transitions in CCCs that can control microstructural evolution and materials properties. Overall, CCCs offer a versatile platform for tailoring materials properties through diverse crystal structures and bonding characteristics, compositional complexity, non-equimolar designs, long- and short-range order, defects, and microstructural and interfacial engineering.

Abstract: Foundation machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) deliver near-ab-initio accuracy at a fraction of the computational cost, yet their promise for Metal-organic Frameworks (MOFs) remains largely unrealized as large unit cells make first-principles training data expensive to generate, fine-tuned models are scarce, and experimentally grounded benchmarks are scarcer still. First, we release the largest and most accurate density functional theory dataset for MOFs to date, computed at the r$^2$SCAN-D4 level of theory across 85524 configurations spanning 19950 unique frameworks and 79 elements, covering empty and gas-loaded structures, geometry optimizations, equations of state, and finite-temperature molecular dynamics. Second, we release a literature-mined benchmark of 3986 verified property values (3146 experimental) extracted from 626 papers by a seven-stage, checkpointed multi-pass large language model pipeline, linked to more than 650 crystallographic information files. Third, we release two universal MLIPs for MOFs, uMOF-MH and uMOF-POLAR, fine-tuned from two architecturally distinct MACE foundation models on the uMOF dataset. On near-equilibrium, ``Tier-1'' properties (bulk modulus, phonon-derived heat capacity) the uMOF models perform comparably to existing foundation and fine-tuned baselines. On harder, dynamics-sensitive properties like gas adsorption enthalpies via Widom insertion and adsorption isotherms, the uMOF models outperform every baseline we test, including MOF-specialized gas-capture models trained on datasets up to three orders of magnitude larger, cutting error by more than 80% to within experimental uncertainty. We trace this advantage to the physical diversity of the training data and to level of theory where a small (1.7%) fraction of MD simulations is decisive for MLIP stability.

Result: 该框架成功统一解释了钙钛矿中光学/电学上的直接带隙半导体特征（轻载流子有效质量和陡峭吸收边）与晶格上的液态动力学（过阻尼八面体运动、准弹性拉曼中心峰、超低热导率）之间的表观矛盾，并对三个争议性问题——缺陷容忍度、铁电性和T-3/2迁移率定律的物理解释——进行了批判性评估，同时提出了七个亟待解决的关键开放问题及所需的测量方案。

Abstract: Lead-halide perovskites APbX3 present a striking dichotomy: they exhibit band-like electronic transport despite their exceptionally soft and anharmonic lattices. Optically and electrically, they resemble conventional direct-gap semiconductors, exhibiting light carrier masses and steep absorption onsets, whereas their lattices display liquid-like dynamics, including overdamped octahedral motions, quasielastic Raman central peaks, and exceptionally low thermal conductivities. Solution-processed films nevertheless sustain micrometre-scale carrier diffusion at defect densities that would severely suppress transport in conventional semiconductors. Here, we argue that these apparently disparate properties emerge from a common microscopic framework: a soft, strongly anharmonic, and polar [PbX3]- framework, strongly influenced by the Pb 6s2 lone pair, which simultaneously shapes the antibonding orbital character of the band edges, the magnitude and multiple timescales of the dielectric response, and the slow relaxational dynamics that dress every charge carrier. We therefore invert the conventional order and develop the lattice before the electronic structure, because the nominally cubic phase is better viewed as a thermally fluctuating ensemble of locally symmetry-broken configurations rather than a single geometry. Within this framework, we discuss excitons, Frohlich large polarons in the intermediate-coupling regime, carrier transport, defect tolerance, dimensional reduction in two-dimensional and nanocrystalline derivatives, and symmetry-breaking phenomena. We critically assess three contested issues - defect tolerance, ferroelectricity, and the interpretation of the T-3/2 mobility law - and identify seven open questions together with the key measurements needed to resolve them

Abstract: Electron correlation, hopping, and ligand-to-metal charge transfer collectively lead to diverse electronic and magnetic phenomena in 3$d$ transition-metal oxides, where directional d orbitals make hopping highly sensitive to symmetry-dependent orbital overlap. This light-controlled defect engineering allows us to increase the yield of the STE signal appearing as optically stimulated luminescence (OSL) and to image the spatial distribution of the initial energy deposition, holding strong potential for ionizing-radiation detection. These findings provide a common framework underlying scintillation and OSL in ionic crystals of the fluorite structure, allowing for optical manipulation of atomic vacancies-interstitial pairs in similar systems.

Abstract: Machine-learned forward models can rapidly predict alloy properties, but their use for inverse design remains challenging when the search should also retain compatibility with quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO). The results show that candidate generation is a major determinant of search performance: local perturbations around previously high-performing latent codes provide the largest workflow-specific improvement, while surrogate-based selection further prioritizes candidates within the enriched search pool. The resulting QUBO-compatible workflow remains competitive with strong classical optimization strategies, although a composition-space genetic algorithm achieves the highest mean score. Finally, the learned quadratic surrogate can be exported directly as a QUBO. These results show that effective data acquisition can be separated from the final QUBO optimization endpoint, providing a benchmarked route for QUBO-compatible data-driven materials inverse design.

Abstract: Understanding how the topology of network forming materials influences their physical properties remains a longstanding challenge. Here, we investigate the topology of experimentally compatible atomistic models of amorphous silica together with the crystalline polymorphs cristobalite and quartz. By comparing multiple atomistic models of amorphous silica that equally reproduce neutron-scattering data, we show that different microscopic descriptions can imply different topological interpretations of glass stability. To characterize the network beyond conventional geometric descriptors, we introduce the linking valence, which quantifies the average number of topological links per network loop. This topological descriptor separates silica into two distinct classes: the mechanically harder quartz exhibit values more than an order of magnitude larger than those of amorphous silica and cristobalite, despite their common tetrahedral building blocks. Spectral analysis of the loop-linking networks provides a complementary distinction, separating amorphous from crystalline phases and revealing differences in the long-range organization of topological constraints. These results establish topological linking as a new framework for connecting the structure and physical properties of network-forming materials.

Abstract: We introduce a Wyckoff-resolved oxidation-state atlas and assignment utility for probabilityranked, charge-neutral assignment from compositions or Wyckoff grammars. The atlas is constructed from a May 2026 snapshot of 154,879 Materials Project-derived structures by staged exact-neutral enumeration over common and known nonzero oxidation states. The learned prior assigns 106,053 materials in composition mode and 114,403 in Wyckoff mode, compared with a broad MP all-integer possible species count of 108,642. A matched MP baseline requiring at least one charge-neutral assignment with exactly one nonzero integer oxidation state per element contains 89,374 materials; relative to this baseline, composition and Wyckoff modes increase coverage by 18.7% and 28.0%. Of the 14,665 materials recovered only in Wyckoff mode, 99.98% exhibit distinct formal oxidation states for the same element on different site tokens. The CSV/Python workflow provides a reproducible prior for structure decoration, generative crystal models, and symbolic Wyckoff-grammar workflows.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 研究以统计真值为参考，在GaN的ECCI显微图像上对比三种自动化检测流水线：经典规则型计算机视觉方法、基于卷积神经网络（CNN）的多阶段分类与定位方法，以及统一单阶段YOLOv8架构；针对YOLOv8部署中的两个领域特定问题，分别提出自适应高斯切片算法（adaptive gaussian tile-sizing algorithm）以优化每张图像的视场，并将推理置信度阈值从默认的0.25下调至0.025。

Result: 规则型方法因需要大量逐图调参而不可靠；多阶段CNN方法达到87%准确率但测试耗时较长且在高密度区域效率不高；YOLOv8在7451个位错、多张图像上实现98.6%计数准确率、98.7%精确率和98.7%召回率，推理速度快；置信度阈值从0.25降至0.025后，在基准图像子区域的计数准确率从90.8%提升至99.3%。

Abstract: Quantifying threading dislocations in semiconductor materials via electron channelling contrast imaging (ECCI) is heavily bottlenecked by slow manual analysis. This work benchmarks three automated detection pipelines on ECCI micrographs of gallium nitride (GaN) against a statistical ground truth. A classical rule-based computer vision approach proved unreliable due to extensive per-image tuning requirements, while a convolutional neural network (CNN)-based multi-stage classification and locator method achieved 87% accuracy but required significant time for a test image and was less efficient in high-density regions. By contrast, a unified single-stage you only look once (YOLOv8) architecture achieved a counting accuracy of 98.6%, alongside 98.7% precision and 98.7% recall across 7451 dislocations over multiple images, with rapid inference times. Successful deployment of YOLOv8 required addressing two domain-specific machine learning challenges. First, to mitigate the model's scale sensitivity, an adaptive gaussian tile-sizing algorithm was developed to optimise the field of view per image. Second, the deployed confidence threshold (0.025) differed substantially from the suggested inference default value for YOLOv8 (0.25), raising counting accuracy from 90.8% to 99.3% on a subsection of the benchmark image. In this case, low confidence scores represented physical signal strength rather than classification ambiguity. This unified, scale-adaptive approach demonstrates practical viability for high-throughput, quantitative semiconductor defect characterisation.

Abstract: In the field of cavity quantum materials, vacuum fluctuations of optical cavities are used for modifying ground-state properties of quantum materials without external driving. Here, one example is the van der Waals (vdW)/dispersion interaction in layered 2D vdW materials, where non-additive long-range correlations can dominate the interlayer binding. While cavity-induced changes of such interactions have been predicted and described using ab initio methods for molecular systems, no efficient description exists yet for extended materials. In this Letter, we close this gap by introducing a periodic formulation of the photon many-body dispersion (pMBD) functional within quantum electrodynamical density-functional theory (QEDFT). Applying this method with efficient $\textbf{q}$-point sampling to bilayer hBN and graphene, we predict cavity-modified stacking, increased equilibrium interlayer distances, and softened layer breathing modes with increasing light-matter coupling strength. Our results establish cavity vacuum fluctuations as a tuning knob for the structural properties of vdW materials.

</details>

Motivation: 反铁磁中许多由时间反演对称性破缺引起的非平凡性质具有技术应用价值，但其时间反演伙伴态通常难以确定性选择，除了少数具有非零净磁化的情况。近期在HoAgGe中零净磁化的kagome自旋冰基态可通过χ^(1)选择TR伙伴，本文旨在将此方案推广到更广泛的无净磁化但有场可切换基态的手性反铁磁类别。

Method: 首先给出χ^(1)及其对称性约束的一般性讨论；随后提出一个同时包含均场（mean-field）和自洽（self-consistent）修正的第一性原理计算框架；将该方法应用于非共线反铁磁族Mn₃XN（X=Ni, Ag, Ga, Zn, Sn）的Γ5g相；最后用三子格玩具模型（toy model）直观说明类似非共线反铁磁中χ^(1)的起源。

Result: 三子格玩具模型揭示了非共线反铁磁中χ^(1)的物理起源，并预言由于纵向与横向单自旋磁化率之间的竞争，χ^(1)呈现非平凡的随温度变化行为。研究表明非线性磁化率可作为在完全补偿反铁磁中访问和切换TR伙伴的通用协议。

Conclusion: 非线性磁化率χ^(1)可以作为完全补偿反铁磁中获得和切换时间反演伙伴的通用手段，为这类材料的实际应用提供了一种确定性选择TR态的新途径。

Abstract: Antiferromagnets (AFM) have attracted considerable attention in recent years because a number of nontrivial, technologically relevant properties associated with time-reversal symmetry (TRS) breaking are discovered in many materials. However, time-reversal (TR) partners of AFM states are generally challenging to be selected deterministically except in a few cases with nonzero net magnetization. After a general discussion of $χ^{(1)}$ and its symmetry constraints, we first present a first-principles formalism for calculating it including both mean-field and self-consistent corrections, and then apply the approach to the noncollinear AFM family Mn$_3X$N ($X=$ Ni, Ag, Ga, Zn, Sn) in the $Γ_{5g}$ phase. An intuitive picture of the origin of $χ^{(1)}$ in similar noncollinear AFM is illustrated using 3-sublattice toy model. The model also predicts a nontrivial temperature dependence of $χ^{(1)}$ due to competitions between the longitudinal and transverse single-spin susceptibilities. Our work shows that nonlinear susceptibility can serve as a general protocol for accessing and switching TR partners in fully compensated AFM.

Abstract: Crystalline C$_{60}$ is a molecular solid whose electronic properties emerge from the interplay of intermolecular hopping, electron correlations, and electron-vibration coupling. The HOMO-derived band exhibits a non-monotonic evolution: the intermediate-thickness film shows larger bandwidth, reduced effective mass, and pronounced gap-like and sub-band features. The experimental trends, together with Holstein-model simulations, point to strengthened effective intermolecular electronic coupling and enhanced electron-phonon-induced spectral renormalization in the intermediate-thickness regime. These results identify a dimensional crossover in C$_{60}$ films and establish layer number as an effective knob for engineering electronic structure and many-body interactions in molecular thin films.

Method: 模型定义在离散三维环面上，采用一类适当归一化的广义 plaquette 作用量（包括 Wilson、Manton 和 Villain 三种形式）。在弱耦合标度下，通过 DeTurck 规范选择，对重标度的对数场进行随机动力学分析，并证明其局部随时间在概率意义上收敛于极限方程。

Abstract: We study the continuum scaling limit of the Langevin dynamics for three-dimensional U(1) lattice Yang--Mills theory. The model is defined on the discrete 3D torus with a general class of plaquette actions that are suitably normalized, including Wilson, Manton, and Villain actions. Under the weak-coupling scaling and in the DeTurck gauge, we prove that, locally in time and in probability, the rescaled logarithmic field converges to the solution of the one-form stochastic heat equation. In particular, the limiting dynamics are universal and do not depend on the higher-order details of the plaquette action.

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文揭示球形表面上多种粒子排序机制（面积均匀、导体制、曲率加权度量）在密度上不可区分，而椭球形非球面形变可解除这一简并，使各排序机制在距壳心到切平面的距离的不同幂次上分离。通过结构因子四极矩和回旋半径可读出特征指数，Riesz s-能量与屏蔽库仑势分别以突变和连续调谐方式实现该指数，盐浓度因此成为调控粒子排序的可实验旋钮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面表面对称性过高，使得多种粒子排序机制（ordering mechanism）在密度分布上完全一致，一体验收不到排序的内部信息，阻碍了对微观相互作用规律的识别。

Method: 从球形表面转向椭球形表面，利用非球面形变打破三种度量路径的简并；推导排序机制与壳心到切平面距离的各次幂的对应关系，并通过结构因子四极矩（structure factor quadrupole）和回旋半径（radius of gyration）在主导阶下提取特征指数；对比Riesz s-能量与屏蔽库仑势（screened Coulomb potential）两种相互作用家族的表现。

Result: 在椭球面上，面积均匀、导体制与曲率加权三种排序机制分离为不同幂次项；Riesz s-能量在边际幂次处发生极限度量的尖锐切换，而屏蔽库仑势的指数随盐浓度连续变化。离子强度（ionic strength）可实现对固定非球面上粒子排序的跨导体-堆积（conductor-to-packing）转变调节。

Conclusion: 宏观形状参数可当作微观相互作用规律的探针：在固定非球面表面上，通过调节盐浓度即可连续改变粒子排序行为，将离子强度转化为连接宏观几何与微观力学的实验控制变量。

Abstract: Spherical surfaces conceal the one-body signature of particle organization: area-uniform, conductor, and curvature-weighted measures coincide there, so the ordering mechanism leaves no trace in the density. Aspherical deformation lifts this degeneracy. On an ellipsoid, these ordering mechanisms separate into distinct powers of the distance from the shell center to its tangent plane. To leading order in deformation, the characteristic exponent can be read from both the structure factor quadrupole and the radius of gyration. Two interaction families realize the exponent in complementary ways: the Riesz $s$-energy, whose limiting measure switches sharply at the marginal power, and screened Coulomb potential, where the exponent is tuned continuously by the salt concentration. Ionic strength thereby becomes an experimental dial that transforms the particle ordering across the conductor-to-packing crossover on a fixed aspherical surface, turning a macroscopic shape parameter into a probe of the microscopic interaction law.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡系统中普遍认为存在无标度关联（scale-free correlations），但绝大多数情形下缺乏显式推导，且已有推导常依赖近似。本文旨在针对一个具体系统——无限持久活性布朗粒子——给出此类关联函数的严格解析结果。

Method: 在低密度（low density）且小游动速度（small-swim-velocity）极限下，对无限持久活性布朗粒子系统进行分析，解析推导密度关联与游动速度关联的标度形式，并通过数值模拟验证解析预言。

Result: 密度关联和游动速度关联均遵循 r^{-(d+1)} 的代数衰减规律，其中在 d=2 维情形下出现对数修正；倒空间中这些关联在 k=0 处表现为尖点（cusps）。解析预测与数值模拟结果吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作为非平衡系统中无标度关联的存在性提供了一个严格的解析例子，进一步巩固了这类关联在非平衡系统中的普适性认知；推导过程借助了Claude模型辅助并经由作者验证。

Abstract: Scale-free correlations are believed to be generic in out of equilibrium systems but their explicit derivations are rare and often involve approximations. Here we analyze a system of infinitely persistent active Brownian particles in the low density and small-swim-velocity limit. We show that both the density and swim velocity correlations decay algebraically as r^{-(d+1)} (with logarithmic corrections in d=2). In reciprocal space these correlations manifest themselves as cusps at k=0. The analytical predictions are confirmed by numerical simulations. The results were obtained through interaction with Claude (Opus 4.8 and 5, and Fable 5) and verified by the author.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的空腔膨胀（cavity inflation）解通常假设材料属性具有径向对称性，而生物组织和工程软材料中常见的非径向刚度异质性（non-radial stiffness heterogeneity）对空腔膨胀的影响尚未被解决，这一空白构成了本研究的动机。

Method: 采用有限元模拟（finite-element simulations）研究超弹性固体中加压空腔的准静态膨胀，材料剪切模量（shear modulus）沿引用笛卡尔方向单调变化；在此基础上发展了一个Rayleigh–Ritz降阶框架，通过确定应变能最小化的迁移幅度来定量描述空腔迁移行为，且无需使用拟合参数。

Result: 有限元模拟表明，在初始小膨胀阶段之后，最显著的对称破缺响应是空腔朝材料较柔顺端（more compliant end）的迁移，而非球形畸变相对较弱；Rayleigh–Ritz框架无需拟合参数即可恢复与力学梯度参数密切相关的空腔迁移关键特征。

Conclusion: 将质心迁移（centroid migration）识别为突出的几何信号，并借助降阶框架预测其演化，为通过空腔膨胀实验对梯度材料进行逆表征（inverse characterization）提供了可行路线。

Abstract: The inflation of a pre-existing, fluid-filled cavity is a timeless topic in the finite-deformation analysis of soft materials. However, classical solutions for cavity inflation rely on radially symmetric material properties, leaving unresolved the effects of non-radial stiffness heterogeneity that are commonly present in biological tissues and engineered soft materials. In this work, we investigate the quasi-static inflation of a pressurized cavity in a hyperelastic solid with shear modulus varying monotonically along a reference Cartesian direction. Finite-element simulations reveal that, beyond an initial small-inflation regime, the most pronounced symmetry-breaking response is the migration of the cavity toward the more compliant end of the material, while nonspherical distortion remains comparatively weak. To analytically quantify this migration-dominated response, we develop a Rayleigh--Ritz reduced-order framework to determine the strain-energy-minimizing migration amplitude for prescribed gradation parameters and inflation level. Without using fitted parameters, the Rayleigh--Ritz framework recovers key features of the cavity migration that are intimately linked to the mechanical gradation parameters. The identification of centroid migration as a salient geometric signal, together with the reduced-order prediction of its evolution, suggests a roadmap for inverse characterization of graded materials through cavity-inflation experiments.

</details>

Result: 该约束将抛物型取向扩散转变为双曲型取向-密度波，并禁止稳态简单剪切；将经验性的Toner-Tu对流系数固定为胶体滚子中测得的流向排列比，为微极主动流体力学中的角声提供力学基础；恢复了鸟类群体的惯性自旋模型，并补充了倾斜力和转向密度耦合；同时恢复了经典一维交通流宏观模型，并在二维中描述了转向、侧向轮胎力和道路几何。

Conclusion: 该无侧滑约束为多种看似不同的自推进群体现象提供了统一的连续介质力学基础，并在向列型液晶理论与微极主动流体、鸟群集体运动及交通流模型之间建立了理论联系。

Abstract: We develop a continuum theory for a fluid of elongated particles that advance along their own axes. The same kinematics is ideally shared by flocks of sheep, self-propelled rods, vehicular traffic, and turning flocks of birds. Within the framework of continua with vectorial microstructure, in which each point also carries an orientation, we impose the no-side-slip (skate) condition $\vv=u\,\nn$ as an ideal, non-integrable internal constraint. We derive the pure equations of motion and the equation governing the constraint reaction from the principle of virtual power. Using a constitutive closure provided by the Ericksen-Leslie theory of nematic liquid crystals, we show how the constraint also shapes collective effects. It turns parabolic orientational diffusion into hyperbolic orientation-density waves and forbids any steady simple shear. It fixes the empirical Toner-Tu convective coefficient to the flow-alignment ratio measured in colloidal rollers, thereby providing a mechanical foundation for angular sound in micropolar active hydrodynamics. It also recovers the inertial spin model of bird flocks, with the addition of a banking force and a turn-density coupling. Finally, it recovers the classical macroscopic models of one-dimensional traffic flow, while describing steering, lateral tyre forces, and road geometry in two dimensions.

We further examine the effect of dipolar chemical emission -- expected from asymmetric micelle deposition at the monomer scale -- and find that the equilibrium structure holds exactly for dimers, whereas for longer chains no such description is possible. Monopolar coupling remains necessary and sufficient for the polarized state; dipolar coupling alone breaks the quasi-equilibrium structure without replacing it with a new static one.

Remarkably, large deformations preserve the small-strain elastic modulus while progressively suppressing strain stiffening, demonstrating a partial mechanical reversibility of the network. We interpret these observations using a hierarchical network picture in which a persistent intermolecular network controls linear elasticity, while a more fragile mesoscale organization enables cooperative alignment and stress amplification under deformation. Our results highlight that nonlinear mechanics of thermoreversible polysaccharide gels are governed not only by molecular connectivity, but also by the reversible formation and eventually destruction of mechanically adaptive hierarchical structures.

Abstract: A protein's function follows from the structure it adopts, and which structure that is depends on the pathway taken. Some produce one geometry, others several: structures sharing a detour route can be cycled between, while those that coexist assemble into superstructures inaccessible to a uniform product. Abstract: Markovian open-system dynamics have widespread applications throughout quantum information science, including algorithmic state preparation. Their convergence is commonly quantified using the worst case global trace distance between the evolving and stationary states. However, this criterion can be unnecessarily stringent when only physically relevant observables are of interest. Here we introduce and study observable-specific mixing times. We prove that, for quasi-local, rapidly mixing Lindbladians, sums of geometrically local observables equilibrate in a time independent of system size, in contrast to the logarithmic dependence of global state mixing. This separation reduces the runtime of dissipative quantum algorithms, including quantum Gibbs samplers, for estimating quantities such as the Gibbs state energy and local order parameters, yielding an overall scaling that is linear in system size. Complementing this quantum result, we develop a quantum-inspired classical algorithm for estimating the same quantities. Its runtime is likewise linear in system size, but scaling exponentially in $\mathcal{O}\big(\log(1/ε)^D\big)$, where $D$ denotes the spatial dimension of the lattice. We further analyse non-interacting Lindbladians over qudits, fermions, and bosons, demonstrating that locality of observables is not always necessary for a qualitatively faster mixing. Small-scale simulations of quantum Gibbs samplers reveal no large hidden constants in our asymptotic analysis and show that the theoretical predictions closely capture the finite-size dynamics.

Method: 提出基于倒装芯片（flip-chip）的传感方案，将待测材料与预制的微波谐振器耦合，从而在不加工待测材料的前提下提取其等效微波介电损耗。同时利用该方法对Ge/SiGe量子阱异质结构及其Ge衬底和中间层进行损耗量化，并对由沉积Pt膜热反应制备的PtSiGe超导薄膜进行系统表征。

Result: 制备的微波谐振器的品质因子（quality factor）与从倒装芯片传感测量中间接提取的电介质损耗一致。研究表明PtSiGe薄膜本身存在较高微波损耗，限制其作为高质量谐振器唯一超导体的适用性；而在反应前对Pt预先镀覆Nb可显著降低微波损耗，并使输运临界温度（critical temperature）提升近三倍。两种超导薄膜的微波损耗温度依赖关系均与能隙不均匀性（gap inhomogeneity）一致。

Conclusion: 这些结果明确了材料选择的约束条件，为平面Ge异质结构上微波电路设计提供了指导，同时展示了一种面向超导量子电路新材料的高周转测试方法。

Abstract: We present a flip-chip-based sensing scheme to measure effective microwave losses associated with target materials for quantum technologies, without requiring any device fabrication on the material under test. Using this approach, we quantify the microwave losses of a strain-engineered Ge/SiGe quantum well heterostructure and investigate losses arising from its Ge substrate and intermediate layers. The quality factors of the fabricated microwave resonators agree with the losses of dielectric materials independently extracted from flip-chip sensing measurements. We further study the superconductor platinum silicon germanide (PtSiGe) prepared by thermal reaction with a deposited Pt film, finding high microwave losses that limit the suitability of the films studied here as the sole superconductor for high-quality resonator applications. By coating Pt with Nb prior to the reaction, we observe a substantial reduction in microwave loss and a nearly three-fold enhancement of the transport critical temperature. The temperature dependence of the microwave loss is consistent with gap inhomogeneity in both superconducting films. These results identify constraints on material choices, provide design guidance for microwave circuits on planar Ge heterostructures, and demonstrate a fast-turnaround testing method for new materials for superconducting quantum circuits.

</details>


### [50] [Exact quantification of nonlocal magic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28563)
*Piotr Sierant*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了多体量子态中"非局域魔法"（nonlocal magic）的精确求解问题：任意纯多比特态的稳定子保真度（stabilizer fidelity）的非局域部分等于其纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum）与最近贝尔对谱之间的距离。该量还统一了一个表面无关的问题——非局域魔法发散是量子态在局域操作与经典通信（LOCC）下实现通用纠缠仿真（universal embezzlement）的充要条件，且该性质由纠缠谱在因子二秩窗口中的权重分布决定，而非纠缠总量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔法（magic/nonstabilizerness）是将克利福德电路（Clifford circuits）提升为通用量子计算的关键资源，已成为多体态的标准诊断工具。然而，对于两方共享的量子态，一个基本问题一直悬而未决：多少魔法存在于双方之间的关联而非各自的局域基中？分离这一非局域魔法需要对所有局域基进行最小化，这一优化此前未能精确求解。

Method: 作者针对稳定子保真度（stabilizer fidelity）求解了上述最小化问题，证明纯多比特量子态的非局域魔法等于其纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum）与最接近的贝尔对（Bell pairs）谱之间的距离。他们还建立了一个等价性定理，将非局域魔法的发散与该态族在局域操作与经典通信下通用纠缠仿真（embezzlement）的能力联系起来。

Result: 关键结果是：判定性质不是纠缠的总量，而是纠缠谱如何在因子二宽度的秩窗口中分布权重。由此，临界链（critical chains）和随机单重态（random-singlet states）虽然具有相同的对数纠缠标度（logarithmic entanglement scaling），但分别具有无界和非零消失的非局域魔法。

Conclusion: 非局域魔法由此成为一种具有操作意义的量子关联性质，可直接通过张量网络模拟（tensor-network simulations）评估，并通过纠缠谱学（entanglement spectroscopy）在实验中探测。

Abstract: Magic, or nonstabilizerness, is the resource that lifts Clifford circuits to universal quantum computation and has become a standard diagnostic of many-body states. For a state shared between two parties, however, a basic question has remained open: how much of the magic resides in the correlations between the parties rather than in their local bases? Isolating this nonlocal magic requires minimizing over all local bases, an optimization that has so far resisted exact solution. Here we solve it for the stabilizer fidelity: the nonlocal magic of every pure multiqubit state is the distance of its entanglement spectrum from the closest spectrum of Bell pairs. The same quantity governs an apparently unrelated task: a family of states universally embezzles entanglement under local operations and classical communication if and only if its nonlocal magic diverges. The deciding property is not the amount of entanglement but the way the entanglement spectrum spreads its weight across factor-of-two windows of rank, so that critical chains and random-singlet states, with identical logarithmic entanglement scaling, carry unbounded and vanishing nonlocal magic, respectively. Nonlocal magic thereby becomes an operationally meaningful property of quantum correlations, directly accessible to tensor-network simulations and, through entanglement spectroscopy, to experiments.

</details>


### [51] [How Long-Range Tails Reshape Non-Hermitian Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28577)
*Ding Gu,Zhanpeng Fu,Yu-Min Hu,Zhong Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究表明，在非厄米（non-Hermitian）系统开放边界条件下，即使极小的长程跳跃（long-range hopping）也能非微扰地重塑短程非厄米系统的能谱和本征态，其机制由长程跳跃的衰减长度与皮肤模（skin mode）局域长度之间的竞争控制，体现为一维下广义布里渊区（GBZ）的压缩，以及高维下的压缩amoeba形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）中普遍存在指数衰减的长程跳跃，但通常被截断为有限程描述。作者试图探究这种截断近似在非厄米开放边界条件下的有效性，发现该近似可能严重失效。

Method: 作者通过解析分析长程跳跃衰减长度与非厄米皮肤模局域长度之间的竞争机制，在临界衰减率处揭示非微扰相变；并在一维中引入压缩广义布里渊区（squeezed generalized Brillouin zone）来描述重建的开放边界能谱，在二维或更高维度中提出压缩amoeba（squeezed amoeba）形式刻画谱密度。

Result: 发现无穷小长程跳跃可在非厄米开放边界条件下非微扰地重建短程非厄米系统的能谱与本征态；一维中压缩GBZ取代原始GBZ，高维中压缩amoeba公式描述重建谱密度；长程跳跃还能定性重塑Green函数，可在实验中直接探测。

Conclusion: 截断长程跳跃的常用近似在非厄米系统开放边界条件下可能严重失效，需在相关理论与实验中谨慎对待；该发现为理解非厄米系统中长程效应的本质提供了新框架，且实验可测。

Abstract: Exponentially decaying long-range hoppings are ubiquitous in realistic tight-binding models and are often truncated to obtain a finite-range description. We show that this approximation can fail dramatically in non-Hermitian systems under open boundary conditions: an infinitesimal long-range hopping can nonperturbatively reconstruct the spectrum and eigenstates of a short-range non-Hermitian system. The mechanism is controlled by a competition between the decay length of infinitesimal long-range hoppings and the localization length of non-Hermitian skin modes, leading to a sharp transition as the decay rate is tuned. In one dimension, we show that a squeezed generalized Brillouin zone (GBZ) replaces the original GBZ of the short-ranged Hamiltonian, yielding the reconstructed open-boundary spectrum. In two or higher dimensions, we formulate a squeezed amoeba formulation describing the reconstructed spectral density. We further show that long-range hoppings can qualitatively reshape Green's function, which can be readily detected in experiments.

</details>

### [52] [Comparing Classical and Quantum Machine Learning for Regression in High Energy Physics Collision Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28084)
*Tariq Mahmood,Zain ul Abidin,Itzel Luviano Soto,Alfredo Raya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了四种经典机器学习架构（SVM、ANN、CNN、LSTM）与其量子对应模型（QSVM、QNN、QCNN、QLSTM）在粒子碰撞事件分类与回归任务中的表现。基于CERN开放数据门户的模拟质子-质子碰撞数据，经典模型尤其是CNN和LSTM获得略优的定量性能，但量子模型以显著更少的可训练参数达到竞争精度——QCNN仅用四个量子比特和深度为三的电路就复现了深层经典CNN的性能，体现了在近端量子设备上的参数效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高能物理实验中，粒子碰撞事件的分类与回归问题始终是计算层面的持续挑战，大量模拟数据需要同时满足速度和精度的处理要求。在量子计算快速发展的背景下，有必要系统评估量子机器学习架构与经典架构在真实物理数据上的性能差异，为资源受限条件下的架构选择提供依据。

Method: 研究选取四种经典机器学习架构——支持向量机（SVM）、人工神经网络（ANN）、卷积神经网络（CNN）和长短期记忆网络（LSTM），与其对应的量子变体——量子SVM（QSVM）、量子神经网络（QNN）、量子CNN（QCNN）和量子LSTM（QLSTM）进行系统对比。所有模型均以来自CERN开放数据门户的模拟质子-质子碰撞事件为训练数据，事件包含电子-正电子和缪子-反缪子末态，以横动量分量作为输入特征、横动量大小作为回归目标。此外还进行了浅层多项式拟合的基线分析以确认回归问题的非平凡性。

Result: 在当前硬件和数据集约束下，经典架构整体上取得略优的定量性能，其中CNN和LSTM表现最为突出。量子模型以显著更少的可训练参数达到竞争性精度：QCNN仅使用四个量子比特和电路深度三即可复现深层经典CNN的性能，展现出真正的参数效率优势。基线分析确认该回归问题对浅层多项式拟合而言是非平凡的，从而支持架构比较本身的相关性。

Conclusion: 本研究刻画了在现实、资源受限条件下经典与量子方法之间的权衡关系，并为未来在实际量子硬件上的研究提供了基准。量子模型在参数效率上的优势表明，近端量子设备（near-term quantum devices）上的量子机器学习在高能物理计算中具有潜在价值。

Abstract: The classification and regression of particle collision events constitute a persistent computational challenge in experimental high energy physics, where large volumes of simulated data must be processed with both speed and precision. This work carries out a systematic comparison of four classical machine learning architectures, support vector machines (SVM), artificial neural networks (ANN), convolutional neural networks (CNN), and long short-term memory (LSTM) networks against their quantum counterparts: quantum SVM (QSVM), quantum neural networks (QNN), quantum CNN (QCNN), and quantum LSTM (QLSTM). All models are trained on simulated proton-proton collision events with electron-positron and muon-antimuon final states from the CERN Open Data portal, using transverse-momentum components as input features and transverse-momentum magnitude as the regression target. Classical architectures, and in particular the CNN and LSTM, achieve marginally better quantitative performance under current hardware and dataset constraints. Quantum models, however, reach competitive accuracy with substantially fewer trainable parameters: the QCNN reproduces the performance of the deep classical CNN using only four qubits and a circuit of depth three, pointing to a genuine parameter-efficiency advantage on near-term quantum devices. A baseline analysis confirms that the regression problem is non-trivial for shallow polynomial fits, supporting the relevance of the architectural comparison. These results characterize the trade-offs between classical and quantum approaches under realistic, resource-constrained conditions and provide a benchmark for future studies on actual quantum hardware.

</details>


### [53] [Euclidean Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28425)
*Nathanael Bosch,Niklas Frederik Schmitz,Michael F. Herbst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出欧几里得傅里叶神经算子（Euclidean Fourier Neural Operator, EFNO），通过将谱核函数参数化为物理波矢量的连续函数，解决了传统傅里叶神经算子（FNO）在跨域迁移时因离散谱权重依赖于周期性域而无法保持算子一致性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统傅里叶神经算子虽然在构造上独立于训练和评估时的网格分辨率，但其离散谱权重由整数傅里叶模态编号索引并对应物理波矢量，使得同一组训练好的权重在不同周期域上作用于不同波矢量，导致FNO在迁移到不同域时静默地表示不同的算子，因而无法适用于跨域迁移任务。

Method: 作者提出将谱核函数参数化为物理波矢量的连续函数，从而打破FNO中离散模态编号与具体周期域之间的耦合。这种连续参数化方式使EFNO能够学习在形状和大小各异的周期域上表现一致的算子。

Result: 在简单的热传导方程以及一个实际材料科学任务（学习不同晶体结构间的交换关联势）上进行了评估，实验证明EFNO能够泛化到未见过的网格尺寸和域。

Conclusion: EFNO作为域无关的傅里叶神经算子替代方案，在需要跨域迁移的应用场景中展现出优于传统FNO的能力，为算子学习在材料科学等实际领域的应用提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Fourier neural operators (FNOs) provide an efficient framework for learning mappings between function spaces as they are, by construction, independent of the grid resolution at which they are trained and evaluated. However, FNOs are not independent of the periodic domain they are applied to: their discrete spectral weights are indexed by integer Fourier mode numbers, which correspond to physical wavevectors. When applied to a different domain, the same trained weights act at different wavevectors, and the FNO silently represents a different operator. This makes FNOs unsuitable for tasks where transfer across domains is crucial. We propose Euclidean Fourier neural operators~(EFNOs) as a domain-independent alternative to FNOs. By parameterizing the spectral kernel as a continuous function of the physical wavevector, the EFNO can learn operators that act consistently across periodic domains of varying shape and size. We evaluate the EFNO on a simple heat equation and on a practically relevant materials science task of learning exchange-correlation potentials across different crystal structures, and demonstrate that the EFNO is able to generalize to unseen grid sizes and domains.

</details>

### [54] [On the Formality of Configuration Spaces of $\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^{n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28200)
*Si Li,Peng Yang,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文完整分类了 $\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^n$ 上构型空间（configuration space）的形式性（formality），并引入了一种可构造的 de Rham–Dolbeault 上同调理论来建模该类构型空间的 CDGA（交换微分分次代数）模型。论文证明当 $(n'=0, n\ge2)$ 或 $(n'=1, n\ge1)$ 时 CDGA 是非形式的，而当 $n'\ge2, n\ge1$ 时通过可容许图（admissible diagrams）的图式 CDGA 与正则化构型空间积分建立显式拟同构，从而证明其形式性，并将其应用于拓扑-全纯场论（topological-holomorphic field theory）的算子代数研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构型空间的形式性是理解空间拓扑与代数结构之间关系的核心问题。该论文旨在对 $\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^n$ 上构型空间的 CDGA 模型提供完整的形式性分类，并探索其在拓扑-全纯场论中算子代数结构上的应用。

Method: 论文定义了一种可构造的 de Rham–Dolbeault 上同调理论，为 $\Conf_m(\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^n)$ 提供可构造的 CDGA 模型。对于非形式情形直接判定；对于 $n'\ge2, n\ge1$ 的情形，作者利用可容许图组成的图式 CDGA（diagrammatic CDGA）以及正则化构型空间积分（regularized configuration space integral），构造了 CDGA 与其上同调之间的显式拟同构（quasi-isomorphism）。

Result: 得到完整分类：当 $(n'=0, n\ge2)$ 或 $(n'=1, n\ge1)$ 时，构型空间的 CDGA 模型非形式；当 $n'\ge2, n\ge1$ 时，模型是形式的。作为应用，论文证明了 $\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^n$（$n'\ge2, n\ge1$）上拓扑-全纯场论的局部算子代数（local operator algebra）同伦等价于顶点代数（vertex algebra）的高维类比对象。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了 $\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^n$ 构型空间形式性的完整刻画，揭示了形式和性与空间维度参数的精确依赖关系，并建立了形式性与拓扑-全纯场论算子代数结构之间的深层联系，为高维顶点代数理论提供了新的框架基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a complete classification of the formality of configuration spaces of $\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^{n}$. We define a constructible de Rham-Dolbeault cohomology theory which provides a constructible CDGA (commutative differential graded algebra) model of $\Conf_m(\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^{n})$. For $(n'=0,n\ge2)$ or $(n'=1,n\ge1)$, the CDGAs are non-formal. For $n'\ge2,n\ge1$, we establish an explicit quasi-isomorphism between the constructible CDGA and its cohomology by using a diagrammatic CDGA of admissible diagrams and a regularized configuration space integral, which leads to the formality. As an application, we show that the local operator algebra of a topological-holomorphic field theory on $\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^{n}$ ($n'\ge2,n\ge1$) is homotopically equivalent to a higher dimensional analog of vertex algebras.

</details>

### [55] [Two-dimensional quantum Griffith singularity in three-dimensional ZrN$_x$ superconducting films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28166)
*Zi-Yan Han,Li-Min Yu,Yu-Cheng Cong,Yang Yang,Zhi-Xiang Sun,Zhi-Qing Li*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在约200 nm厚的外延ZrN$_x$超导薄膜中实验观测到二维量子Griffiths奇异性（2D QGS），尽管该薄膜在超导性质上表现为三维（3D），但其在量子相变附近的磁电阻等温线遵循二维超导系统的幂律标度行为，有效临界指数$z\nu$随温度降低而增大并发散，归因于本征缺陷（如Zr空位和N间隙原子）引起的淬灭无序所导致的非均匀超导稀有区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Griffiths奇异性（quantum Griffiths singularity）此前主要在二维超导系统中被观测，但在三维超导体中是否存在QGS尚不明确。本研究旨在探索三维外延ZrN$_x$超导薄膜中是否会出现QGS现象，以检验无序诱导的非均匀性在量子相变临界行为中的普适作用。

Method: 制备了具有岩盐结构的约200 nm厚外延ZrN$_x$超导薄膜，测量其在垂直于和平行于薄膜平面的磁场下的低温磁电阻等温线；利用二维超导系统理论预测的幂律标度关系分析磁电阻数据以获得有效临界指数$z\nu$；并检验超导体-绝缘体或超导体-金属转变附近的激活标度形式，以验证无限随机临界点（infinite-randomness critical point）行为。

Result: 对于$x \gtrsim 1.30$的薄膜，不同方向的磁电阻等温线在较宽的磁场区间内独立交叉而非汇聚于单一交点；尽管超导性具有宏观三维性质，磁电阻等温线仍遵循二维超导系统的幂律标度而非三维标度；有效临界指数$z\nu$随温度降低而增大并在接近量子相变时发散；电阻率数据服从二维超导系统基于无限随机临界点的激活标度形式。

Conclusion: QGS可存在于三维超导体中，其起源是本征缺陷诱导的淬灭无序产生的空间非均匀超导稀有区域（rare regions），这些稀有区域在量子临界点附近可能表现出有效的二维动力学特征并主导系统的输运性质。研究结果为量子Griffiths奇异性在三维系统中的存在提供了有力证据，凸显了无序诱导的非均匀性在量子相变临界行为中的关键作用。

Abstract: We report the experimental observation of two-dimensional (2D) quantum Griffiths singularity (QGS) in $\sim$200-nm-thick epitaxial ZrN$_x$ superconducting films. The films possess a rock-salt structure and are three-dimensional (3D) with respect to superconductivity. For each film with $x \gtrsim 1.30$, the low-temperature magnetoresistance isotherms under fields perpendicular and parallel to the film plane cross over at a broad magnetic field range independently rather than at a single crossing point. Despite the macroscopic 3D nature of the superconductivity, the magnetoresistance isotherms at selected adjacent temperatures follow the theoretical prediction of power-law scaling for 2D superconducting systems, rather than that for 3D systems. The effective critical exponent $zν$, obtained by analyzing the magnetoresistance isotherms using the 2D power-law scaling, increases with decreasing temperature and diverges as the quantum phase transition is approached. In addition, the resistivity data near the superconductor-insulator or superconductor-metal transitions obey an activated scaling form that describes the quantum phase transition of 2D superconducting systems governed by an infinite-randomness critical point. The QGS in the ZrN$_x$ films is attributed to quenched disorder induced by intrinsic defects, such as Zr vacancies and N interstitials, which creates spatially inhomogeneous superconducting rare regions. The dynamics of these rare regions, which may exhibit effective 2D characteristics near the quantum critical point, dominate the transport properties of the system near the quantum phase transition. Our results provide compelling evidence for the existence of QGS in 3D superconductors and highlight the crucial role of disorder-induced inhomogeneity in determining the critical behavior of quantum phase transitions.

</details>

These results are consistent with commensurate SDW order with a single spin-spinless (or large-tiny spin) stripe. As for the O(1) sites that bridge the NiO$_2$ planes, the internal field is nearly canceled below $T_{\rm SDW}$, indicating an antiparallel spin configuration between adjacent planes. However, below $T_\text{A}$ ($\sim$ 115 K), the spectrum of the O(1) site disappears even though the in-plane SDW order remains robust, implying that the antiparallel spin configuration through the Ni--O(1)--Ni bond is not particularly stable below $T_{\rm A}$, despite the expected strong interlayer spin coupling between the NiO$_2$ planes. Above all, we emphasize that the local spin susceptibility is extremely small at the O(2) site that has a strong covalency with the $d_{3z^2-r^2}$ orbital, indicating a well-developed interlayer spin-singlet formation in the Ni-$d_{3z^2-r^2}$ orbitals bridging the NiO$_2$ planes. These findings shed new light on the interlayer spin-singlet formation and the anomalous spin reconfiguration through the $\text{Ni--O(1)--Ni}$ bonding orbitals connecting the NiO$_2$ planes, which characterize the orbital-selective nature of the bilayer nickelate La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$.

Abstract: We investigate reversible internal-state-selective wave-packet transport induced by spin-dependent Peierls phases in a two-dimensional nearest-neighbor Lieb lattice. A band-resolved calculation in the $q=2$ magnetic unit cell shows that the spin-up spectrum contains two zero-energy flat subbands associated with the sublattice-imbalance flat-band sector, whereas the remaining four subbands retain finite bandwidths. The half-flux sector therefore fails the all-bands-flat condition and does not realize exact Aharonov--Bohm caging for a generic localized initial state. Nevertheless, real-time simulations reveal a pronounced suppression of spin-up propagation relative to the dispersive spin-down component, manifested by a smaller mean-square displacement and an enhanced finite-region retention probability over the pre-reflection time window. Reversing the state-dependent flux interchanges the slow and fast spin channels, while the dynamical contrast remains robust against moderate flux detuning. These results establish spin-dependent synthetic flux as a reversible means of controlling internal-state-resolved matter-wave transport without spin-flip processes or interactions, and provide complementary spectral and real-space criteria for distinguishing exact caging from finite-time dynamical slowing in atomic and photonic flat-band simulators.

Method: 采用协伴随轨道有效场论方法，该方法能够以玻色化自由度（bosonized degrees of freedom）系统地引入广义朗道相互作用，并将费米子圈（fermionic loop）贡献映射为更简单的树级（tree-level）图。论文针对一般各向同性色散 ε(p) 在广义朗道相互作用线性阶 O(F^(2,0)) 下计算密度三点关联函数的修正。

Conclusion: 相互作用效应可以实质性修改具有拓扑探测功能的密度三点关联函数，其影响依赖于能带色散的曲率。这提示在利用密度三点关联函数作为拓扑探针时，必须考虑相互作用修正的潜在影响。

Abstract: Density three-point correlations are known to probe the topology of the Fermi sea in two-dimensional noninteracting systems. Here, we study how these correlations are modified by interactions using the coadjoint-orbit effective field theory. A key advantage of the coadjoint-orbit formulation is that it provides a systematic way to incorporate generalized Landau interactions in terms of bosonized degrees of freedom, mapping fermionic loop contributions onto simpler tree-level diagrams. We show that, for a general isotropic dispersion $ε(p)$, even at linear order in the generalized Landau interaction, $\mathcal{O}(\mathcal{F}^{(2,0)})$, there exists a contribution proportional to the band curvature $ε''(p_F)$ that changes the nonanalytic structure of the free density three-point correlation function.
  This contribution introduces a distinct nonanalytic structure beyond that found in either the noninteracting case or an interacting Galilean-invariant system, showing that interaction effects can modify the topology-detecting density three-point correlation.

</details>

### [62] [A first-principles binary neutron star merger model of GW170817, GRB170817A, and AT2017gfo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27555)

Method: 采用端到端模拟流程：首先进行基于第一性原理的广义相对论磁流体力学（general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamics）中微子辐射转移合并模拟，随后执行核合成（nucleosynthesis）计算和光子辐射转移（photon radiative transfer），最终生成千新星光变曲线。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了统一的第一性原理框架，可用于跨引力波、伽马射线暴和千新星三种观测通道解释双中子星并合事件。

Abstract: The multimessenger observation of the binary neutron star merger event GW170817, associated with its electromagnetic counterparts GRB170817A and AT2017gfo, marked a milestone in astrophysics, yet its unified physical explanation remains elusive. We conduct an end-to-end simulation based on a first-principles general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamics neutrino-radiation transfer merger simulation, followed by nucleosynthesis calculations and photon radiative transfer to generate kilonova light curves. We show that the large-scale dynamo simultaneously produces a relativistic jet with an isotropic-equivalent luminosity of $\sim 10^{51}~{\rm erg~s^{-1}}$ and $\approx 0.08M_\odot$ of neutron-rich ejecta, reproducing the GRB170817A afterglow and the AT2017gfo kilonova light curves. Our results establish a unified first-principles framework for interpreting binary neutron star mergers across gravitational wave, gamma-ray burst, and kilonova observations.

</details>

Method: 该研究通过理论分析考察超子体黏滞在中子星并合（inspiral）阶段对引力波相位演化的贡献，同时评估其对中子星超子核心温度的加热效应。

Conclusion: 超子体黏滞对引力波信号的影响在可预见的探测能力下是不可观测的，但其热效应可能对中子星内部的热演化具有重要意义，值得在后续研究中进一步关注。

Abstract: The paper revisits the role of hyperon bulk viscosity during neutron-star inspiral. We find that hyperon bulk viscosity exerts only a minor influence on the gravitational-wave phase, causing a phase shift of $\sim 10^{-3}\,\rm rad$. This shift is too small to be detected by existing gravitational-wave observatories, and it seems unlikely that next-generation detectors will be able to isolate this effect from other factors. However, our analysis indicates that hyperon bulk viscosity can significantly heat the hyperon core of a neutron star, raising its temperature to approximately $5 \times 10^8\,\rm K$.

Abstract: We investigate the spherical accretion onto a static Konoplya Zhidenko black hole and examine how the deformation of the spacetime affects the dynamics of the accreting matter. By applying the conservation laws of particle number and energy-momentum, we formulate the accretion problem and derive the corresponding Hamiltonian describing the fluid motion. The critical points and sonic transitions are then analyzed for ultra stiff, ultra relativistic, radiation, and sub relativistic fluids. We study the dependence of the critical point structure and flow trajectories on the KZ deformation and compare the results with those obtained for asymptotically safe, Schwarzschild MOG, and Reissner Nordström black holes. The comparison shows that the location and behavior of the critical points vary significantly with the underlying BH geometry and the equation of state of the fluid. In particular, the KZ deformation produces noticeable changes in the radial structure of the accretion flow, with different effects observed for the considered fluid models. These results provide a comparative picture of spherical accretion in different black hole spacetimes and help clarify how modifications of the background geometry influence matter dynamics in the strong field region.

</details>

Conclusion: 该统一模型能够跨多种器件架构、运行条件和材料体系精确描述新兴晶体管的电学特性，为业界提供了一种通用而高效的仿真工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a unified compact model for emerging transistor technologies, including oxide-semiconductor field-effect transistors (OSFETs), 2D material FETs (2DFETs), carbon nanotube FETs (CNFETs), and cryogenic MOSFETs. A unified charge-density formulation is developed to account for quantum confinement, trap charges, and band-tail states in channel charge calculations. A physics-based transport model is introduced to seamlessly capture carrier transport from the long-channel diffusive regime to the short-channel ballistic limit. Scaling models are incorporated to accurately describe 2D electrostatic effects. Cryogenic operation is modeled through the inclusion of band-tail states and temperature-dependent mobility and threshold voltage. The proposed model is validated against experimental data from the fabricated OSFETs with multiple channel lengths and published measurements of 2DFETs, CNFETs, and cryogenic MOSFETs. Excellent agreement is demonstrated across diverse device architectures, operating conditions, and material systems.

</details>

Conclusion: 该研究确立了绿色计算在高能物理和天体物理领域的重要性和基本研究方向，为后续的能源评估、技术优化和跨领域合作提供了明确的研究议程。

Abstract: This research topic aims to explore and promote strategies for green computing in high-energy physics and astrophysics. The primary objectives include assessing current energy consumption patterns, identifying opportunities for resource optimization, and fostering cross-disciplinary collaborations to enhance sustainability. Key questions to be addressed include: What are the current energy demands in these fields? How can existing technologies be leveraged for more efficient resource usage? What synergies can be identified between high-energy and astrophysics projects to promote green computing?

</details>


### [67] [Vectorized Symmetric and Fermionic Tensor Network Implementations for GPU-Accelerated Variational Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27861)
*Si-Jing Du,Johnnie Gray,Ao Chen,Garnet Kin-Lic Chan*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量网络（tensor networks, TN）的变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo, VMC）算法的GPU向量化实现方案，通过发展块稀疏张量的“平坦”张量网络（flat tensor network）形式化框架，实现了abelian对称张量网络（含费米子张量网络）的高效批量计算，在二维Fermi–Hubbard模型中获得了相较于单核CPU最高300倍的GPU加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于张量网络的VMC计算（TN-VMC）在强关联自旋和费米子体系的基态计算中已展现出具有竞争力的精度，但现有TN-VMC算法尚未以能充分利用GPU加速的方式被表述，缺少的关键环节是张量网络振幅和张量网络操作的向量化求值。

Method: 本文通过批量处理相同结构的计算来确保高GPU利用率，针对实践中相关的abelian对称张量网络（包括费米子张量网络）场景，发展了一种用于块稀疏张量表示与收缩的“平坦”张量网络形式化方法，并在此基础上构建了GPU适配的对称TN-VMC工作流程，实现带有批量化的张量网络计算。

Result: 在二维Fermi–Hubbard模型中，使用费米子张量网络变分波函数的实现相较于单核CPU实现获得了高达300倍的GPU加速比。

Conclusion: 所提出的向量化方法与GPU适配的对称TN-VMC工作流程有效填补了TN-VMC算法中GPU加速利用的空白，为大规模强关联体系的张量网络基态变分计算提供了高效的硬件加速路径。

Abstract: Variational Monte Carlo (VMC) calculations based on tensor networks (TN) have recently achieved competitive accuracy in ground-state calculations of strongly correlated spin and fermionic systems. However, existing tensor network VMC (TN-VMC) algorithms have not been formulated in a manner that can fully utilize GPU acceleration. We tackle the key missing ingredient, namely, the vectorized evaluation of tensor network amplitudes and tensor network operations. This ensures high GPU utilization by batching over computations with identical structure. In particular, we show how to achieve vectorization for the practically relevant case of abelian symmetric tensor networks (which includes fermionic tensor networks) by developing a ``flat'' tensor network formalism for block-sparse tensor representation and contraction. Using this, we construct a GPU-adapted symmetric TN-VMC workflow with batched tensor network computation. In the two-dimensional Fermi--Hubbard model, we demonstrate a GPU speedup of up to $300 \times$ over single core CPU implementations, for fermionic TN variational wavefunctions.

</details>

### [68] [Adaptive workforce exploration in complex productivity landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27656)

TL;DR: 本研究借鉴进化生物学中的NK模型（NK model of rugged landscapes），构建了包含专家型和通才型两类员工的组织分工模型，探究任务相互依赖塑造的生产力景观崎岖度如何影响组织优化劳动分工和满足市场需求的能力，并通过群体选择算法揭示非线性适应动力学的涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业化分工和任务分配是提升系统效率和创新能力的关键机制，从生物有机体到社会经济制度均如此，理解任务分布演化及其对组织生产力的影响对于优化组织运行至关重要。

Method: 采用基于NK模型的组织分工模型，模型中包含两类员工——专家型（specialist）员工固定分配至单一任务，通才型（generalist）员工在每个时间步随机选择任务；通过群体选择算法（group selection algorithms）进行仿真分析，考察由任务相互依赖决定的生产力景观崎岖度对组织劳动分工优化能力的影响。

Result: 研究发现该模型能够揭示非线性适应性动力学（nonlinear adaptive dynamics）的涌现，展示了任务相互依赖的崎岖度如何影响组织在分工优化和市场需求响应方面的绩效表现。

Conclusion: 该研究为企业在面对市场需求变化时调整任务分配策略、促进创新提供了理论洞见，表明组织需要根据任务依赖结构选择适宜的专家型与通才型员工配置策略。

Abstract: Specialization and task allocation enhance efficiency and innovation across diverse systems, from biological organisms to socioeconomic institutions. The evolution of task distribution and its influence on organizational productivity encapsulate the dynamics between task dependencies and adaptive strategies. We explore the organizational division of labor, inspired by the NK model of rugged landscapes, which is widely applied in evolutionary biology, and incorporate interdependencies among the attributes of technical experts within an organization. Our model considers two types of employees characterized by their task allocation strategies: specialists, who are permanently assigned to a single task, and generalists, who stochastically select a task at each time step. We investigate how the ruggedness of the productivity landscape, shaped by task interdependency, affects the organization's capacity to optimize labor division and meet market demands. Using group selection algorithms, we reveal the emergence of nonlinear adaptive dynamics, providing insights into how companies can adapt their strategies to meet market demands and foster innovation.

</details>


### [69] [Bound state of a He-3 atom at free He-4 surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27664)
*Massimo Boninsegni*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过量子蒙特卡洛（Quantum Monte Carlo）模拟，本文研究了He-3原子在超流He-4表面及纳米尺度He-4团簇上的束缚状态。在三维自由表面上He-3以约4 K的束缚能被局域在约10 Å厚的低密度表面层内，温度升至约1 K以上时He-3会脱离表面溶解到超流中；而在二维情形下则不存在表面束缚态，且He-3被排除在超流之外。此外，研究还表明在足够小的He-4团簇（数十个原子）上，He-3束缚态不仅存在于此前报道的Cs基底上，而是存在于所有碱金属基底上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: He-3原子与超流He-4表面的相互作用是理解稀薄3He-4He混合体系的重要问题，涉及低温物理中的表面束缚态、维度效应以及纳米尺度体系中的物理行为。本文旨在通过量子蒙特卡洛模拟，系统研究He-3在三维与二维超流He-4表面以及纳米尺度He-4团簇上的束缚行为，并探索弱基底上团簇体系可能揭示的扩展表面物理。

Method: 采用量子蒙特卡洛（Quantum Monte Carlo）模拟方法，对三维自由超流He-4表面上单个He-3原子的束缚态进行数值计算，并同时考察二维体系及吸附于弱基底上的纳米尺寸He-4团簇（含数十个原子）的情形。通过改变温度和系统维度，分析He-3束缚能、局域化及其对基底类型的依赖性。

Result: 在三维自由表面，He-3原子以约4 K的束缚能被局域在约10 Å厚的低密度He-4表面层内；当温度升至约1 K以上时，He-3脱离表面溶入超流。在二维体系中，He-3被排除出超流且不存在表面束缚态。对于纳米团簇体系，He-3束缚态局域在足够小（数十个原子）团簇的周界上，且这一束缚态存在于所有碱金属基底上，而不仅限于此前报道的Cs基底。

Conclusion: He-3在超流He-4表面的束缚行为具有显著的维度依赖性：三维存在稳定的表面束缚态，而二维则完全缺失。温度是控制He-3表面吸附与溶解的关键参数。纳米尺度He-4团簇可为观测扩展表面物理提供可行平台，且其He-3束缚态对碱金属基底具有普适性。

Abstract: Quantum Monte Carlo simulations confirm the existence of a bound state of a single He-3 atom at a free superfluid He-4 surface in three dimensions, localized within a ~10-A thick low-density He-4 surface layer, with a binding energy of approximately 4 K with respect to vacuum. As the temperature is raised above ~ 1 K the He-3 atom leaves the surface to dissolve into the superfluid. The situation is entirely different in two dimensions, as the He-3 atom is excluded from the superfluid and no surface bound state exists. Results are also presented for the a He-3 atom binding to nanoscale size He-4 clusters adsorbed on weak substrates, a physical system that may allow for the observation of some of the physics predicted for extended surfaces. It is shown that a He-3 bound state localized at the perimeter of sufficiently small (a few tens of atoms) clusters exists not just on Cs, as previously reported, but on all alkali substrates.

</details>


### [70] [Solvable relaxation in discrete unitary systems: Ruelle-Pollicott resonances and CMV matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28575)
*Urban Duh,Friedrich Hübner,Marko Žnidarič*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究幺正多体系统中截断传播子的主导特征值，即Ruelle-Pollicott（RP）共振，在CMV矩阵（幺正传播子的典范形式）框架下获得了RP共振及相关范数发散本征矢的一系列精确结果。通过圆上正交多项式理论，识别出两种动力学相位：第一种相位中弛豫源自局部算子的非局域化演变，第二种相位中算子回流反而加速弛豫，且RP共振隐藏在病态特征值的环中而非等同于截断传播子的特征值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruelle-Pollicott共振为理解幺正多体系统的动力学提供了一种优雅的途径，但此前对其与截断传播子特征值之间的关系、以及在不同CMV矩阵类别中的精确行为缺乏系统理解。作者旨在通过数学上成熟的CMV矩阵框架和圆上正交多项式理论填补这一空白。

Method: 将幺正传播子的典范形式（即CMV矩阵）作为研究对象，对最简单的单边移位加杂质CMV类模型，推导出RP共振的闭式解，并验证截断传播子、预解式解析延拓和rigged Hilbert空间三种方法给出相同的RP共振结果。对更实际的CMV矩阵，利用圆上正交多项式的丰富理论分析系统的两种动力学相位。

Result: 对于最简单CMV类，三种独立获取RP共振的方法（截断传播子、预解式的解析延拓、rigged Hilbert空间方法）得到一致结果。在更实际的CMV矩阵中识别出两个相位：第一相位弛豫由局部算子演化为非局域算子且回流可忽略所致；第二相位因大算子回流而弛豫更快，但此时RP共振不等于截断传播子的特征值，而是藏在病态特征值构成的环内。

Conclusion: RP共振与截断传播子特征值的关系并非一成不变，取决于具体CMV矩阵类别；在混沌系统典型的渐近移位动力学中，存在两种截然不同的弛豫相位，其中算子回流在第二相位中起到加速弛豫的出乎意料的作用。

Abstract: Leading eigenvalues of the truncated propagator, known as Ruelle-Pollicott (RP) resonances, are an elegant way of addressing the dynamics of unitary many-body systems. We study unitary propagators in their canonical form, known in the mathematical literature as the CMV matrices, and obtain a number of exact results for RP resonances and the associated norm-diverging eigenvectors. For the simplest CMV class describing a unilateral shift with an impurity, motivated by operator dynamics in dual-unitary circuits, we obtain closed-form results and in particular show that the three independent ways of obtaining RP resonances -- the truncated propagator, analytic continuation of the resolvent, and the rigged Hilbert space approach -- all give the same results. In more realistic CMV matrices, in which shift-like operator dynamics characteristic of chaotic systems is only asymptotic, we rely on the rich theory of orthogonal polynomials on the unit circle and identify two phases. In the first phase, relaxation occurs due to local operators effectively evolving into increasingly nonlocal ones with negligible backflow. Especially interesting is the second phase, which, surprisingly, exhibits faster relaxation because of contributions from the backflow of large operators. Additionally, in the second phase, RP resonances are not equal to the eigenvalues of the truncated propagator, instead, they are ``hidden'' within a ring of ill-conditioned eigenvalues.

</details>
