Abstract: Thermodynamic computers are stochastic physical devices designed to perform calculations at the thermal energy scale. Their operation is constrained by the equations of stochastic thermodynamics, among which are a set of bounds, known as speed limits, that relate a thermodynamic computer's run time to its computational progress and the heat it dissipates. Using the Wasserstein speed limit we assess the thermodynamic efficiency of a simulation model of a thermodynamic computer trained to perform a standard machine-learning classification task. On this task the thermodynamic computer is as capable as a simple multilayer perceptron. We show that different inference protocols allow the computer to operate within 40\% of the thermodynamic limit of efficiency without loss of accuracy, or to perform inference increasingly rapidly at fixed accuracy and thermodynamic efficiency. These results indicate that a thermodynamic computer designed for a particular task retains considerable freedom in its thermodynamic operation.

Method: 研究采用一种近期提出的流体动力学模型（hydrodynamic model），该模型描述由淬火速度切向平流（tangentially advected）的活性表面。研究在二维和三维空间中分别分析非互易XY系统在淬火无序作用下的序参数行为，并通过调节淬火无序的横度（transversality）程度来研究系统的相变特性。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论：淬火无序的横度是控制系统有序化行为的关键参数，可以通过调节该参数在固定无序和噪声方差下诱导相变。这一结果表明，长程淬火无序可以反转其通常抑制有序的角色，在非互易驱动系统中起到生成有序的作用。

Abstract: Can quenched disorder generate ordering in driven systems? Using a recently proposed hydrodynamic model, we show that sufficiently long-ranged quenched disorder can induce long-range order in two-dimensional (2D) nonreciprocal XY systems, even when the clean system exhibits only short-range order at finite noise. Active surfaces tangentially advected by quenched velocities, governed by the same hydrodynamic equation, become statistically flat with super- or subdiffusive relaxation. In three dimensions (3D), quenched disorder combined with nonreciprocity produces a novel transition between strong-coupling and asymptotically noninteracting regimes, supporting either long- or short-range order. In both 2D and 3D, the exponents are nonuniversal, which vary continuously with the degree of transversality of the quenched disorder. The degree of transversality of the quenched disorder can be tuned to induce transitions in the model for fixed disorder and noise variances.

</details>

Abstract: Collective behaviour in living systems is usually modelled as the outcome of a \emph{direct} social drive: agents are rewarded, or hard-wired, to align with or approach their neighbours. Here we show that aggregation can instead emerge from an \emph{indirect} objective. We let reinforcement learning foragers, initially performing a random walk, optimize their dynamics from a purely individual reward for finding replenishable targets, while perceiving only their conspecifics and never the targets themselves. As the visual range grows, the agents undergo a sharp crossover from an environment-tuned individual search to a scale-agnostic collective one, and this crossover coincides with the onset of spatial aggregation. Thus a collective phase arises as a by-product of optimal foraging, without any direct reward for grouping. A minimal analytical first-passage model reproduces the transition as a crossover between the two search strategies. Our results identify indirect, resource-driven reward as a generic route to emergent collective phenomena.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文推广了一维带电单位线段随机顺序吸附（random sequential adsorption, RSA）模型，其中沉积位置由端点和入射粒子的电荷所限定的β核（beta kernel）决定。通过推导四分量母函数方程，论文给出了完整占据统计的精确递推关系，并证明了在相互作用参数趋近1时，边界电荷虽影响有限尺寸行为，但四个边界态的渐近均值与方差密度相同，从而证明了阻塞密度具有自平均（self-averaging）性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀停车场模型与强局域化沉积之间缺乏可解析处理的理论连接，作者希望引入电荷相互作用参数来插值两者，并系统研究电荷选择性对堆积覆盖率与涨落的影响。

Result: 数值结果表明，电荷选择性会提高覆盖密度（coverage），尤其当混合体系中由相反端点电荷主导时更为显著；在λ接近1处，这些体系的涨落被大幅抑制。理论方面，论文证明了虽然边界电荷影响有限尺寸行为，但四个边界态具有相同的渐近均值与方差密度，从而证明了阻塞密度（jammed density）具有自平均性质。

Conclusion: 该模型为均匀RSA、动态生成的无序（dynamically generated disorder）以及相互作用驱动的沉积之间提供了一个可解析处理的桥梁。

Abstract: We generalize a one-dimensional random sequential adsorption model (RSA) of charged unit segments in which the deposition position follows a beta kernel determined by the charges bounding the available gap and those of the incoming particle. The interaction parameter \(0\leqλ<1\) interpolates between uniform car parking and strongly localized deposition. We derive a four component generating function equation that provides exact recursions for the complete occupation statistics, although only the mean and variance are analyzed in detail here. Exact finite-shell solutions serve as benchmarks for the Gauss--Jacobi quadrature and Monte Carlo simulations. We prove that, although boundary charges affect finite size behavior, the four boundary states have the same asymptotic mean and variance densities, consequently, we prove that the jammed density is self-averaging. Numerical results show that charge selectivity increases the coverage, especially for mixtures dominated by opposite endpoint charges, which also exhibit strongly reduced fluctuations near \(λ=1\). The model provides a tractable connection between uniform RSA, dynamically generated disorder, and interaction-driven deposition.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光学薄情形下，黑洞周围的引力透镜会对任何局域源产生多个延迟像，即使无法分辨单个源，也可通过电影中强度涨落的两点时空相关函数来揭示这一效应。此前基于广义相对论磁流体动力学（general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamic, GRMHD）模拟的射线追踪（ray tracing）电影验证了该想法的可行性，但真实观测存在各种数据损坏因素，需要评估这些因素对极端透镜信号识别的影响。

Method: 作者在原有相关函数方法基础上进一步发展，针对真实观测局限所启发的多种可能的数据损坏类型（如模糊化、噪声、采样不足等）进行比较研究，分别考察这些损坏对原始电影和模糊后电影中极端透镜特征识别的影响。此外，提出一种新分析方法，将定义在五维配置空间上的完整相关函数数据约简为二维直方图。

Result: 所探讨的数据损坏类型本身不会阻碍相关测量中极端透镜效应的识别。基于分析结果估算，对于M87*的成功相关测量，需要连续监测约2年以上；对于Sgr A*，测量需以每分钟至少1帧的采样频率进行。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope, EHT）及其扩展对M87*和Sgr A*的观测提供了实际指导，表明即使在真实观测限制下，通过相关函数方法仍有望识别极端透镜效应。提出的二维直方图约简方法有望为黑洞参数推断提供新的工具。

Abstract: In an optically thin setting, gravitational lensing around a black hole creates multiple delayed images of any localized source, appearing at different positions on the observer's screen. Even if particular sources cannot be singled out, this effect may be revealed in a black-hole movie (time-dependent image) by examining the two-point spatiotemporal correlation function of intensity fluctuations in the movie. Recently, the correlation function derived from a movie obtained by ray tracing of a general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulation demonstrated the plausibility of this idea, even when the movie was blurred to angular resolutions corresponding to next-generation terrestrial very-long-baseline interferometric arrays. In this work, we further develop this method and study how some possible corruptions of the movie, motivated by real observational limitations, affect the ability to identify the extreme-lensing signatures by correlations. The effects are compared for both the original and blurred movies. We discuss the implications of our analysis for upcoming observations of M87* and Sgr A*, the two main targets of the Event Horizon Telescope and its future extensions. Our case study suggests that the types of data corruption we have explored, in themselves, do not preclude an identification of extreme-lensing effects in correlation measurements. We use our results to roughly estimate that, most importantly, for a successful correlation measurement, M87* may need to be continuously monitored for $\gtrsim 2$ years, while Sgr A* may need to be measured at a cadence $\gtrsim 1$ frame per minute. Finally, we outline a new analysis method that reduces the data in the complete correlation function---a large dataset defined on a five-dimensional configuration space---to a two-dimensional histogram that could be useful for black-hole parameter inference.

</details>

Abstract: The reduction of mechanical presymplectic structures and its application to the reduction of time-dependent Hamiltonian systems were developed in a recent paper (Gutiérrez Sagredo et al, 2025). This approach overcomes some limitations of the original Albert reduction (Albert, 1989). In this paper we will describe the reduction of time-dependent Hamiltonian systems using the extended formalism. This process combines cotangent bundle reduction with reduction of presymplectic structures of corank 1 and 2. These results generalize previous work (Lacirasella et al, 2012), in the same direction. Several examples will be presented to illustrate the theory from this new perspective.

Abstract: We give a rigorous one-dimensional formulation of Kane's transport proposal for probing the Euler characteristic of a Fermi sea. For a clean, translation-invariant continuum Hamiltonian with real-analytic dispersion, a Fermi sea confined to a finite momentum range, and nonzero Fermi velocity at every Fermi point, we analyze a particular Abel-regularized transport response (obtained by tracing over the Fermi sea) and prove that its large-time limit equals the Euler characteristic. We derive an explicit finite-time formula, show that quantization requires the large-time limit, and obtain a convergence rate under additional nonstationarity assumptions. We also prove the lattice analog, where the sharp commutator is trace-class and a completely filled band contributes zero. Finite-volume calculations illustrate the prescribed order of the thermodynamic and large-time limits.

Motivation: 作者希望探究离散一维遍历薛定谔算子（ergodic Schrödinger operators）的谱性质在非周期性由几何而非势能体现时能在多大程度上保持，以此拓展对这一类系统的理解。

Method: 研究对象为受一维非周期平铺启发构建的 tiling 图与装饰 Z-图（decorated Z-graphs），分别配备标准拉普拉斯算子。通过证明谱为零勒贝格测度并呈广义康托集结构来刻画度量图；利用 Baire 泛型与勒贝格几乎必然的边长度选取论证装饰 Z-图的谱结构；随后借助 Schwartzman 群建立带状标号定理；最后通过分析 IDS 的跳跃间断及其周期逼近的组合结构解答 Sturmian 装饰 Z-图的 Dry Ten Martini 问题。

Abstract: In this thesis, we study the spectral properties of dynamically defined aperiodic metric and discrete graphs. Our goal is to determine to what extent spectral properties of discrete one-dimensional ergodic Schrödinger operators persist when the aperiodicity is manifested through the geometry rather than through a potential. The graphs considered here are inspired by one-dimensional aperiodic tilings, and are called tiling graphs and decorated $\mathbb{Z}$-graphs.

For decorated $\mathbb{Z}$-graphs, we further show that for a Baire-generic and Lebesgue almost-sure choice of the decoration edge lengths, the spectrum is a generalized Cantor set.

We prove a gap labelling theorem, which characterizes the set of possible values taken by the IDS inside spectral gaps. We show that the gap labels are contained in the Schwartzman group associated with the dynamical system generating the graph, up to a geometric scaling factor.
  Lastly, we consider the Dry Ten Martini Problem for discrete Sturmian decorated $\mathbb{Z}$-graphs, asking whether all possible values predicted by the gap labelling theorem are indeed attained by the IDS inside spectral gaps. We answer this question negatively, by identifying a large set of gap labels which are not attained due to jump discontinuities of the IDS. We then show that away from these jump discontinuities, the periodic approximants for Sturmian graphs display the same combinatorial structure as the standard Sturmian Hamiltonians, and use this to obtain an explicit characterization of the realized gap labels for Sturmian comb graphs.

Abstract: We study the holomorphy of the ground state and its energy with respect to the coupling constant for bosonic quadratic Hamiltonians. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解低维量子多粒子系统的能谱结构是数学物理的核心问题，特别是两个费米子在一维晶格上的谱性质尚需完整刻画。本文旨在给出该算子在本质谱边缘处的谱跃迁的完整描述，并阐明临界耦合处的共振现象。

Method: 利用关于总拟动量（total quasi-momentum）$k\in\mathbb T:=(-\pi,\pi]$的直接积分分解（direct-integral decomposition）将问题约化为作用在奇相对坐标空间（odd relative-coordinate space）上的纤维算子$H_\lambda(k)$，随后对该算子的谱进行细致分析。

Conclusion: 该工作对一维两费米子紧邻相互作用系统的谱性质给出了完整刻画，揭示了临界耦合处特征值—共振转变机制，并为强耦合极限下的特征函数局域化行为提供了严格分析。

Abstract: We study a two-particle lattice Schrödinger operator describing two identical fermions on the one-dimensional lattice $\mathbb Z$ with nearest-neighbor interaction of strength $λ\in\mathbb R$. Using the direct-integral decomposition with respect to the total quasi-momentum $k\in\mathbb T:=(-π,π]$, we reduce the problem to fiber operators $H_λ(k)$ acting in the odd relative-coordinate space. For $k\in(-π,π)$, the essential spectrum is \[ σ_{\mathrm{ess}}(H_λ(k)) =
[4-4\cos(k/2),\,4+4\cos(k/2)]. \] We give a complete description of the spectral transition at both edges. The operator has a unique simple eigenvalue outside the essential spectrum if and only if \[ |λ|>2\cos(k/2), \] in which case \[ E(k,λ) =
4+λ+\frac{4\cos^2(k/2)}λ. \] For $|λ|<2\cos(k/2)$ there is no discrete eigenvalue. At the critical coupling $|λ|=2\cos(k/2)$, the eigenvalue merges with the corresponding spectral edge and becomes a threshold resonance, with a bounded non-square-integrable odd solution. We also derive the threshold and strong-coupling asymptotics of the eigenvalue and eigenfunction. In the strong-coupling regime the eigenfunction localizes at the interaction sites, whereas at critical coupling it converges pointwise to the corresponding resonant solution. The exceptional fiber $k=π$, where the hopping vanishes and the essential spectrum collapses to $\{4\}$, is treated separately.

</details>

Abstract: We consider a class of discrete multi-time determinantal point processes whose correlation kernels admit a double-contour integral form, containing Schur processes as simple examples.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The lattice Green's functions of the cubic lattices are classical: each is a symmetric square of a second-order operator, with a closed form in complete elliptic integrals. The hyperkagome lattice, realized by the iridium sublattice of the spin-liquid candidate Na4Ir3O8, has resisted such a treatment: Varma and Monien reduced its density of states to a threefold integral they found no way to solve exactly. From exact lattice moments we obtain an irreducible third-order linear differential (Picard-Fuchs) operator annihilating the Green's function. It is not a literal symmetric square, yet its symmetric square carries a rational solution: the differential Galois group is orthogonal, and an order-two differential intertwiner makes the operator projectively a symmetric square. Our main result is that the underlying second-order operator is the uniformizing equation of the genus-zero modular curve of Gamma_0(30)+, the level-thirty group generated by Gamma_0(30) and all its Atkin-Lehner involutions, an explicit eta quotient parametrizing the natural variable. That uniformizing equation is itself not new - it is a row of the Lian-Yau table of genus-zero groups - so what is new here is the identification of a lattice Green's function with it, together with a proof: the Schwarzian identity is established by an a priori pole-degree bound rather than checked numerically, which also proves the tabulated entry. The hyperkagome Green's function is therefore modular at level thirty, supplying the closed form Varma and Monien sought. Its weight-two period is obtained explicitly: a depth-one quasimodular form which we prove is not a modular form times an algebraic function at any weight.

</details>


### [85] [Non-Closing Double-Commutator Flows and the Small-Coupling Limit of Spin-Boson Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28208)

TL;DR: 本文针对自旋玻色子模型（spin-boson model）的近似对角化问题，基于Brockett–Wegner双对易子流（double-commutator flow）建立了一个严格数学框架。该流在此类非二次型哈密顿量中不封闭，作者通过详细分析非封闭代数结构，显式界定了与自旋-玻色耦合强度相关的高阶误差项，首次为小耦合区间的若干启发式对角化技术提供了严格数学依据，并使基于流的系统高阶对角化与自能重整化（self-energy renormalization）算法成为可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋玻色子模型是开放量子系统（open quantum systems）的典型范例，也是基于单离子阱技术的量子计算机的理论范式，但除高度奇异情形外其完整谱对角化仍是开放问题。作者旨在为广义自旋玻色子体系建立一种严格的近似对角化框架，弥补现有启发式方法缺乏数学严谨性的不足。

Method: 作者借鉴Brockett–Wegner双对易子流的方法，该流由非线性微分方程驱动（这里作用于无界算子）。由于该流在自旋玻色子背景下不封闭，作者克服这一障碍，对由此产生的非封闭代数结构进行详细分析，从而显式地以耦合强度为参数界定了高阶误差项。方法在概念上类比多尺度分析（multi-scale analysis）以及Fröhlich与Pizzo的迭代局部Lie–Schwinger块对角化（block-diagonalization）方法。

Result: 这项工作在最小的非平凡情形下，首次为理论物理文献中广泛使用的小耦合区间启发式对角化技术提供了严格数学证明。框架使基于流的算法可系统性地用于高阶对角化和自能重整化。

Conclusion: 所建立的严格框架填补了自旋玻色子系统近似对角化缺乏数学依据的空白，并开辟了将流式方法（flow-based algorithm）推广到系统性高阶对角化的可行路径，其思路与多尺度分析和迭代Lie–Schwinger块对角化方法在概念上是相通的。

Abstract: Spin-boson models are paradigmatic examples of open quantum systems and serve as the theoretical paradigm for current quantum computers based on single-ion trap technology. Despite their ubiquity, a complete spectral diagonalization of these models remains an open problem, except in highly singular regimes. This paper establishes a rigorous framework for the approximate diagonalization of generalized spin-boson systems. Our approach is inspired by the Brockett-Wegner double-commutator flow, which is a non-linear differential equation governing the evolution of (here unbounded) operators. Unlike relatively recent applications of this flow to quadratic Hamiltonians in quantum field theory, it does not close in the spin-boson context. We overcome this fundamental obstruction by performing a detailed analysis of the resulting non-closed algebraic structure, allowing us to explicitly bound the higher-order error term with respect to the spin-boson coupling strength. Consequently, this work provides the first mathematically rigorous justification for several heuristic diagonalization techniques widely employed in the theoretical physics literature for small-coupling regimes, in the simplest non-trivial cases. More broadly, our framework renders a flow-based algorithm feasible for systematic higher-order diagonalization and self-energy renormalization. This strategy is conceptually akin to multi-scale analysis, or, much more recently, to the iterative, local Lie-Schwinger block-diagonalization method by Fröhlich and Pizzo.

</details>

*Xiuqing Duan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于有符号直线上的二分定理（dichotomy）：对于限制在直线上的有限逆幂势（inverse-power potential）带任意实系数时，临界点数量的精确上界为至多2m−1个（m为投影分类数），由此证明了Gabrielov–Novikov–Shapiro有符号直线猜想，并将Edelsbrunner–Fillmore–Oliveira的猜想3从p≥1推广到所有p>0。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gabrielov–Novikov–Shapiro提出了有符号直线（signed line）猜想，该猜想与临界点（critical points）计数问题相关。此前该猜想仅在特定范围内得到验证，本文希望在其完整范围内证明该猜想，并进一步推广Edelsbrunner–Fillmore–Oliveiro关于正电荷情形的猜想3到更一般的p>0范围。此外，还希望说明临界点数量的上界是紧的，即存在达到该上界的显式构型。

Method: 将有限逆幂势限制到一条直线上，按投影中心和平方高度合并同类项，分类后设剩余类别数为m。采用二分法框架：若m=0则讨论完全消去（exact cancellation）的条件；若m≥1则建立临界点数量的上界。核心结构输入是一个射影配对Haar定理（projective paired Haar theorem）：对有限β>1，2m维空间∑L_j/Q_j^β（其中Q_j为两两不成比例的半定二元二次型，L_j为任意实线性分子）至多有2m−1个射影零点（projective zeros）计重数。通过替换p=2α将结论推广到Edelsbrunner–Fillmore–Oliveira的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文完整证明了Gabrielov–Novikov–Shapiro有符号直线猜想，其适用范围覆盖了作者原始范围并扩展至所有α>0。同时通过统一的代换方法将Edelsbrunner–Fillmore–Oliveira猜想3推广至所有p>0，并构造了达到最佳临界点数量上界的显式构型，表明所得上界是紧的。

Abstract: Let \(α>0\) and restrict a finite inverse-power potential with arbitrary real coefficients to a line. Combine terms having the same projected centre and squared height \((a,b^2)\), delete classes whose coefficient sum is zero, and let \(m\) be the number of remaining classes. We prove the following dichotomy. If \(m=0\), exact cancellation occurs if and only if every class sum vanishes, and every ordinary point of the original domain is critical. If \(m\geq1\), there are at most \(2m-1\) critical points: when all effective heights are positive the zeros are counted with analytic multiplicity, while in the presence of effective sources on the line the assertion is one global distinct-point bound. This proves the signed line conjecture of Gabrielov--Novikov--Shapiro, valid throughout their range and in fact for every \(α>0\). The structural input is a projective paired Haar theorem: for finite \(β>1\), the full \(2m\)-dimensional space \(\sum L_j/Q_j^β\), with pairwise nonproportional positive-definite binary quadratics and arbitrary real linear numerators, has at most \(2m-1\) projective zeros counted with multiplicity. For positive charges, the substitution \(p=2α\) proves Conjecture~3 of Edelsbrunner--Fillmore--Oliveira throughout its stated range \(p\geq1\) and extends the same conclusion to every \(p>0\). For every \(n\), an explicit positive \(n\)-charge configuration attains \(2n-1\) simple critical points.

</details>

### [88] [Deterministic control over launching efficiency of higher-order hyperbolic phonon polaritons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27733)

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文报道了在单晶α-MoO₃薄板中，通过使用亚表面散射体（subsurface scatterers）相较于传统表面散射体，将高阶双曲极化激元（higher-order hyperbolic polaritons，HO-HPhPs）的激发效率提升了10倍，并通过全波数值模拟和传递矩阵方法揭示了空间重叠对激发效率的作用机制。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 实验上采用亚表面散射体替代传统表面散射（如薄片边缘或金发射器）来激发HO-HPhPs；利用全波数值模拟（full-wave numerical simulations）研究HO-HPhP模态分布与散射体位置之间的空间重叠对激发效率的影响；并基于传递矩阵方法（transfer matrix method）开发了一个通用流程，用于选择性设计模态化的HO-HPhP激发。

Result: 实验观测到亚表面散射体可将HO-HPhPs的激发效率提升10倍；数值模拟证实了空间重叠是决定激发效率的关键因素；所开发的传递矩阵方法实现了HO-HPhP模态激发的选择性设计。

Conclusion: 该工作通过亚表面散射策略显著克服了高阶双曲极化激元的激发效率瓶颈，并提供了可扩展的设计方法，推动了双曲极化激元复用（HPhP multiplexing）在片上（on-chip）应用中的能力提升。

Abstract: Hyperbolic materials, which exhibit an extreme form of birefringence enabling the volume confinement and frequency-dependent propagation of deeply sub-diffractional optical modes, offer the opportunity for extreme confinement via the stimulation polaritonic modes, with substantially higher confinement obtained through the efficient excitation of of the higher-order (shorter wavelength) hyperbolic polaritonic modes, which they can support. However, while these higher-order hyperbolic polaritons (HO-HPhPs) form high-momentum ray-like propagation within the bulk, efficient excitation of these modes, especially in contrast to the long-wavelength lower-momentum surface polariton propagating modes, has remained a challenge. Critically, the large momentum mismatch between these modes and free-space light, alongside the spatial mismatch between the sub-diffractional scatterer and the distinct modal distribution of HO-HPhPs, lead to a suppressed launching efficiency of these higher-order modes, limiting their use in nanophotonic applications. Here, we report the experimental observation of a 10-fold enhancement in the excitation efficiency of HO-HPhPs through the use of subsurface scatterers over traditional surface scattering (e.g. a flake edge or gold launcher) within single-crystalline α-MoO3 slabs. We employ full-wave numerical simulations to investigate the role of the spatial overlap between HO-HPhP modal distributions and the scatterer placement upon excitation efficiency. Furthermore, we develop a generalized process using transfer matrix method to selectively design modal HO-HPhP excitation, which advances the capabilities of HPhP multiplexing for on-chip applications.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的光谱测量范式：利用单个二维钙钛矿光电探测器的非线性光电动力学物理编码入射光场，通过机器学习进行逆光谱重建，无需传统色散光学元件或探测器阵列，即可实现紧凑型光谱系统。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 研究确立了非线性材料和界面动力学作为光谱测量计算资源的可行性，指向材料、界面和推理架构联合设计的硬件-算法协同设计（hardware-algorithm co-design）路径，可在无需色散光学或探测器阵列的情况下实现紧凑光谱系统。

Abstract: Spectroscopy conventionally separates optical frequencies before detection, imposing persistent constraints on footprint, complexity and scalability. Here we establish an alternative paradigm in which the nonlinear optoelectronic dynamics of a single two-dimensional perovskite photodetector physically encode the incident optical field and machine learning performs the inverse spectral reconstruction. Using a planar fluorinated phenethylammonium lead iodide (F-PEAI) photodetector, we exploit wavelength- and irradiance-dependent current-voltage signatures arising from the coupled effects of photocarrier generation, trapping, interfacial transport and field-dependent carrier dynamics. A compact variational encoder-decoder preserves the functional and history-dependent structure of these responses by independently projecting forward and reverse voltage sweeps onto a truncated Legendre-polynomial basis before mapping them through a probabilistic latent representation to continuous spectral parameters. Trained on fewer than 400 experimental voltage sweeps, the model generalises to excitation wavelengths excluded from training, reconstructing wavelength with $R^2=0.958$ and a mean absolute error of 8.1 nm, while recovering log-normalised irradiance with $R^2=0.987$. Voltage-resolved analysis further reveals that wavelength and irradiance are encoded differently across the nonlinear device response, with distinct bias regions carrying complementary optical information. These results establish nonlinear material and interface dynamics as a computational resource for spectroscopy and point towards hardware-algorithm co-design in which materials, interfaces and inference architectures are engineered jointly to maximise information content, enabling compact spectroscopic systems without dispersive optics or detector arrays.

</details>


### [90] [Self-partitioned Interfacial Time Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28126)
*Joseph Huang,Yi Yang*

TL;DR: 本文通过引入Rabi-Hatano-Nelson模型，在非厄密多体系统中发现了自分区界面时间晶体（SPITC）相，其中均匀系统自发产生内部可调界面，并在该界面上同时自发破缺时间平移对称性，建立了一种通向时空序的非厄密多体系统新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡多体系统可在时间维度自发破缺对称性（如时间晶体）或在空间维度自发破缺对称性（如自组织畴与界面），但这两类对称破缺能否交织在一起——即仅有涌现界面承载时间晶体序——此前尚属未知。

Method: 引入Rabi-Hatano-Nelson模型以研究非互易性（nonreciprocity）与开放边界条件下界面上时间晶体的形成；绘制周期与开放边界的完整相图；在弱耦合极限下解析推导相边界、SPITC频率与标度指数。

Result: 识别出真空相和Dicke类超辐射相（含静态或主动序）；SPITC频率与自旋-光子耦合强度呈二次标度关系；SPITC界面位置随非互易程度变化具有临界指数-1，区别于静态界面的-1/2。

Conclusion: SPITC完全由非互易性和开放边界条件内在诱发，无需外部泵浦或长程相互作用，为非厄密多体系统中时空序的构造提供了新途径。

Abstract: Nonequilibrium many-body systems can spontaneously break symmetry in time, as in time crystals, or in space, through self-organized domains and interfaces. Whether these two forms of symmetry breaking can intertwine so that an emergent interface alone hosts time-crystalline order remains unknown. In this work, by introducing the Rabi-Hatano-Nelson model, we unveil the existence and mechanism of a self-partitioned interfacial time crystal (SPITC), where a homogeneous system generates its own internal and tunable interface, at which the time-translation symmetry is also spontaneously broken. Such a SPITC phase is intrinsically induced by nonreciprocity and open boundary conditions, without external pumping or long-range interaction. The periodic and open boundary phase diagrams of the system are both mapped out; vacuum and Dicke-like superradiance with static or active orders are identified, with analytical phase boundaries in the weak coupling limit. The frequency of the SPITC is found to scale quadratically with the spin-photon coupling strength, as we derive analytically for the slow dynamics of the spins. The position of the SPITC boundary scales with a critical exponent of $-1$ as a function of the degree of nonreciprocity, in stark contrast to $-1/2$ for an otherwise stationary boundary. Our construction of SPITC establishes a route to spatiotemporal order in non-Hermitian many-body systems.

</details>


### [91] [Three-dimensional optical vortices in nonlinear Bessel waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28167)
*Carlos F. Sánchez,Humberto Michinel,Boris Malomed,Ángel Paredes,José R. Salgueiro*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文研究了贝塞尔结构非线性梯度折射率波导中携带轨道角动量（涡旋）的光子弹（light bullet）的形成、稳定性和传播动力学，发现平方贝塞尔折射率剖面能够有效稳定这类波，并揭示了多种解族的共存关系及其在实验可及参数范围内的传播特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 携带涡旋的光子弹在均匀三维介质中容易发生塌缩（collapse），如何实现其稳定化是亟待解决的关键问题。

Method: 在垂直于传播方向的平面内引入平方贝塞尔（squared-Bessel）折射率剖面，使其作为导波或反导波势来稳定涡旋光子弹；通过求解非线性方程获得不同解族，并分析幂-传播常数（power-vs.-propagation-constant）关系的多样性。

Result: 获得了多种解族，揭示了反映多解族共存特征的多种幂-传播常数关系图；在广泛实验可及参数范围内系统分析了传播动力学。

Conclusion: 工作增进了对多维非线性光局域化（nonlinear light localization）的理解，并为在先进光子学应用中使用结构化光提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: We explore the formation, stability, and propagation dynamics of light bullets carrying orbital angular momentum (vorticity) in Bessel-structured nonlinear graded-index waveguides. While these waves are prone to the collapse in uniform three-dimensional media, we demonstrate that their stabilization is provided by a squared-Bessel index profile in the plane orthogonal to the propagation direction, which may play the role of the waveguiding or antiwaveguiding potential. Different solution families are obtained, revealing a variety of power-vs.-propagation-constant diagrams, characterizing the coexistence of multiple families. We analyze the propagation dynamics in a wide experimentally accessible parameter range. Our results contribute to the understanding of nonlinear light localization in multidimensional settings and suggest new possibilities for using structured light in advanced photonic applications.

</details>

### [92] [A microwave SQUID multiplexing concept for macro-cryogenic calorimeter arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27556)
*N. Ferreiro Iachellini,P. Szypryt,L. Canonica,W. B. Doriese,M. Durkin,A. Giachero,J. A. B. Mates,A. Nucciotti,L. Pattavina,S. Quitadamo,C. Shiu,J. N. Ullom,M. R. Vissers*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文提出将微波 SQUID 复用读出（microwave SQUID multiplexing, μMUX）应用于大规模低温量热器（massive cryogenic calorimeters），用于稀有事件搜索中的中微子-核相干散射（CEνNS）和暗物质探测，并通过噪声建模表明在复用因子高达1000时读出对基线分辨率的恶化仍小于2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单通道直流SQUID读出（dc-SQUID readout）每个探测器需要一套放大链和多根导线，限制了稀有事件搜索中大面积探测器阵列仅能扩展到几十个通道。为克服这一可扩展性瓶颈，需要更高效的复用读出方案。

Method: 基于大规模TES量热器已有的热力学与噪声模型，推导包含HEMT、二能级系统（two-level system）和SQUID贡献的复用读出噪声模型，并分析其随复用因子（multiplexing factor）N_mux的标度关系。将该框架应用于BGO、蓝宝石（sapphire）和TeO2吸收体的量热器中，并设计了一个单塔（single-tower）结构。

Result: 慢热信号每通道仅需约千赫兹采样，因此限制因素并非带宽而是输入线圈灵敏度需达(0.1–1) μA/Φ0，超出当前μMUX器件10–30倍，匹配探测器电流噪声(9–10) pA/√Hz。在总通量噪声≈1.2 μΦ0/√Hz条件下，复用因子N_mux最高至1000时读出对基线分辨率的恶化小于2%。单塔设计包含52块100 g的BGO晶体和一个HEMT放大器。

Conclusion: μMUX读出方案可有效扩展到大规模低温量热器阵列，为CEνNS和暗物质搜索提供公斤级探测器阵列的可行路径。单塔作为多塔阵列的基本单元，具备进一步扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Massive cryogenic calorimeters read out by transition-edge sensors (TES) can reach eV-scale baseline resolution, but the single-channel dc-SQUID readout conventionally used in rare-event searches, with one amplifier chain and several wires per detector, limits practical arrays to a few tens of channels. We propose to apply microwave SQUID multiplexing ($μ$MUX), developed for fast X-ray and neutrino-mass microcalorimeters, to massive BGO (Bi$_4$Ge$_3$O$_{12}$) calorimeters operated at $\simeq 20$~mK; sapphire and TeO$_2$ absorbers are covered by the same framework. Using the established thermal and noise model for massive TES calorimeters, we derive a noise model for the multiplexed readout, including the HEMT, two-level-system and SQUID contributions, and its scaling with the multiplexing factor $N_{\rm mux}$. Since the slow thermal signals require only about a kHz of sampling per channel, the limiting requirement is not bandwidth but an input-coil sensitivity of $( 0.1$ to $1)\,μ$A$/Φ_0$, a factor of $10$ to $30$ beyond current $μ$MUX devices, matched to the detector current noise of ($9$ to $10$)~pA$/\sqrt{\rm Hz}$, which is independent of the absorber mass. With a total flux noise of $\simeq$$1.2\,μΦ_0/\sqrt{\rm Hz}$, the readout degrades the baseline resolution by less than $2\%$ up to $N_{\rm mux}=1000$, negligible compared with the TES-limited resolution. These results are implemented in a single-tower design: 52 BGO crystals of 100~g each and one HEMT amplifier. The tower is the unit of a multi-tower array of several kilograms aimed at CE$ν$NS and dark matter searches.

</details>


### [93] [Migration of Belle II TOP Feature Extraction from the Zynq Processing System to PCIe40 Readout PCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27814)
*Harsh Purwar,Shahab Kohani,Vasily Shebalin,Martin Bessner,Matt Andrew,Thomas Browder,Tommy Lam,Kurtis Nishimura,Sourav Patra,Vladimir Savinov,Marko Staric,Gary Varner,Satoru Yamada,Keisuke Yoshihara*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文描述了Belle II实验中TOP探测器的一项关键架构升级：将波形特征提取任务从探测器上的Zynq片上系统（SoC）处理系统（PS）迁移到探测器外的PCIe40读出PC（ROPC）上，从而消除了由单粒子翻转（SEU）引起的PS死锁对数据采集路径的干扰。迁移后TOP死时间从约1%降至接近零，并在L1触发率30 kHz和MCP-PMT率约5 MHz/PMT条件下实现了稳定运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Belle II的TOP探测器虽在初期运行良好，但随着亮度和束流本底增加，辐射暴露的Zynq SoC中单粒子翻转（SEU）发生频率上升，导致嵌入式处理系统（PS）死锁并中断数据采集，成为制约高亮度条件下稳定运行的关键瓶颈。

Method: 将原本由探测器上Zynq PS执行的波形特征提取任务迁移至探测器外的PCIe40读出PC（ROPC）上执行，新架构使事件数据路径绕过PS，特征提取在探测器辐射环境之外完成。

Result: 该迁移消除了SEU引起的PS死锁对事件数据路径的干扰，经最终固件补丁后，典型TOP死时间从约1%降至接近零；在L1触发率30 kHz和MCP-PMT击中率约5 MHz/PMT的条件下实现了稳定运行。

Conclusion: 新的迁移架构保障了TOP前端电子学和Belle II数据采集系统在更高亮度与更高本底条件下具有可靠运行能力，同时保持了带电粒子鉴别（PID）性能，本文详细阐述了该架构的部署、验证及运行效果。

Abstract: The Belle II Time-of-Propagation (TOP) detector is a key system for charged-particle identification (PID). Although TOP operated successfully during initial Belle II data taking, increasing luminosity and beam-induced background led to more frequent single-event upsets (SEUs) in the radiation-exposed Zynq systems-on-chip (SoCs). Resulting lockups of the embedded processing systems (PSs) interrupted data acquisition. To mitigate this limitation, waveform feature extraction, a critical task of the on-detector Zynq PS, was migrated to the off-detector PCIe40 readout PCs (ROPCs). The new architecture bypasses the PS in the event data path and performs feature extraction outside the detector radiation environment. This change removed SEU-induced PS lockups from the event data path and enabled stable operation of the TOP front-end electronics and the Belle II data-acquisition system under increased luminosity and background conditions. The typical TOP deadtime decreased from about 1% to a level consistent with zero after the final firmware patch, and stable operation was demonstrated at a Level-1 (L1) trigger rate of 30 kHz with a microchannel-plate photomultiplier-tube (MCP-PMT) hit rate of approximately 5 MHz per PMT. This paper describes the migrated architecture, its deployment and validation, and its operational and PID performance.

</details>


### [94] [Full-field fluorescence computed tomography (F3CT) using a calibrated virtual cone-beam pinhole geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28275)
*Thomas Zillhardt,Yunhui Chen,Alexander Rack,Matthew Veale,Matt Wilson,Philip J. Withers,Nicola Viganò*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: F3CT是一种基于同步辐射的超光谱全场荧光计算机断层成像（hyperspectral full-field fluorescence computed tomography）技术，通过针孔孔径与能量分辨二维探测器相结合，避免了光栅扫描，实现全场照明下的三维元素成像。在生物和地质样品上展示该方法，能分辨斑马鱼组织中的银染分布和岩心中的高能荧光特征，并与同几何构型下获取的相位衬度断层图像（phase-contrast tomograms）实现配准的联立结构成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维X射线荧光（XRF）成像通常依赖光栅扫描（raster scanning），成像速度受到逐点扫描的限制，无法满足高通量三维元素映射的需求。作者旨在提出一种无需光栅扫描、在全场照明条件下实现三维元素分布成像的方法，并同时建立结构-化学联合层析成像（correlated structural and chemical tomography）的基础。

Method: 该方法将针孔孔径（pinhole aperture）与能量分辨二维探测器（energy-resolving 2D detector）相结合，替代传统逐点扫描机制。采用虚拟锥束模型（virtual cone-beam model）和两阶段标定-重建工作流（two-stage calibration-reconstruction workflow），在全场照明下实现三维元素映射。随后在同一实验几何构型下顺序采集相位衬度层析图像，以提供共配准（co-registered）的结构背景信息。

Result: 在生物和地质样品上验证了该方法：能够分辨斑马鱼组织中银染（silver-stain）的空间分布，以及岩心中高能荧光特征（high-energy fluorescence signatures）。通过顺序采集的相位衬度层析图像，获得了与元素分布共配准的结构上下文信息。

Conclusion: F3CT为三维XRF成像提供了一条高通量路径，建立了结构成像与化学成像联合断层成像的框架基础，并有望在未来的原位（in-situ）和操作态（operando）实验中得到应用。

Abstract: We present F3CT, a synchrotron-based hyperspectral full-field fluorescence computed tomography technique that avoids raster scanning by combining a pinhole aperture with an energy-resolving 2D detector. A virtual cone-beam model and two-stage calibration-reconstruction workflow enable 3D elemental mapping under full-field illumination. The method is demonstrated on biological and geological specimens, resolving silver-stain distributions in zebrafish tissue and high-energy fluorescence signatures in rock cores. Phase-contrast tomograms acquired sequentially under the same experimental geometry provide co-registered structural context. F3CT provides a high-throughput route to 3D XRF imaging and establishes a foundation for correlated structural and chemical tomography, with potential for future in-situ and operando implementations.

</details>

### [95] [Criticality and universality in network dismantling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27613)
*Lorenzo Cirigliano,Claudio Castellano,Minsuk Kim,Filippo Radicchi,Hanlin Sun*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应有偏渗流（adaptive biased percolation）过程用于网络拆解（network dismantling），并首次在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下研究其物理机制，发现该过程表现出普适相变（universal phase transition），即巨型连通分量（giant connected component）与最大2-核（2-core）同时突然消失，且此普适性在不同度分布网络中成立，暗示可发展不依赖拓扑的理论来解释网络拆解的临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络拆解问题在过去十年中已被广泛研究，但绝大多数工作聚焦于为大型有限网络设计高效算法，而网络拆解过程本身的物理机制——即节点或边移除如何影响网络结构连通性——在热力学极限下仍基本未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 引入一种自适应的有偏渗流过程，能够以最优方式拆解网络；通过对合成网络模型的系统分析，考察该渗流过程中的连通性变化，并结合真实网络进行模拟验证。

Result: 该渗流过程表现出普适相变，其特征为巨型连通分量与最大2-核的突然且同步消失，该现象在度分布截然不同的网络中均成立；真实网络模拟进一步支持这一普适性，表明网络拆解的物理对广泛的拓扑属性不敏感。

Conclusion: 研究结果提示，可能存在一种不依赖拓扑（topology-agnostic）的理论来解释网络拆解的临界行为。

Abstract: Identifying the smallest set of elements whose removal dismantle a complex network, known as the network dismantling problem, is a fundamental task with many practical applications. Whereas network dismantling has been extensively studied over the past decade, most work has focused on developing efficient algorithms for large but finite networks. By contrast, the physics of the network dismantling process, namely how the network structural connectivity is affected by the removal of nodes or edges, remains largely unexplored in the thermodynamic limit. Here, we shed light on this understudied aspect of network dismantling by introducing an adaptive biased percolation process able to optimally dismantle a network. Through a systematic analysis of synthetic network models, we find that the proposed percolation process displays a universal phase transition, characterized by the abrupt and simultaneous disappearance of both the giant connected component and the largest 2-core, across networks with markedly different degree distributions. Simulations on real networks further support this universality, indicating that the physics of network dismantling is insensitive to a broad range of topological properties. Together, these results suggest that a topology-agnostic theory could be developed to explain the critical behavior of network dismantling.

</details>

### [96] [Mode-Specific Dynamics of CO2 Hydrogenation on Copper: The Hidden Role of Molecular Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27850)
*Junfan Xia,Zhikai Jiang,Yaolong Zhang,Bo Peng,Hua Guo,Bin Jiang*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本研究基于全维第一性原理神经网络势能面，对Cu(111)表面CO₂催化加氢制甲酸反应进行了精确的动力学预测，结果表明弯曲振动激发增强反应活性，但转动激发才是喷口温度升高导致反应性增加的主导因素。这一模式特异的机理洞察修正了传统的Eley-Rideal机制解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜表面CO₂催化加氢制甲酸是CO₂利用的关键基元步骤。以往实验和理论研究表明该反应遵循Eley-Rideal机制，且受弯曲振动激发促进，但缺乏直接的状态分辨证据。

Method: 基于全维第一性原理神经网络势能面（full-dimensional first-principles neural network potential energy surface），对Cu(111)表面CO₂加氢反应进行准确的动力学模拟计算。

Result: 计算近定量地重现了实验测量的反应概率及其随喷口温度和入射能量变化的依赖关系。状态分辨结果表明，弯曲振动激发虽增强反应性，但无法单独解释喷口温度升高带来的反应性增加；转动激发起主导作用，主要原因归因于CO₂接近过渡态时分子极取向各向异性的显著变化。

Conclusion: 该模式特异的机理洞察修正了传统解释，为催化剂上CO₂加氢的精确控制奠定了基础。

Abstract: Catalytic hydrogenation of CO2 to formate on copper is a key elementary step for CO2 utilization. Previous experimental and theoretical studies suggested an Eley-Rideal mechanism for this reaction, promoted by bending vibrational excitation, yet direct state resolved evidence remains lacking. Here, we present accurate dynamical predictions for CO2 hydrogenation on Cu(111) based on a full-dimensional first-principles neural network potential energy surface. Our calculations near-quantitatively reproduce the measured reaction probabilities, including their nozzle-temperature and incidence-energy dependence. Our state-resolved results indicate that while vibrational excitation of the bending mode enhances reactivity, it alone cannot account for the observed reactivity increase with nozzle temperature. Instead, rotational excitation plays a dominant role, mainly attributable to the significant change in anisotropy of the molecular polar orientation as CO2 accesses the transition state. This mode-specific mechanistic insight revises the conventional interpretation and lays the foundation for the precise control of CO2 hydrogenation on catalysts.

</details>


### [97] [Projected q-Shells for Nested Gausslet Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28005)
*Steven R. White*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出投影q壳层（projected q-shells, PQS）方法，一种对White和Lindsey的嵌套笛卡尔高斯小基组（nested Cartesian gausslets）的改进方案，在保持双指标（two-index）相互作用优势的同时，近似恢复标准嵌套构造中因壳层分割而丢失的两个多项式完备阶（polynomial completeness order），从而在相近基组尺寸下获得更小的电子结构和相互作用误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子结构（electronic-structure）计算需要在有限表示中同时刻画电子波函数及其库仑相互作用（Coulomb interaction）。Gausslet基组是少数能实现双指标相互作用的小型基组方法，但如何进一步减少所需基函数数量同时维持高精度是持续的挑战，本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 引入PQS方法，对标准嵌套构造进行改进。标准构造将connecting shell分割为互不相交的面（faces）、边（edges）和角（corners），使多项式完备阶从q降至q-2；PQS通过近似恢复这两个丢失的阶来提升完备性。作者证明了在理想无畸变母壳（ideally undistorted parent shell）情形下，PQS基组的张成空间等价于重叠块（overlapping patches）的过完备基，并分析了坐标畸变（coordinate distortions）对该等价性的影响。同时考察了PQS在矩性质（moment properties）上的损失与完备阶提升之间的权衡。

Result: 在相近基组尺寸下，PQS在H2+、H2和C2等小分子上比标准嵌套方法产生更小的单电子误差和相互作用误差；改进效果在更紧凑、q值更小的基组中最为显著。尽管PQS的矩性质在无畸变情形下也会变得不精确，但完备阶的提升足以弥补这一损失。

Conclusion: PQS通过近似恢复标准嵌套构造中丢失的两个多项式完备阶，在保持双指标相互作用框架的同时提升了gausslet基组在紧凑情形的精度，为电子结构计算中的高精度小型基组设计提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Electronic-structure calculations require a finite representation of both the electronic wave functions and their Coulomb interaction. Conventional compact basis sets lead to a four-index interaction. Gausslet basis sets are one of a very small number of approaches that allow a much smaller two-index interaction, similar to a real-space grid, but with far fewer points. The continuing challenge in their development is reducing further the number of functions needed while maintaining high accuracy. Here we introduce projected q-shells (PQS), an improvement to the nested Cartesian gausslets of White and Lindsey. Their standard nesting construction divides a joining shell into disjoint faces, edges, and corners, reducing its polynomial completeness from order q to order q-2. PQS approximately restores the two lost orders. In terms of completeness, we prove that for an ideal, undistorted parent, the span of the PQS basis is equivalent to an overcomplete basis of overlapping patches. Coordinate distortions, which are always used in practice, harm this equivalence only slightly. The moment properties that allow two-index interactions also become inexact, even without distortion, but we show that the improvement in order more than makes up for this loss. For similar basis sizes, PQS gives smaller one-electron and interaction errors on small molecules, such as H2+, H2, and C2, than standard nesting. The improvement is largest for more compact bases with smaller q.

</details>

Zhelezov,S. Z. Zheng,X. Z. Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次观测到Cabibbo压低衰变过程Ξ_c^0→pK^-，利用LHCb实验在13 TeV质心能量下收集的5.4 fb^-1 pp碰撞数据，测得分支比为(4.5±0.5±0.2±0.9)×10^-5，并确定该衰变的衰变不对称参数α=0.32±0.15±0.01。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对Cabibbo压低（Cabibbo-suppressed）过程的探测有助于检验标准模型（Standard Model）的味物理预言，同时衰变不对称参数α的测量可为重子衰变中的CP破坏研究提供参考基准。

Method: 利用LHCb探测器在质心能量13 TeV下采集的pp碰撞数据（积分亮度5.4 fb^-1），通过完整衰变链Ξ_b^-→Ξ_c^0(→pK^-)π^-进行信号重建；分支比的确定以Ξ_b^-→Ξ_c^0(→pK^-K^-π^+)π^-为归一化通道。

Result: 首次观测到Ξ_c^0→pK^-衰变，其分支比测量为(4.5±0.5±0.2±0.9)×10^-5（分别为统计、系统及归一化通道分支比不确定度）；衰变不对称参数测得为α_{Ξ_c^0}=0.32±0.15±0.01。

Conclusion: Ξ_c^0→pK^-衰变的存在得到实验确认，所测分支比与不对称参数为后续CP破坏研究和重子衰变理论模型的检验提供了新的实验数据。

Abstract: A search for the Cabibbo-suppressed decay $Ξ_c^0 \to pK^-$ is performed using $pp$ collision data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $5.4\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$, collected by the LHCb experiment at a centre-of-mass energy of $13\,\mathrm{TeV}$. The decay is observed for the first time and its branching fraction measured to be $(4.5\pm0.5\pm0.2\pm0.9)\times10^{-5}$, where the uncertainties are statistical, systematic and from the branching fraction of the normalisation channel $Ξ_b^- \to Ξ_c^0 (\to p K^- K^- π^+) π^-$. Using the decay chain $Ξ_b^- \to Ξ_c^0(\to pK^-)π^-$, the decay asymmetry parameter of the $Ξ_c^0 \to pK^-$ decay is determined to be $α_{Ξ_c^0}=0.32\pm0.15\pm0.01$.

</details>


### [99] [Next generation tracking and vertexing detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28089)
*Armin Ilg*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了未来多种对撞机类型（如正负电子、电子-强子、缪子-缪子及强子对撞机）在顶点探测与径迹重建方面的需求与挑战，识别出空间精度提升、低物质量设计和粒子鉴别能力集成等共性趋势，并指出满足这些需求需要传感器、前端电子学、读出、冷却、供电与机械结构的综合优化与大量研发投入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确高效的径迹与顶点重建（track and vertex reconstruction）是充分挖掘对撞机实验物理潜能的关键，而未来对撞机类型多样、碰撞环境各异，其跟踪系统需求各不相同，因此有必要系统性地梳理各类未来对撞机对顶点探测与径迹跟踪系统的要求与挑战。

Method: 本文采用综述性分析方法，基于对撞机类型（e^+e^-、e^-强子、μ^+μ^-和强子对撞机）及其碰撞环境、测量精度要求和束流结构来分类讨论未来跟踪系统的需求，并对比不同技术方案（如单片式有源像素传感器、气体探测器、硅与闪烁光纤跟踪器）的适用特性。

Result: 研究发现，各类型对撞机在跟踪系统需求上呈现若干共性趋势：需要更高精度的空间测量、低物质量设计，部分场景需集成粒子鉴别能力。单片式有源像素传感器（monolithic active pixel sensors）是正负电子与电子-强子对撞机顶点探测器的主流选择；气体探测器可提供每径迹多次测量且物质量极低，而硅与闪烁光纤跟踪器提供更少但更精密的测量并能承受更高击中率。10 TeV部分子质心系对撞机需要全径迹系统的精密时间测量以抑制束流背景或缓解堆积效应；未来强子对撞机则要求超出现有传感器技术的抗辐射能力。

Conclusion: 满足未来对撞机顶点探测与径迹跟踪需求需要重大研发投入，并需对传感器、前端电子学、读出电路、冷却、供电和机械结构进行整体集成优化，而非仅聚焦单一技术环节。

Abstract: Precise and efficient track and vertex reconstruction is essential to exploit the physics potential of collider experiments. The requirements of future tracking systems are determined by the collider type and its collision environment, required measurement precision, and beam structure. This contribution reviews the requirements and challenges of vertexing and tracking detectors at future $e^+e^-$, $e^-$-hadron, $μ^+μ^-$, and hadron colliders. Several common trends emerge across the different collider types. Tracking systems require increasingly precise spatial measurements, low-material designs, and, in some cases, integrated particle identification capabilities. Monolithic active pixel sensors are currently the leading option for vertex detectors at $e^+e^-$ and $e^-$-hadron colliders. Tracker concepts range from gaseous detectors, which provide many measurements per track and can achieve very low material budgets, to silicon and scintillating-fibre trackers, which provide fewer but more precise measurements and can tolerate higher hit rates. At 10 TeV parton centre-of-mass colliders, precision timing throughout the tracking system is needed for beam-background rejection or pile-up mitigation. For future hadron colliders specifically, radiation tolerance beyond that demonstrated by existing sensor technologies is required. Ultimately, meeting the vertexing and tracking requirements of future colliders will require substantial R&D and the integrated optimisation of sensors, front-end electronics, readout, cooling, powering, and mechanics.

</details>


### [100] [Characterizing Single-Signal Events from Atmospheric-Neutrino Neutral-Current Interactions in Large Liquid Scintillator Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28095)
*Zhenning Qu,Jie Cheng,Wan-lei Guo,Gaosong Li,Yu-Feng Li,Liang-jian Wen*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型液体闪烁体探测器（large liquid scintillator detector）中大气中微子（atmospheric neutrino）中性流相互作用产生的单事件信号（singles），系统分析了主要模型依赖来源，并给出了预测事件率和能谱，揭示了现有及未来探测器对研究中微子-核相互作用和背景估计的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型液体闪烁体探测器中大气中微子中性流相互作用为研究单事件信号提供了新机会，而此类信号对中微子-核相互作用模型具有敏感性，系统的模型依赖性分析尚属空白。

Method: 对大气中微子单事件的模型依赖性进行系统研究，覆盖初级中微子-核相互作用、残余核退激（de-excitation）以及闪烁体中的二次相互作用，并分别给出中性流和带电流（charged-current）贡献的事件率与瞬发能谱，同时估算亚100 MeV 大气中微子在自由质子上的弹性散射低能贡献。

Result: 模型依赖性主要来源于初级中微子-核相互作用，尤其体现在碳上的中性流过程；退激对单事件选择至关重要但引起的谱变化较小；二次相互作用整体为次主导效应。

Conclusion: 研究结果展示了当前及未来大型液体闪烁体探测器（如江门中微子实验，JUNO）在大气中微子单事件研究、中微子-核相互作用模型检验以及罕见事件搜索背景估计方面的物理潜力。

Abstract: Neutral-current interactions of atmospheric neutrinos in large liquid scintillator detectors offer a new opportunity to study single-signal events (hereafter singles), characterized by a prompt energy deposition on the MeV-to-GeV scale and no identified delayed signal. In this work, we systematically investigate the model dependence of atmospheric-neutrino singles due to the primary neutrino-nucleus interaction, residual-nucleus de-excitation, and secondary interactions in the scintillator. Our results show that the dominant model dependence originates from the primary neutrino-nucleus interaction, especially for neutral-current processes on carbon, whereas de-excitation is essential for the singles selection yet leads to relatively small spectral variations among realistic models. Secondary-interaction effects are also subdominant overall. We further present the predicted event rates and prompt-energy spectra for neutral-current singles, along with the charged-current contribution. Separately, we estimate the low-energy contribution from elastic scattering of sub-\SI{100}{\MeV} atmospheric neutrinos on free protons. These results highlight the physics potential of current and future large liquid scintillator detectors, such as the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory, to study atmospheric-neutrino singles, probe neutrino-nucleus interaction models, and improve background estimates for rare-event searches.

</details>


### [101] [Vertex reconstruction for a search for neutron-antineutron conversions with HIBEAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28314)
*Alexander Burgman,Sze Chun Yiu,Yamna Shaikh,David Milstead,Eily Merjy,Lucas Åstrand,Kenneth Österberg,Fredrik Oljemark,Matthias Holl,Valentina Santoro,André Nepomuceno,Joshua L. Barrow*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文针对欧洲散裂源（European Spallation Source）上HIBEAM/NNBAR实验中反中子（antineutron）在薄靶箔上湮灭产生的带电粒子径迹，研究了时间投影室（time projection chamber）中的径迹聚类与箔面顶点重建问题，对比了经典非机器学习方法与图神经网络（graph neural network）方法，发现经典重建方法与机器学习方法在顶点坐标重建上表现相当，但后者可提供有价值的事件形状信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HIBEAM/NNBAR实验旨在探测中子到反中子的转化，其关键观测量是反中子湮灭产生带电粒子的重建顶点（reconstructed vertex）。为准确测量该物理量，需要可靠且高效的径迹聚类与顶点重建算法，因此有必要系统比较不同方法在该特定几何拓扑下的性能。

Method: 研究采用多种重建算法并进行对比，包括确定性聚类（deterministic clustering）、无径迹投影（trackless projection），以及混合聚类/图神经网络链（hybrid clustering/graph-neural-network chain），后者包含径迹分类与顶点细化（vertex refinement）步骤。

Result: 对于HIBEAM时间投影室中几何结构简单的事件，经典重建方法在顶点坐标重建方面与基于机器学习的方法表现相当（on-par）。然而，定制化的机器学习方法能够提供事件形状信息（event-shape information），这可能对下游分析具有重要意义。

Conclusion: 作者认为，在HIBEAM时间投影室当前实验条件下，经典重建算法已能满足顶点重建需求；但机器学习方法额外提供的事件形状信息可作为下游分析的重要补充。

Abstract: The HIBEAM/NNBAR programme (incorporated into the FINESSE/NNBAR programme) at the European Spallation Source is proposed to search for neutrons converting to antineutrons. An important observable is the reconstructed vertex arising from charged particles produced by an antineutron annihilating on a thin target foil and which pass through a time projection chamber. This paper studies track clustering and foil-plane vertex reconstruction for this topology. Both non-machine-learning methods and graph-neural-network methods are tested and compared with each other, including deterministic clustering, trackless projection and a hybrid clustering/graph-neural-network chain with track classification and vertex refinement. We conclude that, for the geometrically simple events in the HIBEAM TPC, classical reconstruction methods perform on-par with machine-learning based methods in terms of vertex coordinate reconstruction. Custom machine-learning based methods can, however, deliver event-shape information that may be important in downstream analyses.

</details>


### [102] [Search for scalar leptoquarks produced via muon-quark scattering in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28377)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次报道了在μ子-夸克（muon-quark, μq）相互作用中搜索TeV能标标量轻夸克（scalar leptoquark, LQ）的实验结果，基于CMS探测器在LHC上收集的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度138 fb⁻¹），未观测到超出背景预期的信号，并对LQ质量与耦合参数平面设定了排除上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在质子内部量子涨落产生的带电轻子组分所诱导的轻子-夸克相互作用，进而搜索TeV能标的标量轻夸克共振产生，以探求超越标准模型的新物理信号。

Method: 利用LHC上CMS探测器记录的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度138 fb⁻¹），研究一个质子中的μ子与另一质子中的夸克发生相互作用，实现单个LQ的共振产生及其后续衰变；主要观测量为μ子-喷注系统的不变质量（invariant mass），其分布峰值对应LQ质量。

Result: 数据与背景预言一致，未观察到显著信号；对LQ产生截面与衰变分支比的乘积设定了上限。在LQ(uμ)情景区间排除了质量1.5–3.6 TeV、耦合0.2–0.6范围内的参数区域；在LQ(bμ)情景区间排除了质量高于1.8 TeV、耦合大于1的参数区域，较之前CMS搜索结果扩大了覆盖范围。

Conclusion: 本次搜索在TeV能标μq相互作用中未发现标量轻夸克存在的证据，排除了此前CMS搜索未覆盖的更大的参数空间，为限制轻夸克模型提供了新的实验约束。

Abstract: The first search for TeV-scale scalar leptoquarks (LQs) produced in muon-quark ($μ$q) interactions is presented. It is based on proton-proton collision data recorded at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV with the CMS detector at the LHC, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. Quantum fluctuations inside the proton generate charged-lepton components, enabling the study of lepton-induced processes. In particular, the interaction of a muon from one colliding proton with a quark from the other proton enables the study of resonant production of a single LQ and its subsequent decay. The final state includes one or two muons and a jet that can come from the hadronization of either a light quark (u) or a b quark. The primary observable is the invariant mass of the muon-jet system, whose distribution peaks at the LQ mass. The data are found to be in agreement with the background predictions. Upper limits are set on the product of the LQ production cross section and its decay branching fraction to the $μ$q final state. The results exclude broad regions in the parameter plane of the LQ mass and the LQ-$μ$q coupling, improving upon previous CMS searches by extending the coverage for LQs with masses between 1.5 and 3.6 TeV, and couplings between 0.2 and 0.6 for the LQ(u$μ$) scenario; and masses above 1.8 TeV, and couplings above 1 for the LQ(b$μ$) scenario.

</details>


### [103] [A High- and Variable-Dimensional Measurement of the $Z$+jets Differential Cross Section with the ATLAS Experiment and Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28449)
*Kevin Greif*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本论文将人工智能（artificial intelligence）技术应用于大型强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider, LHC）上ATLAS实验的物理结果改进，核心工作是基于AI反卷积（unfolding）算法对Z+喷注（Z+jets）产生过程的全相空间截面进行微分测量，这是LHC上首次实现此类测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC上质子-质子对撞产生的高碰撞率和大数目末态粒子使得探测器数据集规模庞大、维度极高，传统的分析技术难以充分利用所有信息；因此需要开发能够挖掘全部可用信息的新数据分析手段，以阐明已知相互作用并搜寻新物理现象。

Method: 论文采用基于人工智能的算法来改进ATLAS实验的物理分析流程，核心方法为一种AI驱动的反卷积算法，用于处理探测器效应，从而在全相空间（full phase space）内实现Z+喷注产生截面的微分测量，测量对象涵盖每个末态带电粒子的运动学分布。

Result: 研究成功完成了LHC首次基于AI反卷积算法的Z+喷注产生截面全相空间微分测量，该测量为Z+喷注产生过程提供了完整的实验表征，明确了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了AI技术在LHC高维复杂数据处理中的有效性与潜力，为未来在一系列LHC过程中开展同类全相空间测量提供了原理验证（proof-of-principle），也为进一步推动此类测量方法在更多对撞过程中的应用奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Proton-proton collisions at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) offer the opportunity to observe the interactions of fundamental particles at very high energy scales. The high collision rate and large number of final state particles produced per collision imply that the datasets produced by detectors such as the ATLAS experiment are large and high dimensional. The complexity of these datasets calls for the use of novel data analysis techniques which can exploit all of the available information to illuminate known interactions and search for new ones. This thesis presents applications of artificial intelligence (AI) techniques to improve the physics results of the ATLAS experiment. It centers on a full-phase-space measurement of the Z+jets production cross section at the LHC, where the cross section is measured differential in the kinematics of every final state charged particle through the use of an AI-based unfolding algorithm. This is the first such measurement performed at the LHC, which provides a complete experimental characterization of the Z+jets production process and a proof-of-principle for the further pursuit of such measurements on a host of LHC processes.

</details>

### [104] [Magnonic Combinatorial Memory based on a network of coupled active ring circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28457)
*Mykhaylo Balinskiy,Paulo Julio,Jeffrey Vargas,Diana Bisono Balaguer,Jacob Greenstein,Alexander Khitun*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了一种基于耦合有源环电路（active ring circuit, ARC）网络的磁子组合存储器（Magnonic Combinatorial Memory, MCM）。该存储器通过自旋波在钇铁石榴石（YIG）铁氧体薄膜中的传播实现电路间耦合，将信息位编码于网络信号传播路径中，利用自动振荡的存在与否定义存储状态。实验在室温下实现了超过30 dB的On/Off比，并通过三个耦合ARC编码了27位二进制响应模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统存储器在容量扩展方面存在局限性，本文旨在探索一种基于磁子学的新型组合存储器架构，通过将信息编码在信号传播路径中而非传统存储单元中，实现位数随耦合电路数量呈二次方增长的存储扩展能力，从而为存储器设计提供根本性优势。

Method: 作者构建了由三个通过单晶YIG薄膜中传播的自旋波相互耦合的有源环电路组成的网络。每个电路包含宽带放大器、磁子延迟线、可调频率滤波器、可调移相器和功率检测器。电路间的耦合通过在铁氧体薄膜上方放置微磁铁实现。MCM地址由移相器和频率滤波器的状态组合定义，MCM状态则由自动振荡（auto-oscillations）的存在与否定义。

Result: 实验实现了三个磁子ARC通过单晶Y3Fe2(FeO4)3（YIG）薄膜的连接，成功编码了27位二进制响应模式，对应于27种实验可达到的相移组合。系统在室温下稳定运行，On/Off比超过30 dB，展示了MCM的稳健操作特性。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明MCM方案具有稳健性和可行性，其位数随耦合电路数量二次方增长的扩展规律为存储器设计提供了根本性优势。文中同时讨论了该方法的优势与不足，为后续改进和应用提供了方向。

Abstract: Magnonic Combinatorial Memory (MCM) is a type of memory where the bits of information are encoded in the signal propagation paths in the network. In this work, we consider MCM based on the network of a coupled active ring circuit (ARC). Each circuit includes a broadband amplifier, a magnonic delay line, an adjustable frequency filter, an adjustable phase shifter, and a power detector. The coupling between the circuits is via spin waves propagating in the common delay line - ferrite film. There may or may not be auto-oscillations in the active ring circuits, depending on the combination of circuit parameters and circuit coupling. The address of MCM is defined as the combination of the states of the phase shifters and frequency filters, while the MCM state is defined as the presence/absence of the auto-oscillations. The coupling between the circuits is achieved by placing micromagnets on top of the ferrite film. The number of bits that can be encoded in the network increases quadratically with the number of coupled circuits. This scaling provides a fundamental advantage over conventional memory. We present experimental data obtained for three magnonic ARCs connected via a single-crystal yttrium iron garnet Y3Fe2(FeO4)3 (YIG) film. The data illustrate an example of encoding a 27-bit binary response pattern, corresponding to the 27 experimentally accessible phase combinations. The results demonstrate a robust operation of MCM with an On/Off ratio exceeding 30 dB at room temperature. The advantages and shortcomings of the proposed approach are discussed.

</details>

### [105] [The asymptotic structure of forward scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27815)
*Nicholas Lohr,Izak Oltman,Ethan Sussman,Yuzhou Joey Zou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究渐近欧几里得空间上微扰平面波（perturbed plane waves）在前方方向的结构，在Melrose–Zworski的微局部分析框架基础上，通过双面紧化（two-faced compactification）给出物理空间中的初等描述，并证明完全多齐性（polyhomogeneity），同时揭示主面和前脸处的两个模型问题分别对应输运方程和量子倒谐振子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扰动平面波是欧几里得空间及渐近欧几里得空间散射理论中的基本对象。Melrose–Zworski利用与相交的Lagrange子流形对相关的Lagrange分布给出了微局域描述，但这一描述在前方方向（forward direction）涉及出射球面波与入射平面波的奇异结合，较为复杂。本文旨在为渐近欧几里得情形提供更初等的物理空间渐近描述。

Method: 采用双面紧化 X↩R^d 来分别刻画两个渐近区域（主面和前脸），从而给出物理空间中的渐近描述。作者利用这一紧化结构证明完全多齐性，并将主面（'bf'）上的模型问题归结为输运方程（transport equation），将前脸（'ff'）上的模型问题归结为量子倒谐振子（quantum inverted harmonic oscillator）。

Result: 论文证明了解在前方方向上的完全多齐性（full polyhomogeneity），并确定了两个渐近边界上的渐近模型：主面对应于输运方程，前脸对应于量子倒谐振子。与Melrose–Zworski的一般渐近锥形（asymptotically conic）框架相比，此处涉及的振荡积分取到了最复杂的形式。

Conclusion: 通过在渐近欧几里得情形下建立物理空间中的初等渐近描述，本文为Melrose–Zworski的微局域理论在此特例下提供了更直接的理解途径，并以输运方程和量子倒谐振子作为两个边界处的模型问题。

Abstract: Perturbed plane waves are fundamental objects in scattering theory on Euclidean space and asymptotically Euclidean spaces. In this paper, we investigate the structure of perturbed plane waves in the $\textit{forward}$ direction, in which the outgoing spherical wave is typically singular and conjoined to the incoming plane wave. Melrose & Zworski provided a microlocal description (in the more general setting of asymptotically conic manifolds) using their notion of Lagrangian distributions associated to pairs of intersecting Legendrian submanifolds, on the way to proving that the S-matrix is an FIO. Here, we revisit the problem in the asymptotically Euclidean case, for which the oscillatory integrals used by Melrose--Zworski attain their most complicated form (relative to the more general asymptotically conic case). We seek a more elementary description in terms of physical-space asymptotics. These are specified using a two-faced compactification $X\hookleftarrow \mathbb{R}^d$, with one face for each asymptotic regime. We prove full polyhomogeneity. A transport equation arises as a model problem at the main face (`bf'). The quantum inverted harmonic oscillator arises as a model problem at the front face (`ff').

</details>

### [106] [Guide for the Hubble Tension: Mid-Time Solutions and Generalized PAge Parameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28260)
*Jing-Yi Jia,Da-Chun Qiang,Hao Wei*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文指出原始PAge参数化存在的缺陷，提出了一系列广义PAge（GPAge）参数化，使其可扩展到CMB红移范围并提高低红移精度，进而利用晚时观测与CMB数据重新审视哈勃张力问题。研究发现，与标准ΛCDM明显不同的GPAge模型可以缓解甚至解决哈勃张力，且被所有信息准则强烈偏好；出人意料的是，中期宇宙（mid-time）的新物理可能是解决哈勃张力的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈勃张力（Hubble tension）已成为过去十年宇宙学中最严峻的挑战之一。在假设所有观测数据均正确的前提下，需要修改标准ΛCDM宇宙学；然而近期文献中针对早期和晚期宇宙修改都出现了no-go论证，特别是基于PAge参数化针对晚期修改的一般性no-go论证存在缺陷，需要被纠正。

Method: 作者指出原始PAge参数化的若干缺陷，提出多种广义PAge（GPAge）参数化方案。这些新参数化能够扩展到宇宙微波背景辐射（CMB）红移范围以上（z>z_CMB），从而纳入CMB观测数据，同时在低红移区间也具有更高精度。利用这些GPAge参数化，结合晚时观测数据和CMB观测数据，重新评估哈勃张力。

Result: 研究发现，与标准ΛCDM宇宙学显著不同的GPAge模型可以有效缓解甚至解决哈勃张力；这些模型在所有信息准则（information criteria）方面都被观测数据压倒性地优先于ΛCDM。最引人注目的结果是，解决哈勃张力的关键可能在于中期宇宙的新物理。

Conclusion: 广义PAge（GPAge）参数化是研究哈勃张力的有效工具，而中期宇宙（mid-time）的新物理可能是统一解决哈勃张力的关键突破口，这暗示对标准ΛCDM模型的修改不应局限于早期或晚期宇宙。

Abstract: In cosmology, the Hubble tension has become one of the most serious challenges in the last decade. Assuming that all the observational data are right, one of the ways out is to modify the standard $Λ$CDM cosmology. In the literature, the theoretical modifications are mainly made in the early and the late/local universes. But on both sides, some no-go arguments have been proposed recently. In particular, the general no-go arguments for the late-time modifications are mainly based on the PAge parameterization. In the present work, we note that there are some flaws in the (original) PAge parameterization, and then propose various generalized PAge (GPAge) parameterizations, which can be extended to $z>z_{\rm CMB}$ and hence the cosmic microwave background (CMB) can be taken into account, while they are also more accurate at low redshifts. With these GPAge parameterizations, we revisit the Hubble tension by using not only the late-time observations but also the observational data of CMB. We find that the Hubble tension could be alleviated (or even resolved) in GPAges significantly different from the standard $Λ$CDM cosmology, while they are overwhelmingly preferred over $Λ$CDM by the observational data in terms of all the information criteria. Surprisingly, we find that the new physics in the mid-time might be the key to resolve the Hubble tension.

</details>


### [107] [Explicit gauge-invariant variables in multifield inflation beyond linear order and Hamiltonian dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28337)
*Julien Grain,Hugo Holland,Lucas Pinol*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文在哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）框架下，针对多场暴胀（multifield inflation）在二阶扰动理论中，首次显式构造了所有大尺度规范不变相空间变量（gauge-invariant phase-space variables），并证明了在平坦规范（flat gauge）和共动规范（comoving gauge）下仍可恢复标准的规范不变涨落，同时通过二阶和三阶哈密顿量的推导与文献验证了一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论耦合多个标量场是微分同胚不变的约束系统，朴素地计数微扰自由度会高估真实物理模式数，因为规范冗余和约束方程会去除非动力学模式。尽管线性涨落问题已解决，但多场暴胀中二阶扰动级别的规范不变相空间变量此前尚无显式构造，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于著名的Sasaki-Mukhanov变量，作者构建了一个有限维的二次修正基，该基在规范变换下不变；该过程对任意场数量和任意尺度均通用，但本文限于超哈勃尺度（super-Hubble scales）给出显式解。随后推导了多场暴胀的二次和三次哈密顿量（采用规范无关方式构造），再通过进入平坦规范进行规范固定以验证一致性，并提出向更高阶微扰理论推广的形式化证明思路。

Result: 作者证明了可以在超哈勃尺度下恢复通常的平坦规范涨落和共动规范涨落作为大尺度规范不变组合，从而对规范固定流程进行了稳健的一致性检验。推导出的二次和三次哈密顿量与基于拉格朗日（Lagrangian）方法的文献结果完全吻合。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在二阶扰动下可构造具有规范不变性的相空间变量，并提供了一个形式化证明其存在性的框架，以及向更高阶扰动理论推广的步骤草图。

Abstract: General relativity coupled to multiple scalar fields is a diffeomorphism-invariant constrained system. Consequently, a naive counting of the perturbative degrees of freedom unavoidably overestimates the true number of physical modes propagating in the theory, as gauge redundancies and constraint equations remove non-dynamical ones. While this problem has been solved for linear fluctuations, this work presents the first explicit calculation of all large-scale gauge-invariant phase-space variables in multifield inflation and at second order in perturbation theory, in a Hamiltonian language. Building upon the well-known Sasaki-Mukhanov variables, we show how to construct a finite-dimensional basis of quadratic corrections which are invariant under gauge transformations. Although our procedure is generic to any number of fields and at any scale, we restrict to super-Hubble scales for their explicit solution, which we deliver. Henceforth, we prove that it is possible to recover the usual flat-gauge and comoving-gauge fluctuations as large-scale gauge-invariant combinations, making for a robust consistency check of the gauge-fixed procedure to connect theoretical predictions above the horizon to observations. We derive the quadratic and cubic Hamiltonian of multifield inflation in a gauge independent manner, then we gauge fix our theory by going into the flat gauge, and we show perfect agreement with the literature on this topic, usually based on a Lagrangian approach. After these concrete steps, we propose a more formal proof of the existence of gauge-invariant variables at quadratic order, and we provide a sketch of the procedure that should allow to go to higher orders in perturbation theory.

</details>

### [108] [Microscopic Theory of Drag in a Bose Condensate Interacting with a Moving Reservoir](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27898)
*Hassan Alnatah,Shuang Liang,Shouvik Mukherjee,Qi Yao,Ashton S. Bradley,David W. Snoke*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文从微观层面推导了与运动储层相互作用的玻色—爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）的拖曳（drag）理论。通过在Born–Markov近似的框架下对费米子热库自由度进行积分，得到带拖曳势的有效Gross–Pitaevskii方程，进而推导出封闭形式的拖曳系数表达式，并将其应用于激子—极化激元凝聚体与漂移电子气的相互作用模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立玻色—爱因斯坦凝聚体与运动介质（moving reservoir）相互作用时拖曳效应的微观理论。从底层粒子相互作用出发，而不依赖唯象模型，以获得拖曳力的封闭解析表达式。

Method: 从凝聚体与漂移费米子热库（drifting fermionic bath）之间的相互作用出发，在Born–Markov近似下将热库自由度积分掉，得到一个包含拖曳势的有效Gross–Pitaevskii方程；在此基础上推导有效拖曳力及拖曳系数，该系数由储层密度涨落和凝聚体密度分布决定。

Result: 获得了拖曳系数的封闭解析表达式；并作为应用，模拟了激子—极化激元凝聚体（exciton–polariton condensate）与漂移电子气相互作用过程中的拖曳现象。

Conclusion: 该微观理论将拖曳系数与储层的密度涨落及凝聚体密度分布直接关联，为理解凝聚体在流动介质中的动力学提供了一条从微观相互作用出发的定量途径。

Abstract: We derive a microscopic theory of drag for a Bose-Einstein condensate interacting with a moving reservoir. Starting from interactions between a condensate and a drifting fermionic bath, we integrate out the bath degrees of freedom within the Born--Markov approximation to obtain an effective Gross--Pitaevskii equation with a drag potential. From this potential we derive an effective drag force and obtain a closed expression for the drag coefficient, determined by the reservoir density fluctuations and the condensate density profile. As an application, we simulate the drag for an exciton--polariton condensate interacting with a drifting electron gas.

</details>


### [109] [Coherence Resonance Phenomena in an Atom-Dimer Two Mode BECs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27947)
*Avinaba Mukherjee*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了受Feshbach共振耦合的原子-分子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）在非平衡状态下的动力学，具体考察了高斯白噪声（Gaussian white noise）分别作用于耦合强度和失谐（detuning）时对系统相干-共振点（coherence-resonance point）的影响。通过玻色约瑟夫森结（bosonic Josephson junction）和布洛赫球（Bloch-sphere）形式，发现当噪声的特征时间尺度与系统内在时间尺度匹配时，噪声表现出更强的相干作用，并导致时间平均纯度（purity）和Husimi-Q分布出现极值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解噪声在原子-分子BEC耦合系统中的非平衡动力学行为，特别是噪声如何影响系统的相干性与共振特性。

Method: 采用玻色约瑟夫森结和布洛赫球形式化方法，对包含高斯白噪声（分别作用于耦合强度和失谐）的原子-分子BEC系统进行动力学分析。

Result: 相干（耦合）噪声和失衡（失谐）噪声以依赖初始极化（initial polarization）和Feshbach失谐的方式改变相干-共振点；当噪声特征时间尺度与系统内在时间尺度匹配时，噪声发挥更明显的相干作用，时间平均纯度和Husimi-Q分布出现极值。

Conclusion: 噪声对原子-分子BEC动力学的相干性具有非单调的调控作用，其效果与时间尺度匹配密切相关。

Abstract: We investigate the non-equilibrium dynamics of atomic-molecular Bose-Einstein condensates coupled via a Feshbach resonance, with Gaussian white noise acting on both the coupling strength and the detuning. Using the bosonic Josephson-junction and Bloch-sphere formalisms, we examine how coherence (coupling) noise and imbalance (detuning) noise modify the coherence-resonance point as functions of the initial polarization and the Feshbach detuning. Noise plays a more coherent role when its characteristic timescale matches the intrinsic timescales of the dynamics, leading to extrema in the time-averaged purity and the Husimi-Q distribution.

</details>


### [110] [Sound propagation in one-dimensional quantum droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28028)
*Zizhou Yuan,Jiarui Xiao,Xiao-Long Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了由对称玻色-玻色混合物形成的一维量子液滴（quantum droplet）中的声传播行为，利用扩展Gross-Pitaevskii方程提取声速，发现其受密度轮廓和量子压贡献的强烈影响，且与常规玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（BEC）的声学行为存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子液滴中的声传播因自束缚（self-bound）性质和量子涨落（quantum fluctuations）的作用而与常规BEC不同，但目前对有限尺寸和约束效应如何影响声速的理解尚不充分。

Method: 采用扩展Gross-Pitaevskii方程模拟一维对称玻色-玻色混合物形成的量子液滴，通过实时传播局域密度扰动提取声速，并与低能激发谱进行比较，同时考察粒子数和外加谐振势约束的影响。

Result: 发现与常规BEC不同，有限液滴的声速受密度轮廓和量子压贡献的强烈影响；声速随粒子数增加而下降，液滴从高斯型轮廓演化为平顶（flat-top）轮廓并趋近体相量子液滴值；外部谐振约束会压缩液滴并增强声速，使其趋近囚禁BEC的声学行为。

Conclusion: 声传播可作为探测有限尺寸效应以及自束缚量子液滴与常规玻色气体之间交叉行为的灵敏工具，并为超冷³⁹K液滴中的实验观测提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Sound propagation in quantum droplets differs from that in conventional Bose-Einstein condensates (BECs) because of their self-bound nature and the role of quantum fluctuations. We investigate sound propagation in one-dimensional quantum droplets formed by a symmetric Bose-Bose mixture, focusing on finite-size and confinement effects. Using the extended Gross-Pitaevskii equation, we extract the sound velocity from the real-time propagation of localized density perturbations and compare it with the low-energy excitation spectrum. We find that, unlike in a conventional BEC, the sound velocity of a finite droplet is strongly affected by its density profile and quantum-pressure contribution. It decreases with increasing particle number as the droplet evolves from a Gaussian-like to a flat-top profile, approaching the bulk quantum-droplet value. In contrast, external harmonic confinement compresses the droplet and enhances the sound velocity, driving the system toward the acoustic behavior of a trapped BEC. Our results establish sound propagation as a sensitive probe of finite-size effects and the crossover between self-bound quantum droplets and conventional Bose gases, and suggest a feasible route for experimental observation in ultracold $^{39}$K droplets.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum thermodynamics near the border of a one-dimensional Bose gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28164)
*Carsten Henkel*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究超冷玻色气体在半开放势阱中的准一维体系，通过求解连续谱中的Bogoliubov方程，探讨了气体边缘区域超越局域密度近似（local density approximation）的粒子与能量分布。研究发现凝聚体密度梯度会增强边界区域的密度主导激发，边缘密度剖面不存在Friedel振荡，但表现出由热激发粒子泄漏产生的偶极特征。计算在巨正则系综（grand-canonical ensemble）和热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域密度近似常用于描述气体边缘行为，但在边缘梯度较强的区域可能失效；本文旨在超越该近似，通过直接求解连续谱中的Bogoliubov方程，更精确地刻画超冷玻色气体边界附近的基本激发及其粒子与能量含量。

Method: 采用半开放势阱约束的超冷玻色气体模型，将体系限制在准一维几何中；对基本激发（elementary excitations）在连续谱中求解Bogoliubov方程，并利用适合的准均匀体系参考解来对比讨论边界区域的过剩（缺失）粒子及其能量。计算在巨正则系综与热力学极限条件下进行。

Result: 凝聚体密度的梯度会增强气体边界区域中密度主导型激发的贡献；边缘附近的密度剖面不呈现Friedel振荡，而是显示出由热激发粒子外溢带来的偶极型特征。

Conclusion: 超越局域密度近似后，气体边缘的粒子与能量分布呈现出新的物理特征（偶极密度结构与增强的密度激发），表明边界梯度的效应不可忽略，并可为后续实验观测与理论深化提供参考。

Abstract: We consider an ultracold Bose gas in a half-open potential well, otherwise confined to a quasi-one-dimensional geometry. The Bogoliubov equations for its elementary excitations are solved in the continuous spectrum to explore the particle and energy content near the edge of the gas, beyond the often applied local density approximation. In particular, gradients in the condensate density enhance density-dominated excitations in the border region. We discuss excess (missing) particles and their energy by comparing to suitable reference solutions for quasi-homogeneous systems. The density profile near the edge shows no Friedel oscillations, but a dipolar feature from the spill-out of thermally excited particles. The calculations are performed in the grand-canonical ensemble and in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>

### [112] [Inclination-Induced Crossover in the Velocity Scaling of Lubrication-Mediated Droplet Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28203)
*Haruka Hitomi,Ko Okumura*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验测量浸没在黏性油中倾斜表面上的水滴蠕滑运动，发现液滴速度随尺寸的标度指数随倾角从3/2连续过渡到9/4，表明倾斜角是调控润滑介导液滴运动的关键参数，且该标度演化与液滴宏观形状的演变并不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液-液体系中润滑介导（lubrication-mediated）的液滴运动标度行为尚未被充分理解，尤其是倾角对速度-尺寸标度关系的影响机制缺乏系统的实验与理论分析。

Method: 在宽范围的液滴尺寸和倾角下实验测量浸没于黏性油中倾斜表面上滑动水滴的速度、宽度和高度，并结合基于Landau-Levich-Derjaguin薄膜形成和动态弯月面（dynamic meniscus）中黏性耗散的标度分析来解释观测到的标度行为。

Result: 液滴速度随半径呈幂律增长，但指数强烈依赖倾角：小倾角时接近3/2，大倾角时逐渐接近9/4，呈现连续倾角诱导的标度交叉；同时该速度标度的演化比宏观液滴形态的演变更为显著，速度标度趋近煎饼状（pancake）规律时，液滴几何尺寸仍远偏离煎饼极限形貌。

Conclusion: 倾斜角是控制润滑介导液滴运动的关键参数，观测到的标度交叉源于液滴下方主导润滑耗散机制的转变，该转变并不直接反映在全局液滴形貌中，凸显了液-液体系中全局形状演化与局部耗散动力学之间的差异。

Abstract: We experimentally investigate the creeping motion of water droplets sliding along an inclined surface immersed in a viscous oil. The droplet velocity, width, and height are measured over a broad range of droplet sizes and inclination angles. The velocity increases with droplet radius according to a power law, but the corresponding exponent depends strongly on the inclination angle. At small inclinations, the exponent is close to 3/2, whereas at larger inclinations it progressively approaches 9/4. The results therefore reveal a continuous inclination-induced crossover in the velocity-size scaling of lubrication-mediated droplet motion. A scaling analysis based on Landau-Levich-Derjaguin film formation and viscous dissipation in the dynamic meniscus reproduces these limiting behaviors, which correspond to the quasi-spherical and pancake scaling regimes, respectively.
  Remarkably, the evolution of the velocity scaling is substantially more pronounced than the corresponding evolution of the macroscopic droplet shape. While the velocity data progressively approach the pancake scaling law at large inclinations, the global droplet dimensions evolve in a more complex manner than expected from a simple pancake-shape picture. In particular, the approach to pancake-like velocity scaling occurs even when the measured droplet dimensions remain far from the corresponding pancake-limit geometry.
  These observations suggest a crossover in the dominant lubrication-dissipation mechanism beneath the droplet that is not directly reflected in the global droplet morphology. The results identify inclination angle as a key control parameter governing lubrication-mediated droplet motion and highlight the distinction between global shape evolution and local dissipation dynamics in liquid-liquid systems.

</details>

### [113] [Noise-robust navigation from an adaptive run-and-tumble policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27751)
*Aniruddha Datta,Shiladitya Banerjee*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过一个最小化的活性布朗粒子（active Brownian particle）模型，基于最优性原则推导出生长的“跑-停-转”（run-and-tumble）导航策略，发现方差适应（variance adaptation）内在地涌现为该策略的一部分，使生物在噪声引导信号下仍能保持有限的趋化漂移（chemotactic drift），但适应也带来了在安静环境中性能下降的代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物体在导航时常常面临噪声引导信号的干扰，方差适应在感觉系统中普遍存在，但其在导航中的作用尚未被研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索方差适应如何影响生物体的运动导航行为。

Method: 作者引入了一个最小化的活性布朗粒子模型，其“跑-停-转”运动策略由最优性原则推导得出。通过该理论框架，分析方差适应如何在最优策略中自然涌现，并对比适应性与非适应性粒子在不同噪声水平下的导航表现。

Result: 方差适应作为最优策略的一部分自发涌现；适应性粒子在噪声增大时能够保持趋化漂移有限，而非适应性粒子的趋化漂移则呈指数衰减。此外，适应行为在安静环境中会降低导航性能，且需要调节适应灵敏度才能发挥有效作用。

Conclusion: 方差适应是噪声环境下保持导航功能的关键机制，但其代价是牺牲安静环境中的性能，暗示生物体需要在适应性与灵敏性之间进行权衡，以适应不同噪声水平的环境。

Abstract: How do organisms navigate when the signals guiding them are noisy? Variance adaptation, the rescaling of sensitivity to noise, is common in sensory systems, but its role in navigation is unexplored. We introduce a minimal active Brownian particle whose run-and-tumble policy follows from an optimality principle. Variance adaptation emerges as part of this policy. Adaptation keeps chemotactic drift finite as noise grows, while a non-adaptive particle's collapses exponentially. Adaptation also carries a cost, degrading performance in quiet environments and requiring a tuned adaptation sensitivity.

</details>
