### [114] [AdS/CFT, Ultralimits and Baby universes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27563)
*Eyoab Bahiru*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类边界理论CFT_p（p∈βℕ/ℕ），在AdS/CFT对偶（AdS/CFT duality）框架下可被理解为若干理论的系综（ensemble）。这些理论仅在边界CFT的无穷N极限中涌现，且依赖于p，即依赖于在存在混沌或振荡算子时取广义无穷N极限的不同方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在理解AdS/CFT对偶中，当边界时空维数大于一时，引力路径积分与边界理论之间的关系，特别是解释引力路径积分如何对应于某种理论平均而非单一确定的理论。

Method: 作者提出将广义无穷N极限称为“超极限”（ultralimit），其中p是ℕ上的自由超滤子（free ultrafilter）。基于此构造边界理论CFT_p，并提出引力路径积分计算的是CFT_p在各扇区中的平均；在适当情况下该平均可产生婴儿宇宙（baby universes）与时空虫洞（spacetime wormholes）。该提议被应用于Antonini-Sasieta-Swingle（AS²）类态。

Result: 引力路径积分可被解释为对CFT_p各扇区的平均，在适当情形下产生婴儿宇宙和时空虫洞；该方案成功应用于AS²类态。

Conclusion: 通过引入基于超滤子的超极限概念，本文为AdS/CFT对偶中引力路径积分的系综解释提供了一种新框架，并将该框架推广至边界时空维数大于一的情形。

Abstract: We propose boundary theories, CFT$_{p}$ with $p\in β\mathbb{N}/\mathbb{N}$, which can be interpreted as an ensemble of theories in the context of AdS/CFT duality, when the boundary spacetime dimension is more than one. These theories are only emergent in the infinite $N$ limit of the boundary CFT and depend on $p$, which corresponds to the different ways one can take a generalized form of the infinite $N$ limit, in the presence of a chaotic or an oscillatory operator. This generalized infinite $N$ limit is called an \emph{ultralimit}, and $p$ is a \emph{free ultrafilter} on $\mathbb{N}$. We propose that the gravitational path integral computes an average of CFT$_{p}$ in each of its sectors; and in the appropriate cases, it produces baby universes and spacetime wormholes. We apply this proposal to the Antonini-Sasieta-Swingle (AS$^{2}$) like states.

</details>


### [115] [An Algebraic Obstruction to Ising Criticality for Finite-BCH Entanglers in Wavelet MERA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27564)
*Enrico Bertuzzo,Olindo Corradini,Claudia Frugiuele*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了将多尺度纠缠重整化拟设（MERA）扩展到相互作用量子场论时，使用有限阶Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff（BCH）多项式纠缠子的可行性。作者在二维相互作用的φ⁴理论中，通过wavelet MERA（wMERA）框架从解析上证明，此类纠缠子产生的有效势在一般情形下必然具有Landau形式，从而将整个类别限制在平均场普适性（mean-field universality）内；数值结果也确认临界指数β始终为1/2，无法趋近Ising值1/8，表明要复现非平均场临界行为可能需要非多项式或无限阶BCH构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将MERA推广到相互作用量子场论时，非高斯纠缠子的计算处理可能构成显著挑战，而自然策略是考虑Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff（BCH）展开在有限阶终止的多项式纠缠子。作者希望探究这类纠缠子是否能覆盖足够的普适性类别（universality classes）以正确描述相互作用场论中的临界行为。

Method: 作者在wavelet MERA（wMERA）框架下处理二维相互作用φ⁴理论，从解析角度分析任何有限阶BCH多项式纠缠子所生成的有效势的形式，并辅以数值计算检验各拟设（ansätze）下的临界指数β。

Result: 解析上证明，一般情形下有限BCH多项式纠缠子产生的有效势必然具有Landau形式，将整个类别限制在平均场普适性；在非一般的退化情形中，指数偏离平均场但仍无法复现Ising值。数值上，临界指数β在所有考虑的拟设中均保持为1/2，不随非局域范围或变分复杂度的增加而向Ising值1/8漂移。

Conclusion: 有限BCH多项式纠缠子类别存在代数阻碍（algebraic obstruction），无法覆盖非平均场普适性类别。要正确复现如Ising临界指数等非平均场行为，可能需要转向非多项式纠缠子或无限阶BCH构造。

Abstract: The computational treatment of non-Gaussian entanglers could pose a significant challenge when extending the Multi-Scale Entanglement Renormalization Ansatz (MERA) to interacting quantum field theories. A natural strategy is therefore to consider polynomial entanglers for which the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff (BCH) expansion terminates at finite order. In this work, we identify an algebraic obstruction that limits the universality classes accessible to this family of entanglers. Working within the wavelet MERA (wMERA) framework applied to the interacting $φ^4$ theory in two dimensions, we show analytically that the effective potential generated by any finite-BCH polynomial entangler is necessarily of Landau form in the generic case, establishing mean-field universality for the full class; in non-generic, degenerate cases the resulting exponent departs from mean-field but still fails to reproduce the Ising value. As a numerical illustration, the critical exponent $β$ remains consistent with its mean-field value $β= 1/2$ across all ansätze considered, with no drift toward the Ising value $β= 1/8$ as the nonlocality range or variational complexity increases. Reproducing non-mean-field criticality therefore might require non-polynomial or infinite-BCH constructions.

</details>


### [116] [Tidal effects on radiation in gravitational shockwave collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27585)
*Isabelle Blackstad,Himanshu Raj,Raju Venugopalan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了轻质量致密天体（能量μL）与重质量黑洞（能量μH≫μL）在冲击波对撞中产生的高频引力波辐射，建立了以冲击波传播子形式表达的散射形式解，揭示了与企鹅（Lipatov）顶点和再散射过程相关的引力子发射结构，并在软极限下简化为外引力子交换的Weinberg辐射振幅，同时展示了与杨-米尔斯冲击波中胶子辐射的双拷贝对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频引力波辐射为探测黑洞形成过程中的经典及半经典动力学提供了新窗口，而超相对论极限下的冲击波对撞（shockwave collision）是研究此类过程的理想框架，需要系统的理论计算方案。

Method: 通过冲击波传播子（shockwave propagator）形式解获得O(μLμH^n)阶潮汐响应函数的快照；采用微扰展开，从两个Regge化引力子聚变产生的Lipatov顶点出发计算引力子发射，并逐步推广至更高效阶，包含引力子从Regge化引力子再散射及三Regge化引力子顶点发射等贡献。

Result: 在软极限（ω→0）下，O(μLμH)阶Lipatov振幅退化为单引力子交换的Weinberg振幅的超相对论极限；O(μLμH^2)阶的再散射和三维顶点贡献同样简化为双引力子交换的Weinberg辐射振幅。全文还揭示了与致密-稀薄对撞中杨-米尔斯胶子辐射的双拷贝（double copy）结构对应关系，并将其与Veneziano等人的eikonal多重散射框架相联系。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了冲击波对撞中高频引力波辐射的系统微扰计算框架，其软极限下与Weinberg振幅的一致性为文中方法提供了关键验证，同时双拷贝结构的凸显暗示了引力与规范理论超相对论散射之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We compute high frequency gravitational wave radiation in shockwave collisions of a light mass compact object with energy $μ_L$ off a heavy compact object (black hole) with energy $μ_H \gg μ_L$. A formal solution is obtained in terms of shockwave propagators that provide snapshots of $O(μ_Lμ_H^n)$ tidal response functions sensitive to classical and potentially semi-classical dynamics of black hole formation. In a perturbative expansion, the first nontrivial contribution of $O(μ_Lμ_H)$ is the emission of a graviton from the Lipatov vertex generated by the fusion of two reggeized gravitons. For graviton frequencies $ω\rightarrow 0$, the Lipatov amplitude reduces to the ultrarelativistic limit of the single-graviton exchange Weinberg amplitude. At $O(μ_Lμ_H^2)$, the Lipatov amplitude includes a piece accounting for the rescattering of the graviton off a reggeized graviton and another, corresponding to graviton emission from a vertex fusing three reggeized gravitons. These nontrivial contributions again simplify to the Weinberg radiative amplitude for two-graviton exchange in the soft limit. We highlight throughout double copy structures to gluon radiation in dilute-dense collisions of Yang-Mills shockwaves. We relate our shockwave results to the eikonal multiple scattering framework of Veneziano et al.

</details>


### [117] [Positive Geometry of Yang-Mills Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27594)
*Mattia Arundine,Veronica Calvo Cortes,Joris Koefler,Facundo Rost*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了德西特（de Sitter）空间中三点和四点树级杨-米尔斯（Yang-Mills）关联子的正几何（positive geometry）表述，通过宇宙学格拉斯曼流形（cosmological Grassmannian）上的螺旋度剥离代表元实现，发现物理奇异性对应于几何边界，并证明了完整四点结果需要同调（homological）形式的正几何表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将正几何方法从平直时空推广到德西特空间中的宇宙学关联子，为高点多体关联子提供几何描述基础。当前的宇宙学杨-米尔斯关联子缺乏系统的几何构造框架。

Method: 采用螺旋度剥离（helicity-stripped）代表元在宇宙学格拉斯曼流形上进行Pffafian（或旋量）嵌入，将物理奇异点视为几何边界。在三点情形，关联子被构造为非负象限（non-negative orthant）的典范形式（canonical form）；在四点情形，通过Mandelstam除子划分Pffafian正域，并利用因子化条件与正确平直空间极限的唯一性约束选择定向区域并集，或通过同调圈的有符号线性组合构造完整结果。

Result: 三点杨-米尔斯关联子成为格拉斯曼流形非负象限的典范形式；四点情形中，Mandelstam除子将Pffafian正域划分为四个正几何区域，因子化条件和平直空间极限唯一地选出其中两个区域的定向并集，其典范形式复现了约化色序（reduced color-ordered）杨-米尔斯关联子，而完整色序关联子由唯一确定的有符号同调圈线性组合给出。

Conclusion: 正几何的广同调表述是捕捉完整四点结果所必需的，该构造为日后高阶宇宙学关联子的几何描述提供了具体起点。

Abstract: We develop a positive-geometric formulation of tree-level Yang-Mills correlators in de Sitter space at three and four points through their helicity-stripped representatives on the cosmological Grassmannian. In its Pfaffian (or spinor) embedding, physical singularities become natural geometric boundaries. At three points, the Yang-Mills correlator is the canonical form of the non-negative orthant in the Grassmannian. At four points, the Mandelstam divisors partition the Pfaffian-positive domain of the Grassmannian into four positive geometries. Requiring factorization into three-point forms, together with the correct flat-space limit, uniquely selects an oriented union of two of these regions, whose canonical form reproduces the reduced color-ordered Yang-Mills correlator. The full color-ordered correlator, on the other hand, arises from a uniquely fixed signed linear combination of homology cycles. Thus, the broader homological formulation of positive geometry is essential for capturing the complete four-point result. Our construction provides a concrete starting point for a geometric description of higher-point cosmological correlators.

</details>


### [118] [The Late-time Ramp of the Double-Scaled SYK Model from the large-$n$ tail of Cactus Diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27627)
*Yao Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文直接从仙人掌图（cactus diagrams）推导出双标度SYK模型中有限温度谱形状因子（spectral form factor）的晚期斜坡（ramp），重现了随机矩阵理论预言的线性斜坡，包括其温度依赖性和对称因子，为斜坡提供了微观起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单迹（single-trace）可观测量可用弦图（chord diagram）精确描述，但多迹（multi-trace）求和受弦交点权重 q_{IJ} 的阻碍，无法简单平均为单个 q。作者旨在建立斜坡现象的微观机制，填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将具有 n 次跨迹配对的仙人掌图分解为两个芽（buds）B_n 和一个核（kernel）K_n，分析其在固定 0≤q<1 条件下的大 n 渐近行为，并对尾部进行重求和。关键思想是利用斜坡相关的非解析贡献由大阶尾部控制、对低阶解析项的有限变化不敏感这一性质。

Result: 获得解析奇异贡献 Z_s^{sing}(β+it,β-it)=s_p c_N |t|/(2π) ∫ e^{-2βE} dE + O(1)，其中对称因子 s_p 取决于 p 是否被 4 整除。该结果重现了随机矩阵理论预言的线性斜坡，包含温度依赖与对称因子，并与半经典预言一致。

Conclusion: 该项工作为一般 q 形变量子代数框架内斜坡现象提供了直接的微观起源。平台（plateau）行为不在本研究的分析范围之内。

Abstract: We derive the late-time ramp of the finite-temperature spectral form factor in the double-scaled SYK model directly from cactus diagrams, which is introduced as a multi-trace generalization of chord diagram in Ref \cite{Berkooz:2020fvm}. Although single-trace observables admit an exact chord-diagram description, multi-trace sums are obstructed by the chord-intersection weights $q_{IJ}$, which cannot be averaged independently to $q$. Our key idea is that the non-analytic contribution responsible for the ramp is controlled by the large-order tail of the Cactus-diagram expansion and is insensitive to finite changes in its low-order analytic terms. Decomposing the contribution with $n$-cross-trace-pairings Cactus diagram into two buds $B_n$ and a kernel $\mathcal K_n$, we determine their large-$n$ asymptotics with fixed $0\leq q<1$ and resum the resulting tail. For $β_L=β+it$ and $β_R=β-it$, we obtain \begin{equation*} Z_s^{\mathrm{sing}}(β+it,β-it) =s_p c_N \frac{|t|}{2π} \int_{E_{min}}^{E_{max}} e^{-2βE} dE+O(1), \qquad s_p=\begin{cases}2,&4\mid p,\\1,&4\nmid p.\end{cases} \end{equation*} This result reproduces the linear ramp predicted by random matrix theory, including its temperature dependence and symmetry factor, and agrees with the semiclassical predictions. It provides a direct microscopic origin of the ramp within the general $q$-deformed quantum algebra. While the plateau lies beyond the scope of the present analysis and calculation.

</details>


### [119] [A geometric resolution limit from vacuum entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27640)
*Isbelia Martin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文建立了一个统一框架：电磁真空的纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）受弱时空曲率调控后，对量子激发施加一个由全局几何决定的最小径向分辨率下限，并由此导出依赖于环境的质量尺度，进而从第一性原理计算出电子质量的比例因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子场论中粒子质量参数是人为输入的，该工作试图从真空纠缠结构出发，将质量重新理解为真空所施加速度信息分辨极限的探针，从而将电子质量等基本参数与全局几何和纠缠信息联系起来。

Method: 从真空纠缠的面积定律出发，引入线性化引力（linearized gravity）通过微扰格林函数（perturbative Green's function）提供自然紫外截断；利用切向到径向的精确几何投影得到最小径向尺度 Δr_min ∝ r_s²/R。进一步通过曲率诱导的模密度偏移（mode density shift）的频谱分析，在Python数值框架（N=3000径向网格点，n_modes=1000）下从第一性原理确定无量纲因子 α。

Result: 推导出全局环境相关的质量尺度 m_geo ≡ ħR/(c r_s²)，对地球约2.84×10⁻³² kg；频谱分析给出 α(2)≈33，与电子基准值 α≈32天然匹配，无需可调参数。结果表明真空结构本身禁止在地面实验室测量比电子更轻的经典粒子。

Conclusion: 该框架将质量重铸为真空强加分辨率极限的探针，该极限由全局几何与纠缠决定；纠缠熵由此成为量子真空结构可测量的实验签名（signature）。

Abstract: We establish that the entanglement entropy of the electromagnetic vacuum, when regulated by weak spacetime curvature, imposes a fundamental lower bound on the radial resolution available to quantum excitations. Starting from the area law of vacuum entanglement, we demonstrate how linearized gravity introduces a natural ultraviolet regulator via the perturbative Green's function. An exact geometric projection from tangential to radial resolution yields a minimal radial scale $Δr_{\min} \propto r_s^2/R$, where $r_s$ is the Schwarzschild radius of the enclosing body and $R$ is the boundary radius.
  We analyze how particle propagation regimes depend on the relation between the Compton wavelength $λ_C$ and $Δr_{\min}$, showing that consistency $λ_C \gtrsim Δr_{\min}$ defines a global, environment-dependent mass scale $m_{\text{geo}} \equiv \hbar R/(c r_s^2)$. Measured particle masses satisfy $m = αm_{\text{geo}}$, where $α$ is a dimensionless factor encoding the vacuum's informational structure. For Earth, $m_{\text{geo}} \approx 2.84 \times 10^{-32}$ kg.
  We determine $α$ from first principles via spectral analysis of the curvature-induced mode density shift, implemented using a Python framework with $N=3000$ radial grid points and $n_{\text{modes}}=1000$. The calculation yields $α(2) \approx 33$, naturally matching the electron benchmark ($α\approx 32$) without adjustable parameters.
  This framework reframes mass as a probe of a vacuum-imposed resolution limit set by global geometry and entanglement. The vacuum structure itself forbids measurement of classical particles lighter than the electron in terrestrial laboratories, establishing entanglement entropy a measurable signature of quantum vacuum structure.

</details>


### [120] [Universal and Transcendental Structures in Protected ABJM Two-Point Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27701)
*Marco S. Bianchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了ABJM理论（ABJM theory）中受保护标量算符（protected scalar operators）两点函数（two-point functions）的双圈修正（two-loop corrections），推导出适用于任意维度和多迹（multi-trace）结构的紧致有限秩公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解ABJM理论中受保护算符的相关函数在高阶微扰层次上的结构，特别是验证均匀超越性（uniform transcendentality）的性质。

Method: 对ABJM理论中任意维度和多迹结构的受保护标量算符计算双圈修正，将结果归结为精确的树级度量与Young格（Young lattice）上的简单核（kernel），并研究平面极限（planar limit）下的约化形式。所需积分被组织为均匀超越的组合。

Result: 获得了普适的紧致有限秩公式，平面极限简化为一个简单的分拆理论（partition-theoretic）规则，且积分形成均匀超越组合，为该类受保护两点函数的均匀超越性提供了证据。

Conclusion: 受保护算符两点函数的双圈修正具有简洁的数学结构，并支持均匀超越性在该类相关函数中的成立。

Abstract: We consider two-loop corrections to two-point functions of protected scalar operators in ABJM theory. We infer a compact finite-rank formula valid for operators of arbitrary dimension and multi-trace structure. The result is governed by the exact tree-level metric and a simple kernel on the Young lattice, while the planar limit reduces to a simple partition-theoretic rule. The required integrals organize into uniformly transcendental combinations, providing evidence for uniform transcendentality of these protected two-point functions.

</details>


### [121] [Self-Dual Yang-Mills in AdS$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27731)
*Simon Heuveline,Romain Ruzziconi,Ahmed Sheta,Andrew Strominger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究欧几里得AdS$_4$空间中具有Neumann边界条件的量子杨-米尔斯理论（quantum Yang-Mills theory），在复耦合参数$\frac{1}{g_\pm^2}$下定义"自对偶极限"（self-dual limit）$g_- \to 0$。该极限理论在微扰意义下良好定义，等价于具有特定边界条件的BF自对偶杨-米尔斯理论，其中$g_+$充当圈计数参数。文章给出了该极限下所有树级零加号和单加号边界关联函数（boundary correlators）的消失证明，以及双加号树级关联函数的闭合表达式，并展示了平直空间极限下总能量极点正确约化为单负号胶子振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在探索AdS/CFT对应（AdS/CFT correspondence）框架下自对偶杨-米尔斯理论与边界理论的联系。与平直空间中$θ$参数不影响微扰动力学不同，在AdS$_4$中复耦合结构引入了新的非平凡效应，理解这种差异对揭示全息原理的结构具有重要意义。

Method: 作者在欧几里得AdS$_4$中研究带Neumann边界条件的量子杨-米尔斯理论，将其作为复耦合$\frac{1}{g_\pm^2}$的函数进行分析。通过定义$g_- \to 0$且$g_+$固定的"自对偶极限"，论证该极限在微扰意义下良定义，并等价于具有特定$B$-$F$边界关系的BF形式自对偶杨-米尔斯理论。文章进一步计算了边界关联函数（包括树级零加号、单加号和双加号关联函数），并取平直空间极限与已知单负号胶子振幅进行比较。

Result: 研究表明，在自对偶极限下所有树级零加号和单加号边界关联函数均消失；对于任意数目胶子的双加号树级关联函数给出了闭合形式表达式。在AdS$_4$的平直空间极限中，树级边界关联函数中的总能量极点（total energy poles）正确地约化为单负号胶子振幅（single-minus gluon amplitudes）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了AdS$_4$中量子杨-米尔斯理论自对偶极限与BF自对偶杨-米尔斯理论的等价性，揭示了全息框架下$θ$参数对微扰动力学的非平凡贡献——这与平直空间行为形成鲜明对比。所得闭合形式的关联函数表达式为研究自对偶理论的胶子散射提供了新工具。

Abstract: Quantum Yang-Mills theory in Euclidean AdS$_4$ with Neumann boundary conditions is studied as a function of the complex couplings $\frac{1}{g_\pm^2} \equiv \frac{1}{g^2}\mp \frac{i θ}{8 π^2}$. We define a "self-dual limit" by $g_- \to 0$ with $g_+ ~{\rm fixed}$. We argue that the limiting theory is well-defined perturbatively and equivalent to the BF formulation of self-dual Yang-Mills theory with a particular boundary condition relating $B$ and $F$ at the boundary $\partial$AdS$_4$. $g_+$ is the loop-counting parameter of the self-dual theory. This is in stark contrast to flat space, where $θ$ has no effect on perturbative dynamics. In the self-dual limit, it is shown that all tree-level zero-plus and single-plus boundary correlators vanish. A closed form expression is given for any number of gluons for the double-plus tree correlators. Finally, we show that in the flat space limit of AdS$_4$, the total energy poles in the tree boundary correlators correctly reduce to the single-minus gluon amplitudes.

</details>


### [122] [Seiberg dualities for quiver gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27803)
*Yuanyuan Fang,Jing Feng,Dan Xie*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了带基本、双基本和秩二张量物质的箭袋规范理论（quiver gauge theories）中的塞伯格对偶（Seiberg duality），从大N超共形指标（superconformal index）推导出矩阵Hilbert级数的因式分解方程，该方程自然地编码了未修饰介子谱的约束，并应用于两节点箭袋验证了已知的SU–SU和SO–USp乘积群对偶，同时讨论了有限N下由反常和算符谱匹配给出的一致性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塞伯格对偶的存在对未修饰介子（undressed mesons）谱施加了强约束，但现有框架缺乏对这些约束的系统性表征。作者旨在通过矩阵Hilbert级数（matrix Hilbert series）的因式分解方程来统一刻画这一约束结构，并建立从大N超共形指标推导该因子化的通用方法，以推广和验证已知的对偶关系。

Method: 作者推导了含有基本（fundamental）、双基本（bifundamental）和秩二张量（rank-two tensor）物质的通用箭袋规范理论的大N超共形指标闭式表达式，并由此导出矩阵Hilbert级数的因式分解方程。随后将该方法应用于两节点箭袋，分别恢复SU–SU和SO–USp乘积群对偶，并对有限N情形结合反常匹配和算符谱匹配进行一致性检验。

Result: 论文获得了编码塞伯格对偶约束的矩阵Hilbert级数因式分解方程，并系统地恢复了已知的SU–SU和SO–USp两节点对偶；此外还导出了有限N下由反常（anomaly）和算符谱（operator spectrum）匹配构成的一致性条件。

Conclusion: 矩阵Hilbert级数的因式分解方程为大N极限下塞伯格对偶的谱约束提供了统一的代数表述，该框架可覆盖含基本、双基本和秩二张量物质的一般箭袋理论，并可在有限N下通过反常与算符谱匹配进一步验证对偶关系的有效性。

Abstract: We study Seiberg duality for quiver gauge theories with fundamental, bifundamental, and rank-two tensor matter. The existence of a Seiberg dual description places strong constraints on the spectrum of undressed mesons, which is naturally encoded in a factorization equation for the matrix Hilbert series. We derive this factorization from a closed-form large-$N$ superconformal index for general quiver gauge theories with these matter contents. We apply the method to two-node quivers and recover known $SU$--$SU$ and $SO$--$USp$ product-group dualities. We also discuss finite-$N$ consistency conditions from anomaly and operator-spectrum matching.

</details>


### [123] [Superconformal index of large N and long quivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27933)
*Mohammad Akhond,Leonardo Santilli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个普适框架，用于计算箭形容器规范理论（quiver gauge theory）在大N极限下的生成函数，并将其应用于4维N=2理论超共形指标和5维N=1理论的0-瞬子指标，涵盖任意形状有限箭图和长箭图极限，同时处理的规范群包括特殊酉群（special unitary）和正交辛群（orthosymplectic）。在Hall-Littlewood极限下，级数展开的指标能再现期望的Higgs分支手征环生成元；长箭图极限下的精确表达式为全息对偶的翘曲AdS5和AdS6背景的超引力指标提供了新预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 箭形容器规范理论中生成函数的计算缺乏统一方法，尤其是在大N极限下覆盖任意箭图形状和多种规范群的场景。既有方法在处理特殊酉群和正交辛群等不同规范群时缺乏通用性，且长箭图极限下的精确结果与全息对偶预测的关联尚未系统建立。

Method: 本文发展了一个普适框架（universal framework）来计算大N极限下箭形容器规范理论中的生成函数，并将其应用于4维N=2超共形指标（superconformal index）和5维N=1理论的0-瞬子指标（0-instanton index），覆盖任意形状的有限箭图以及长箭图（long quiver）极限，同时处理特殊酉群与正交辛群规范群。

Result: 在Hall-Littlewood极限下，指标级数展开捕获了预期的Higgs分支手征环（chiral ring）生成元。在长箭图极限下获得的精确表达式为对偶翘曲AdS5和AdS6超引力背景的指标提供了新的预测。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了良好的通用性，能够统一处理不同类型规范群和箭图几何，并连接了场论侧指标计算与全息对偶（holographic duality）下的超引力指标预测。

Abstract: We develop a universal framework for computing generating functions in quiver gauge theories in the large $N$ limit. We apply this method to the superconformal index of 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ gauge theories, as well as to the 0-instanton index of 5d $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories, both for finite quivers of arbitrary shape and in the long quiver limit, including both special unitary and orthosymplectic gauge groups, demonstrating its versatility. In the Hall--Littlewood limit, we show that the series expansion of the resulting index captures the expected Higgs branch chiral ring generators. Our exact expressions in the long quiver limit provide new predictions for the supergravity index of the holographically dual warped AdS$_5$ and AdS$_6$ backgrounds.

</details>


### [124] [Holographic Ensembles in Type IIB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27956)
*Jesse van Muiden*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了type IIB弦理论在Schur扭曲AdS₅×S⁵背景上的全息系综，发现由于五形式场（five-form）的自对偶性，标准体积作用无法区分固定磁通与固定势两种系综，但可通过附加拓扑项（topological term）的耦合来区分，并在单圈水平上验证了其分别对应边界理论的正则系综与巨正则系综。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全息对应（holographic correspondence）框架下，需要理解超引力极限中不同的系综选择如何反映在体理论（bulk theory）的规范结构上。由于type IIB超引力中五形式场的自对偶性质，系综的选择无法通过标准的爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量（Einstein-Hilbert action）直接体现，因此需要引入新的机制加以区分。

Method: 作者在Schur扭曲的AdS₅×S⁵背景上研究type IIB弦理论的全息系综，并将体理论中的系综选择与耦合的附加拓扑项（topological term）联系起来。他们计算了体理论配分函数（bulk partition function）至单圈阶（one-loop），包含Kaluza-Klein行列式与系综变换求和的测度，并将结果与边界上N=4超对称杨-米尔斯（N=4 SYM）的Schur指标进行对比。

Result: 计算表明，拓扑项的符号决定了体理论配分函数与边界理论配分函数的匹配方式：一种符号对应边界理论在固定阶数（fixed rank）下的正则系综（canonical ensemble），另一种符号对应固定化学势（fixed chemical potential）下的巨正则系综（grand canonical ensemble）。两者的匹配在微扰水平上精确成立。

Conclusion: 本文建立了全息体系中体理论与边界理论系综选择的精确对应关系，指出系综差异性在体理论中通过拓扑项而非标准作用量体现，并验证了其在单圈水平的自洽性，为理解全息系综与量子场论配分函数之间的关系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We study holographic ensembles in type IIB string theory on the Schur twisted AdS$_5 \times S^5$ background, and its corresponding boundary partition function: the Schur index of $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM. In the supergravity limit the two natural ensembles are either at fixed flux on $S^5$, corresponding to a boundary theory at fixed rank, or its conjugate choice of fixed potential on the boundary of AdS$_5$, corresponding on the boundary to a grand canonical ensemble of theories at fixed chemical potential. Due to the self-dual nature of the five-form in type IIB supergravity the choice of ensembles is not seen in the standard bulk action, but rather by the coupling of an additional topological term. We compute the bulk partition function to one loop, including the Kaluza-Klein determinant and the measure of the ensemble-changing sum, and show that depending on the sign of this topological term it matches the perturbative boundary partition function in either the canonical or the grand canonical ensemble.

</details>


### [125] [Revisiting the $U(1)$-$CP$ tension in QCD from a 1PI-action perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28253)
*Paolo Di Vecchia,Francesco Sannino,Graham M. Shore,Gabriele Veneziano,Shimon Yankielowicz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在QCD框架内考察同时解决U(1)问题和强CP问题的张力。通过对1PI有效作用量施加Ward恒等式约束，证明在小非零θ角下避免CP破坏的唯一条件是反常贡献在赝标量质量矩阵中可忽略，而这恰恰意味着U(1)问题未被解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含轻夸克和小θ角（θ-bar angle）的QCD中，U(1)问题和强CP问题（strong-CP problem）之间存在内在张力，作者试图揭示这一张力的数学本质。

Method: 考虑一个由规范不变（复合）算符构建的1PI有效作用量Γ（1PI effective action）的低能展开，对Γ施加Ward恒等式（Ward identity）约束，并在次要假设下进行严格证明。

Result: 证明在小非零θ角下，CP破坏仅当反常诱导的赝标量质量矩阵贡献远小于非反常部分时才可避免，即U(1)问题必须未解决。QCD中的拓扑磁化率（topological susceptibility）以及纯杨-米尔斯理论中拓扑磁化率的推广，是确立该张力和确定θ角领头阶修正的关键。

Conclusion: U(1)问题的解决与强CP问题的解决在QCD内部难以同时实现；拓扑磁化率是联系两者并量化其冲突的核心物理量。

Abstract: We discuss the tension one encounters in trying to solve, simultaneously, the $U(1)$ and strong-$CP$ problems in QCD with light quarks and a small $\barθ$ angle within QCD itself. We do so by considering the low-energy expansion of a $1$PI effective action $Γ$, a functional of a properly chosen set of gauge-invariant (composite) operators. After enforcing Ward-identity constraints on $Γ$, we prove, under minor assumptions, that $CP$ violation at small non-vanishing $\barθ$ can be avoided only if the anomaly-induced contribution to the pseudoscalar mass matrix is completely negligible when compared to its non-anomalous counterpart - that is, only if the $U(1)$ problem is not solved. The topological susceptibility in QCD and a precise generalization of the one in pure Yang-Mills theory emerge as the most important players in establishing the above tension and in determining the corrections to various known results at leading order in $\barθ$.

</details>


### [126] [Scattering Equations as the lowest order K-identities in the calculation of Stringy Scaling of Hard String Scattering Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28311)
*Sheng-Hong Lai,Jen-Chi Lee,Yi Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文明确证明了早期提出的 n 点 K-恒等式（K-identities）在单张量硬弦散射振幅（HSSA）计算中的有效性，并引入 G 函数生成无穷集合的广义 K-恒等式（GKI），其最低阶对应 CHY 形式主义中的散射方程，次低阶对应 K-恒等式，并猜想高阶集合可用于计算高阶及多张量 HSSA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前在单张量硬弦散射振幅的鞍点计算中，K-恒等式是获得弦性标度行为并与零规范态退耦计算一致匹配的关键，但其数学证明此前尚未明确给出。本文旨在显式证明这些恒等式，并进一步推广以扩展其应用范围。

Method: 作者显式证明了 n 点 K-恒等式，并引入一个 G 函数来生成一组无穷的广义 K-恒等式（GKI）。这些 GKI 按阶分类：最低阶对应 CHY 形式主义中计算场论振幅所用的散射方程，次低阶对应此前用于单张量 HSSA 计算的 K-恒等式。

Result: K-恒等式被显式证明成立，确保了其在鞍点计算中的弦性标度行为与零规范态退耦结果的一致性。同时，通过 G 函数构造的 GKI 体系将散射方程、K-恒等式及更高阶恒等式统一在一个层级结构中。

Conclusion: 作者提出猜想：GKI 的更高阶集合可应用于计算高阶 HSSA 和/或多张量 HSSA，从而为弦振幅计算的进一步推广提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We prove explicitly the n-point K-identities we proposed previously in the calculation of one tensor hard string scattering amplitudes (HSSA). These K-identities were the key to obtain the stringy scaling behavior in the saddle point calculation of the n-point HSSA and, on the other hand, to consistently match with the calculation of decoupling of zero norm states. Moreover, we introduce a G function to generate an infinite set of generalized K-identities (GKI). The lowest order set of these GKI is the scattering equations (SE) used in the calculation of field theory amplitudes in the CHY formalism. The next to leading order set of these GKI is the K-identities used previously in the calculation of one tensor HSSA. We conjecture that the higher order sets of these GKI can be used to calculate higher order HSSA and/or multi-tensor HSSA.

</details>


### [127] [Stress-Testing Swampland Bounds with Class S Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28333)
*Gabriel Fenati,Miguel Montero,Irene Valenzuela*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在AdS₅引力时空中利用CFT对偶检验了尖锐化距离猜想（Sharpened Distance Conjecture, SharpDC）与修正距离猜想（Refined Distance Conjecture, RDC），证明了SharpDC对任意具有有限个规范因子且单圈β函数为零的大N拉格朗日超对称规范理论成立，同时发现该猜想在类S理论的大中心荷极限中并非普遍成立，并揭示了RDC与黎曼面模空间Weil-Petersson直径上界之间的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 距离猜想在弦论与量子引力中具有重要意义，但其更精细的形式——SharpDC与RDC——在具体AdS/CFT例子中的适用性尚未被系统检验。本文旨在通过全息对偶在AdS₅引力时空中严格检验这些猜想的有效范围。

Method: 采用CFT对偶方法，在AdS₅引力时空中检验SharpDC与RDC。对SharpDC，作者分析了任意大N拉格朗日超对称规范理论（具有有限个规范因子M且单圈β函数为零）并给出证明；同时考察类S（Class S）理论在大中心荷极限下的行为。对RDC，则在无管道的类S理论模空间中进行分析，将问题转化为对黎曼面模空间Weil-Petersson直径的上界估计。

Result: 证明了SharpDC对任意有限个规范因子且单圈β函数为零的大N拉格朗日超对称规范理论成立。但发现SharpDC并非普遍成立：在类S理论的M→∞极限（N固定）中，存在违反该猜想的明确反例，此时体内部几何尺寸远大于AdS曲率标度。同时，RDC在无管道类S理论的大N极限下相当于对亏格为g的黎曼面模空间Weil-Petersson直径给出了精确上界，该上界与现有全部数学文献一致但尚未被证明。

Conclusion: SharpDC在广泛的超对称规范理论类别中成立，但并非在所有极限下普遍有效，尤其在大中心荷的类S理论极限中会出现违反案例。RDC则在类S无管道理论的大N极限中归结为一个尚待证明的几何数学猜想，体现了全息距离猜想与模空间几何之间深刻的联系。

Abstract: We test the Sharpened Distance Conjecture (SharpDC) for CFTs, as well as the Refined Distance Conjecture (RDC), in AdS$_5$ gravitational spacetimes, using the CFT dual. The former provides a lower bound on the exponential mass decay rate of the tower of states becoming light at infinite distance in the conformal manifold, while the latter bounds the bulk field range that can be traversed before the exponential behaviour of the tower kicks in. We prove the SharpDC for any large $N$ Lagrangian supersymmetric gauge theory with a finite number $M$ of gauge factors and vanishing one-loop $β$-function. We also investigate whether it universally holds in the limits of large central charge, finding that it does not: We exhibit concrete examples in Class S theories where the conjecture is violated in the $M\rightarrow\infty$ limit with $N$ fixed, where the size of the bulk internal geometry is much larger than the AdS curvature scale. We also test the RDC in the moduli space of Class S theories without punctures in the large $N$ limit, showing that it amounts to a precise upper bound on the Weil-Petersson diameter of the moduli space of Riemann surfaces of genus $g$. This bound is consistent with all the mathematical literature up to date, but so far remains unproven.

</details>


### [128] [Positivity constraints on the dynamics of cusped impurities in CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28531)
*Jeevan Chandra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将对尖点反常维度（cusp anomalous dimension）的概念推广到具有平面共形边界或界面的体系中，通过构造包含三个角和尖点到边界距离的几何，识别出短距离与远距离两种大对数贡献，并利用反射正性（reflection positivity）推导出对数系数对角度Hessian的负半正定性约束，最后在自由标量理论、麦克斯韦理论、平面N=4超杨-米尔斯理论以及全息D3-D5缺陷CFT中进行了显式验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖点反常维度作为线缺陷自由能中的普适对数贡献，是刻画缺陷动力学的有用观测量。本文的动机在于将其推广至存在平面共形边界或界面的情形，以探究边界或界面对缺陷动力学的影响。

Method: 构造由三个角和尖点到边界距离表征的几何构型，分离出短距离与远距离两个对数项；利用反射正性对缺陷射线横向变形施加约束，推导对数系数角度Hessian的负半正定性及洛伦兹运动学延拓的约束条件；在自由标量、麦克斯韦、平面N=4超杨-米尔斯理论弱耦合及全息D3-D5缺陷CFT强耦合下分别计算与验证。

Result: 在弱耦合（自由标量、麦克斯韦、平面N=4超杨-米尔斯理论）中显式验证了约束条件；在强耦合全息D3-D5缺陷CFT中发现尖点相对界面的几何驱动了竞争者弦世界面之间的Gross-Ooguri型相变，并观察到该场景中尖点自由能与全息角纠缠熵的密切联系。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了含平面共形边界或界面的尖点线缺陷自由能的对数系数约束体系，并揭示了全息强耦合下的相变现象及与角纠缠熵的深层联系。

Abstract: The cusp anomalous dimension is the universal logarithmic contribution to the free energy of a line defect with a cusp, and provides a useful observable characterizing defect dynamics. We generalize this notion to cusped line defects in the presence of a planar conformal boundary or interface. The geometry is characterized by three angles, and the distance of the cusp from the boundary or interface which separates short-distance and long-distance regimes. The resulting free energy contains two large logarithmic terms: a short-distance contribution governed by the usual vacuum cusp anomalous dimension, and a new long-distance contribution sensitive to the boundary or interface. Using reflection positivity for transverse deformations of the defect rays, we derive several constraints on the coefficients of these logarithmic terms, which can be packaged into negative semi-definiteness of their angular Hessians. We also derive constraints on the continuation of these coefficients to Lorentzian kinematics. We compute these coefficients and verify the constraints explicitly in free scalar and Maxwell theories and in planar $\mathcal N=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory at weak coupling for various choices of boundary conditions. At strong coupling, we compute the logarithmic coefficients in the holographic D3-D5 defect CFT and find a Gross-Ooguri-like phase transition between competing string worldsheets driven by the geometry of the cusp relative to the interface. We also observe a close connection of the cusp free energy in this setup with holographic corner entanglement entropy.

</details>


### [129] [The Lagrangian Amplituhedron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28561)
*Syl Tom Krovi,Ran J. Tessler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了amplituhedron的拉格朗日（Lagrangian）类比物，研究其几何性质及BCFW分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在将amplituhedron这一几何对象推广到拉格朗日框架下，以探索散射振幅（scattering amplitudes）的更深层几何结构。

Method: 文章采用构造性方法，构建amplituhedron的拉格朗日类比物，并对其几何性质与BCFW分解进行系统研究。

Result: 成功构造出amplituhedron的拉格朗日类比对象，并阐明了其几何性质及BCFW分解结构。

Conclusion: 该构造为理解散射振幅的几何与组合结构提供了拉格朗日视角的新工具，可能有助于深化对BCFW递推关系的几何理解。

Abstract: The article constructs the Lagrangian analog of the amplituhedron, studies its geometric properties and BCFW decompositions.

</details>

### [130] [Landau theory of quenched criticality in linear in-context learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28059)
*Daesik Kim,Sumin Choi,Hyojae Jeon,Jung Hoon Han*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过将线性上下文学习（in-context learning）中的双重下降奇异性（double-descent singularity）表述为淬火无序系统（quenched disordered system）的临界现象，构建了一个朗道理论（Landau theory），将参数波动识别为奇异误差的微观起源，并通过数值解独立验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性上下文学习模型的预测误差在预训练样本数量与可学习参数数量相当时会出现双重下降奇异性，但其微观机制尚不清楚。本文旨在将此插值奇异性（interpolation singularity）系统化为统计物理学中的临界现象，以建立对线性上下文学习中插值临界性的坚实统计物理理解。

Method: 将插值奇异性表述为淬火无序系统的临界现象；比较同一线性ICL模型的退火（annealed）与淬火（quenched）描述，识别样本间连通涨落（connected sample-to-sample fluctuations）为奇异误差的微观起源；通过积分重整化脊参数（renormalized ridge parameter）ξ的腔自洽方程（cavity self-consistency equation）构造朗道势；以规范化样本复杂度（normalized sample complexity）τ为温度，临界温度τc=1，导出临界指数(β_cr, δ_cr, γ_cr)=(1,2,1)。

Result: 朗道理论在序参数上是三次的，临界指数为(β_cr, δ_cr, γ_cr)=(1,2,1)；朗道磁化率（Landau susceptibility）正是预测误差涨落贡献中发散的量；大上下文区间出现以序参数受抑为特征的赝能隙型（pseudogap-like）区段；理论预测与原始学习问题的数值解在数量上表现良好的一致性。

Conclusion: 本文为线性上下文学习中的插值临界性提供了统计物理学层面的系统描述，揭示了该系统的临界行为本质上由配分函数分母的涨落贡献所驱动，并通过数值验证确立了其定量有效性，为后续研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) allows a pretrained model to infer a new task from examples supplied in its prompt without updating its parameters. In linear models of ICL, the prediction error develops a double-descent singularity when the number of pretraining samples becomes comparable to the number of learnable parameters. We formulate this interpolation singularity as a critical phenomenon of a quenched disordered system. By comparing annealed and quenched descriptions of the same linear ICL model, we identify the connected sample-to-sample fluctuations of the learned parameters as the microscopic origin of the singular error. A Landau potential is constructed by integrating the cavity self-consistency equation for the renormalized ridge parameter $ξ$. The role of (magnetization) order parameter is played by $ξ$, while the bare ridge parameter $λ$ becomes its conjugate magnetic field. The normalized sample complexity $τ$ acts as a temperature and the double-descent singularity occurs at the critical temperature $τ_c =1$. The Landau susceptibility is precisely the quantity that diverges in the fluctuation contribution to the prediction error. The order parameter is closely related to the fraction of zero eigenvalues of the empirical relaxation matrix in the ridgeless limit, which define flat directions in the learning dynamics. The Landau theory is generically cubic in the order parameter with critical exponents $(β_{\rm cr},δ_{\rm cr},γ_{\rm cr})=(1,2,1)$. In the large-context regime, there appears a pseudogap-like regime characterized by suppressed order parameter. Predictions of the Landau theory are independently confirmed from numerical solutions of the original learning problem with good quantitative agreement. Our results pave the way for solid statistical-physics understanding of the interpolation criticality in linear in-context learning.

</details>

### [131] [CGC and saturation approach: Impact-parameter dependent model of perturbative QCD and combined HERA data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.06406)
*Michael Roa,José Garrido,Miguel Guevara*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将由色玻璃凝聚体/饱和（Color Glass Condensate/saturation）方法提出的模型与HERA实验数据拟合，并确定其参数。该模型结合了非线性BK演化方程的解析解以及饱和动量对碰撞参数b的指数依赖（满足Froissart定理），并在小x区间的宽Q²范围内成功描述了质子结构函数F₂、纵向结构函数F_L、粲结构函数、专属矢量介子产生及深层虚拟康普顿散射（DVCS）等各类过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在用基于色玻璃凝聚体/饱和有效理论的高能QCD模型与HERA实验数据进行系统比较，通过拟合获得模型参数，从而验证该理论框架在小x深非弹性散射（DIS）中的预测能力，并为未来实验（如电子-离子对撞机EIC和LHeC）提供基于第一性原理的可靠预测依据。

Method: 模型包含两个符合深非弹性散射理论认知的特征：一是采用非线性Balitsky-Kovchegov（BK）演化方程的解析解；二是假设饱和动量Q_s随碰撞参数b呈指数衰减，即Q_s ∝ exp(-mb)，该假设确保了大b处散射振幅满足Froissart定理的正确行为。随后将模型结果与HERA联合数据在小x区域的F₂、F_L、F₂^{c̄c}、专属矢量介子（J/ψ, φ, ρ）产生及DVCS过程进行对比。

Result: 在宽Q²范围和小x区域内，模型对上述多个过程均与实验数据取得了良好一致，从而验证了该CGC/饱和模型的适用性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为基于色玻璃凝聚体/饱和有效理论构建从第一性原理出发的高能QCD可靠预测方法提供了强有力指引，尤其对即将开展的电子-离子对撞机（EIC）和LHeC实验具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: In this paper we confront the CGC/saturation approach of Ref.~\cite{CLMS} with the experimental combined HERA data and obtain its parameters. The model includes two features that are in accordance with our theoretical knowledge of deep inelastic scattering. These consist of: \textit{i}) the use of analytical solution for the non-linear Balitsky-Kovchegov (BK) evolution equation and \textit{ii}) the exponential behavior of the saturation momentum on the impact parameter $b$-dependence, characterized by $Q_s$ $\propto\exp(-mb)$ which reproduces the correct behavior of the scattering amplitude at large $b$ in accord with Froissart theorem. The model results are then compared to data at small-$x$ for the structure-function of the proton $F_{2}$, the longitudinal structure function $F_{L}$, the charm structure function $F_2^{c\bar{c}}$, the exclusive vector meson ($J/ψ,φ,ρ$) production and Deeply Virtual Compton Scattering (DVCS). We obtain a good agreement for the processes in a wide kinematic range of $Q^2$ at small-$x$. Our results provide a strong guide for finding an approach, based on Color Glass Condensate/saturation effective theory for high energy QCD, to make reliable predictions from first principles and for forthcoming experiments like the Electron-Ion Collider and the LHeC.

</details>


### [132] [Next-to-Leading-Order Electroweak Corrections to Quantum Observables in Lepton-Lepton Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27559)
*Spencer Chang,Thomas Driscoll,Sokratis Trifinopoulos*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究虚次前导电弱（next-to-leading-order electroweak）辐射修正对轻子–轻子碰撞中纠缠（entanglement）与魔法（magic）等量子可观测量（quantum observables）的影响，在单圈精度下计算了ℓ⁺ℓ⁻ → τ⁻τ⁺和ℓ⁺ℓ⁻ → ZZ过程的纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）与稳定子二阶Rényi熵（stabilizer second Rényi entropy），发现辐射修正会相干地改变末态的量子结构，并为未来轻子对撞机提供精度敏感的量子探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息和粒子物理的交叉研究近年来受到关注，但此前的工作大多停留在树图（leading order）精度，尚未系统考察辐射修正对末态量子关联编码的影响。作者旨在探索虚电弱高阶修正是否以及在多大程度上会改变由纠缠和魔法所刻画的量子特性，从而为寻找新物理提供新的观测途径。

Method: 将末态粒子视为多量子比特（multi-qudit）系统，其中自旋1/2费米子编码为量子比特（qubit），质量矢量玻色子编码为量子三比特（qutrit）。在单圈（one-loop）精度下，针对ℓ⁺ℓ⁻ → τ⁻τ⁺和ℓ⁺ℓ⁻ → ZZ两个过程，利用包含虚次前导电弱修正的振幅计算纠缠熵和稳定子二阶Rényi熵，并与树图结果进行对比。

Result: 辐射修正会相干地（coherently）修正末态的量子结构：最大纠缠区域发生位移，产生的魔法量（magic）相对树图结果发生变化。这些变化表明量子可观测量对电弱动力学具有精度敏感性。

Conclusion: 纠缠和魔法等量子可观测量可作为未来轻子对撞机上探测电弱动力学的新型精度敏感探针，并为将其用于新物理搜索开辟了途径。

Abstract: We study how virtual next-to-leading-order electroweak corrections affect the quantum observables of entanglement and magic in lepton--lepton collisions. Treating the outgoing particles as multi-qudit systems, $\textit{i.e.}$, qubits for spin-$\tfrac{1}{2}$ fermions and qutrits for massive vector bosons, we compute entanglement entropy and the stabilizer second Rényi entropy for the processes $\ell^+\ell^- \to τ^-τ^+,\, ZZ$ at one-loop accuracy. We show that radiative corrections coherently modify the quantum structure of the final state, shifting regions of maximal entanglement and altering the generated magic relative to leading order. Our results demonstrate that these quantum observables provide novel, precision-sensitive probes of electroweak dynamics at future lepton colliders and open a path toward their use in searches for new physics.

</details>


### [133] [Future of Artificial Intelligence for Science in Japan 2024 Community Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27807)
*Yoshitaka Itow,Jia Liu,Hirokazu Maesaka,Vinicius Mikuni,Nhat-Minh Nguyen,Hironao Miyatake,Atsushi J. Nishizawa,Patrick de Perio,Daniel Ratner,Kazuhiro Terao,Leander Thiele,Omar Alterkait,Francois Drielsma,Rocio Garcia,Masako Iwasaki,Ahsani Hafizhu Shali,Federica Tarsitano,Takahiro Terada,Junjie Xia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文是FAIRS Japan 2024非会议(unconference)过程形成的白皮书，总结了加速器物理、宇宙学与天体物理、中微子物理三大物理领域的科学挑战与人工智能/机器学习(AI/ML)研究机遇，并提炼了多个跨领域共同技术主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在三大物理研究领域中识别AI/ML应用的科学挑战与研究机会，并通过跨领域对比来发现通用技术主题，从而指导未来研究投入方向。

Method: 采用FAIRS Japan 2024非会议(unconference)过程进行讨论与头脑风暴，围绕加速器物理、宇宙学与天体物理、中微子物理三个主要领域展开，并对各领域的技术需求进行归纳与交叉对比。

Result: 识别出七个跨领域共同技术主题：高维重建(high-dimensional reconstruction)、快速准确的模拟(fast and accurate simulation)、不确定性传播(uncertainty propagation)、模拟与数据失配(simulation-to-data mismatch)、异常检测(anomaly detection)、实时决策(real-time decision-making)以及共享基础设施(shared infrastructure)。

Conclusion: 尽管三个物理领域在科学目标和实验约束上各不相同，它们共享大量通用的AI/ML技术需求，这为跨领域合作与共享方法开发提供了基础。

Abstract: This white paper summarizes scientific challenges and AI/ML research opportunities identified through the FAIRS Japan 2024 unconference process. The discussion focuses on three major physics domains: accelerator physics, cosmology and astrophysics, and neutrino physics. Although each domain has distinct scientific goals and experimental constraints, several common technical themes emerge: high-dimensional reconstruction, fast and accurate simulation, uncertainty propagation, simulation-to-data mismatch, anomaly detection, real-time decision-making, and shared infrastructure.

</details>


### [134] [Vector-boson-fusion timing references for four-dimensional displaced-vertex searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27868)
*Renjie Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用HL-LHC精确计时层（precision timing layer）的时间信息来增强对长寿命粒子（long-lived particles）衰变搜索的灵敏度。以矢量玻色子聚变（vector-boson fusion, VBF）产生的希格斯玻色子稀有衰变h→ss、s→bb̄为例，通过引入类CMS-MTD桶部计时层将每位移顶点转变为四维对象，使时间增强灵敏度与纯空间灵敏度之比在标称工作点提升约2.8倍，在更严格的延迟顶点要求下可提升一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长寿命粒子在径迹探测器内衰变可通过其衰变顶点的空间位移来搜索，然而这类衰变同样会延后到达，这一特性未被位移顶点搜索所利用。HL-LHC的精确计时层为此提供了新物理可能性，但已有研究尚未探索该手段。

Method: 研究针对VBF过程pp→hjj、h→ss、s→bb̄的奇异衰变，利用VBF标记喷注（tag jets）识别硬散射顶点，其瞬发中心径迹由类CMS-MTD桶部计时层定时，结合位移顶点的中心径迹确定每事件起始时间，将每位移顶点转化为四维对象。因快速模拟无法确定重味和仪器位移顶点本底的绝对归一化，采用无归一化优值——时间增强灵敏度与纯空间灵敏度的比值作为主要结果。

Result: 加入计时层在标称工作点将时间增强与空间灵敏度之比提升约2.8倍，在更严格的延迟顶点要求下可提升达一个数量级，原因是空间选择后幸存的重味本底在碰撞时间上是瞬发的。改进幅度随衰变长度增加而增大，将灵敏度延伸到纯空间搜索失去径迹探测器接收度的长寿命区域。在衰变长度ctau≈100mm处增益基本与质量无关，其后趋势反转：短寿命时轻标量因更大洛伦兹增强（boost）获益更多，长寿命时重标量获益更多，因轻态会泄漏出径迹探测器。

Conclusion: 基于类CMS-MTD计时层的时间信息可显著增强对长寿命粒子衰变顶点的搜索灵敏度和寿命覆盖范围，且该增益在空间本底被抑制后尤为突出，为HL-LHC上的位移顶点搜索提供了一种互补且可扩展的新途径。

Abstract: Long-lived particles that decay inside the tracker are searched for via the spatial displacement of their decay vertices. However, the same decays also arrive late, providing a handle made available by a precision timing layer at the HL-LHC that displaced-vertex searches do not exploit. We study the exotic decay of a Higgs boson produced through vector-boson fusion (VBF), $pp\to hjj$ with $h\to ss$, in which the long-lived scalar decays as $s\to b\bar b$, and use the VBF tag jets to identify the hard-scatter vertex. Its prompt central tracks, timed by a CMS-MTD-like barrel layer that also times the central tracks of the displaced vertex, define a per-event start time, turning each displaced vertex into a four-dimensional object. Because a fast simulation cannot determine the absolute normalization of the heavy-flavour and instrumental displaced-vertex backgrounds, we take as the primary result a normalization-free figure of merit: the ratio of the timing-enhanced sensitivity to the spatial-only sensitivity. Adding the timing layer improves this ratio by a factor of $\simeq2.8$ at a nominal working point and by up to an order of magnitude for a tighter delayed-vertex requirement, because the heavy-flavour background that survives the spatial selection is prompt in collision time. The improvement increases with decay length, extending sensitivity into the long-lifetime regime in which purely spatial searches lose tracker acceptance. The gain is essentially mass-independent at a decay length of $\ctau\simeq100\mm$, where the trend with scalar mass reverses: lighter scalars benefit more at short lifetimes because of their larger boost, whereas heavier scalars benefit more at long lifetimes as the lighter states leak out of the tracker.

</details>


### [135] [Magnetic Moments and Radiative Transitions of the $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ and its partner states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27961)
*Jing Wu,Yi-Kun Wang,Kai-Bao Chen,Yan-Rui Liu,Jian-Bo Cheng,Wei-Hua Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文基于色磁相互作用（chromomagnetic interaction, CMI）系统地研究了S波双重味四夸克态（doubly heavy tetraquark states)的磁矩（magnetic moments, MMs）和辐射衰变宽度（radiative decay widths），通过磁矩和M1跃迁的预言为区分紧致四夸克结构与分子态结构提供了补充探针，并识别出与观测到的T_cc(3875)^+相关的一系列辐射跃迁通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在质量谱和强衰变性质之外，需要新的观测量来区分四夸克态的不同结构（紧致态vs分子态）。磁矩和辐射衰变宽度作为互补探针，可用于揭示双重味四夸克态的内部结构。

Method: 基于色磁相互作用模型，利用CMI本征矢量（eigenvectors）计算紧致构型下四夸克态的磁矩和M1跃迁率（M1 transition rates）。同时定义表征J^P=1^+四夸克系统磁矩的磁耦合矩阵（magnetic coupling matrices），用于约束磁矩的取值范围。

Result: 预测作为紧致四夸克态（compact tetraquark state）的T_cc(3875)^+（I(J^P)=0(1^+)）磁矩为0.45 μ_N，而0(1^+) DD^*分子态的磁矩约为-0.07 μ_N。识别出五个与T_cc(3875)^+相关的辐射跃迁通道，宽度范围为6.07 keV到306.37 keV。结果显示J^P=1^+的bc̄q̄q̄'和J^P=1^+的QQ̄n̄s态的磁矩受双夸克自旋混合（diquark-spin mixing）影响。

Conclusion: QQ̄n̄n̄'、QQ̄s̄s̄和bc̄n̄s̄系统中的辐射跃迁过程可用于揭示初态或末态的四夸克结构。该研究为未来粒子物理实验中奇异态（exotic states）的搜索提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: We systematically investigate the magnetic moments (MMs) and radiative decay widths of S-wave doubly heavy tetraquark states based on chromomagnetic interaction (CMI). They provide a complementary probe to distinguish compact from molecule structures in addition to the mass spectrum and strong decay properties. Using the CMI eigenvectors, we compute the MMs and M1 transition rates for the tetraquarks in the compact configuration. We predict the MM of the observed $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ with $I(J^P)=0(1^+)$ to be 0.45 $μ_N$ when treating it as a compact tetraquark state, whereas the MM of the $0(1^+)$ $DD^*$ molecule is about $-0.07\,μ_N$. Five radiative transition channels related to the $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ are identified with widths ranging from $6.07$ keV to $306.37$ keV. Our results show that the MMs of the $J^P=1^+$ $bc\bar{q}\bar{q}^\prime$ ($q/q^\prime=u,d,s$) and $J^P=1^+$ $QQ\bar{n}\bar{s}$ ($Q=b,c; n=u,d$) states are influenced by the diquark-spin mixing. Through the analyses of radiative transitions between different tetraquark states, we find that such processes in the $QQ\bar{n}\bar{n}^\prime$, $QQ\bar{s}\bar{s}$, and $bc\bar{n}\bar{s}$ cases may serve to reveal the tetraquark structures of the initial or final states. We also define the magnetic coupling matrices characterizing the MMs of the tetraquark system with $J^P=1^+$, with which the range of MM can be constrained. The present study provides a valuable reference point for the search of exotic states in future particle physics experiments.

</details>


### [136] [Investigation of S-wave tetraquark bound and resonant states with all Jacobi coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28284)
*Xin-He Zheng,Yao Ma,Liang-Zhen Wen,Shi-Lin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在组分夸克势模型（constituent quark potential model）框架下，系统研究了 Qs\bar{n}\bar{n}、QQ\bar{n}\bar{n}、QQ\bar{Q}\bar{Q} 和 ss\bar{s}\bar{s} 四类 S 波四夸克体系，通过引入 K 型雅可比坐标并结合复标度方法识别束缚态与共振态，证实 K 型构型对提取高激发共振至关重要，并找到了 T_{cs0}(2900)、X(6900) 和 X(7200) 的共振候选态，同时排除了 φ(2170) 和 X(2370) 作为全奇异 S 波四夸克凝聚态的解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四夸克态（tetraquark）中的共振态研究对理解强子谱与强相互作用至关重要，而传统 H 型构型可能不足以描述高激发共振；此外，若干实验观测态（如 T_{cc}(3875)^+、X(6900) 等）的四夸克本质尚未定论，亟需系统性的理论研究加以鉴别。

Method: 在组分夸克势模型中，作者对所有 S 波四夸克体系同时纳入 H 型和全部 K 型雅可比坐标（Jacobi coordinates），采用随机参数生成策略优化基组展开，并运用复标度方法（complex scaling method）识别束缚态与共振态。

Result: 计算表明，传统 H 型构型足以描述低激发态（如 T_{cc}(3875)^+ 分子候选态），但 K 型构型的引入对提取高激发共振态至关重要，使仅用 H 型无法获得的更高能量共振得以识别。此外，研究找到了 T_{cs0}(2900)、X(6900) 和 X(7200) 的共振候选态，而在 2.6 GeV 以下未发现全奇异凝聚态极点，从而不支持将 φ(2170) 和 X(2370) 解释为 S 波 ss\bar{s}\bar{s} 四夸克凝聚态。

Conclusion: K 型雅可比坐标的纳入对完整描述四夸克共振谱是必要的；T_{cs0}(2900)、X(6900) 和 X(7200) 有望作为 S 波四夸克共振态得到理解，而 φ(2170) 与 X(2370) 的全奇异四夸克解读则缺乏理论支持。

Abstract: We systematically explore the $S$-wave tetraquark systems $Qs\bar{n}\bar{n}$, $QQ\bar{n}\bar{n}$, $QQ\bar{Q}\bar{Q}$, and $ss\bar{s}\bar{s}$ ($Q=c,b$; $n=u,d$) within the constituent quark potential model. We incorporate all K-type Jacobi coordinates in addition to the conventional H-type configurations, optimize the basis expansion via a stochastic parameter generation strategy, and apply the complex scaling method to identify bound and resonant states. Our calculations demonstrate that while conventional H-type configurations suffice for low-lying states such as the $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ molecular candidate, the inclusion of K-type configurations becomes important for extracting highly excited resonances, allowing higher-energy resonances absent in H-only calculations to be identified. Furthermore, we identify resonance candidates for the $T_{cs0}(2900)$, $X(6900)$, and $X(7200)$, whereas the absence of fully-strange compact poles below 2.6 GeV challenges the interpretation of $φ(2170)$ and $X(2370)$ as $S$-wave compact $s s \bar{s} \bar{s}$ tetraquarks.

</details>


### [137] [A doubly critical point in the color-superconducting regime of the RG-consistent NJL model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27677)
*Hosein Gholami,Marco Hofmann,Michael Buballa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在具有重整化群（renormalization-group, RG）一致正规化方案的三味Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型中，系统研究了中性β平衡夸克物质的相图，发现了CFL相在不同耦合强度下的熔化模式差异，并首次在模型中明确展示了uSC相与dSC相在双重临界点交汇的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NJL模型在温度或夸克化学势接近模型截断标度时会出现截断伪影，需要采用RG一致的正规化方案来消除这些伪影。

Method: 采用RG一致正规化方案的三味NJL模型，对中性β平衡夸克物质进行系统研究；通过改变标量双夸克通道的耦合常数，在化学势-温度平面上映射出完整的相图并识别无隙区域，重点分析CFL相的熔化模式。

Result: 在较大耦合下，CFL相经由dSC相熔化，与领头阶Ginzburg-Landau分析预期一致；降低耦合后相结构显著丰富，高密度区CFL相仍经dSC相熔化，但在较低化学势区域出现uSC相，uSC与dSC两相在双重临界点（doubly critical point）交汇，该临界点的存在早已被预言但此前从未在模型中明确证实。

Conclusion: RG一致正规化方法有效去除了NJL模型的截断伪影，并揭示出低耦合条件下更为丰富的相结构，首次在具体模型中证明了uSC-dSC双重临界点的存在。

Abstract: Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) models of color-superconducting quark matter suffer from cutoff artifacts once temperature or quark chemical potential become comparable to the model cutoff. These artifacts can be removed employing a renormalization-group (RG) consistent regularization scheme. In this article we perform a systematic study of neutral beta-equilibrated quark matter within the three-flavor NJL model with RG consistent regularization. Varying the coupling constant in the scalar diquark channel, we map out the phase diagram in the plane of chemical potential and temperature, and identify the gapless domains. We particularly focus on the melting pattern of the color-flavor locked (CFL) phase. At larger couplings the CFL phase melts through a so-called $d$SC phase, as expected from leading-order Ginzburg--Landau analyses. Lowering the coupling, the phase structure becomes markedly richer: While at large densities the CFL phase still melts through a $dSC$ phase, we find a $uSC$ phase at lower chemical potential. These phases, $uSC$ and $dSC$ meet at a doubly critical point whose existence had been anticipated long ago but was never demonstrated explicitly in a model.

</details>


### [138] [Expansion attractor in classical Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28106)
*Clemens Werthmann*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将流体力学吸引子（hydrodynamic attractor）的概念扩展到不经历水动力化（hydrodynamization）却仍通过膨胀丢失记忆的系统中，发现系统经历三个阶段：早期记忆迅速丢失、中期部分恢复、晚期记忆缓慢洗牌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体力学吸引子概念的应用大多局限于重离子碰撞中的水动力化场景。为了加深对这一普遍现象的理解，需要将其推广到其他情境，以区分哪些特征是真正普遍的，而非共形Bjorken流平衡化特有的现象。

Method: 研究一个不水动力化但通过膨胀丢失记忆的系统的行为，观察其演化过程中压比$P_L/ε$随时间的变化规律。具体分析了早期、中期和晚期三个阶段中膨胀与相互作用对系统演化的相对贡献。

Result: 系统经历三个阶段：早期膨胀驱动压比$P_L/ε$以$τ^{-2}$的方式收敛至$-1$；中期相互作用主导的自由度以时间二次方增长，部分恢复记忆；晚期相互作用仅以洗牌（shuffle）方式在状态空间中重新分配信息，无显著记忆丢失或恢复。

Conclusion: 记忆丢失并不必须是水动力化的前奏，即便在不水动力化的系统中，膨胀本身也能导致记忆丢失和部分恢复，这表明吸引子现象中某些特征具有超越水动力化情境的普遍性。

Abstract: Applications of the concept of a hydrodynamic attractor have been mostly constrained to the context of hydrodynamization in heavy ion collisions. Deepening the understanding of the general phenomenon requires generalizing it to other contexts in order to see which facettes are truly universal and not a peculiarity of equilibrating conformal Bjorken flow. As part of this endeavor, this work aims to study the behaviour in a system that does not hydrodynamize, but nevertheless loses memory through expansion. We find that the system goes through three stages. At early times, expansion drives the ratio $P_L/ε$ to converge to $-1$ as $τ^{-2}$. This memory loss is partially restored at intermediate times, when interaction dominated degrees of freedom through expansion dominated evolution grow quadraticallly in time. At late times, interactions shuffle of information in state space without significant memory loss or restoration.

</details>


### [139] [Fixed points of the CKM matrix renormalization group running to all orders in perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28149)
*Brian P Dolan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了标准模型（Standard Model）中无质量1圈重整化群（renormalization group）演化下CKM矩阵六个不动点（fixed points）在全阶微扰论（perturbation theory）中仍然成立的一个证明，消除了先前证明中所需的一个额外假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前已发现标准模型中CKM矩阵的1圈重整化群演化存在六个不动点，但此前的全阶证明需要对重整化群演化形式附加额外假设。本文旨在消除该假设，给出一个更普适的证明。

Method: 作者通过严格的数学论证（具体证明步骤未在摘要中详述），逐阶检验或利用结构性质证明这六个不动点在微扰论的所有阶数下均保持为不动点，从而移除此前证明所需的额外条件。

Result: 证明了标准模型中无质量1圈CKM矩阵重整化群运行的六个不动点对全微扰阶均成立，且新证明不再依赖先前版本中的额外假设。

Conclusion: 此前关于这些不动点全阶稳定性的证明所依赖的附加假设已被消除，使该结果的适用范围更加一般化。

Abstract: A proof is given that the six fixed points of the massless 1-loop renormalization group running of the CKM matrix in the Standard Model found previously at 1-loop are fixed points to all orders in perturbation theory. A previous proof required an extra assumption on the running which is eliminated in the proof presented here.

</details>
