### [140] [A different kind of continuum limit for the three-dimensional U(1) gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27976)
*Andreas Athenodorou,Claudio Bonati,Ivan Soler Calero*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究三维紧致U(1)格点规范理论（compact U(1) lattice gauge theory）的连续极限问题，通过在Wilson作用量中引入与磁单极子总数耦合的新参数μ，找到一条保持轻胶球质量与弦张力平方根比值不变的常物理线，为该理论提供了具有物理上良好行为连续极限的正则化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维紧致U(1)格点规范理论中，色禁闭（color confinement）可以通过磁单极子（magnetic monopoles）动力学进行分析理解，但其连续极限（continuum limit）是病态的——胶球质量与弦张力（string tension）平方根之比在接近连续极限时趋于零。为克服这一病态行为，需要寻找一种改进的正则化方案。

Method: 在Wilson作用量（Wilson action）的基础上进行简单扩展，将格点磁单极子的总数与一个新参数μ耦合。通过同时调节β和μ两个参数，寻找保持轻胶球质量与弦张力平方根之比为常数的常物理线（line of constant physics），并检验其他低激发胶球质量是否满足相同的标度行为。

Result: 通过同时调节β和μ，识别出一条常物理线，沿该线轻胶球质量与弦张力平方根之比保持恒定；在数值不确定度范围内，其他低激发胶球质量也满足相同的标度关系。沿该轨迹，格点单位下的弦张力随β增大而减小。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，改进的格点作用量可能为三维紧致U(1)规范理论提供一种具有物理上良好行为连续极限的正则化（regularization），从而解决该理论原始连续极限病态的问题。

Abstract: In three-dimensional compact U(1) lattice gauge theory color confinement can be understood analytically through the dynamics of magnetic monopoles. However, its continuum limit is pathological, since the ratio of the glueball masses to the square root of the string tension vanishes as the continuum limit is approached. We investigate a simple extension of the Wilson action in which the total number of lattice monopoles is coupled to an additional parameter $μ$. By tuning $β$ and $μ$ simultaneously, we identify a line of constant physics along which the ratio of the lightest glueball mass to the square root of the string tension remains constant. We further show that the same scaling is satisfied, within numerical uncertainties, by the other low-lying glueball masses considered in this work. Along this trajectory, the string tension in lattice units decreases with increasing $β$, thus suggesting that the modified lattice action may provide a regularization of three-dimensional compact U(1) gauge theory with a physically well-behaved continuum limit.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Collinear altermagnetism for 3D chiral higher-order topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27552)
*Andreas Hadjipaschalis,Jennifer Cano*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出利用最近发现的共线交变磁性（altermagnetism）作为实现$C_4\mathcal{T}$保护手性高阶拓扑绝缘体（HOTI）的新途径，构建了三维拓扑绝缘体与Lieb晶格上d波交变磁体结合的微观模型，通过解析与数值计算证明磁性可移动并打开表面狄拉克锥（Dirac cone）的能隙，从而产生手性铰链通道，并识别了有望实现该提议的候选材料类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管高阶拓扑绝缘体（higher-order topological insulators, HOTIs）研究已取得显著进展，但受手性$C_4\mathcal{T}$对称性保护的高阶拓扑绝缘体在电子材料中迄今尚未被实现，其中$C_4\mathcal{T}$表示四重旋转与时间反演对称性的乘积。这一缺失的相类型构成了当前凝聚态物理研究中的一个重要空白，亟需寻找新的物理机制来实现该类拓扑相。

Method: 作者构建了一个微观模型，将三维拓扑绝缘体与Lieb晶格上的共线d波交变磁体（collinear $d$-wave altermagnet）相结合。通过解析与数值计算，分析磁性对表面狄拉克锥的影响及其如何产生所需的手性铰链通道，并在此基础上识别有望实现该提议的候选材料类别。

Result: 计算表明，交变磁性能够移动并打开表面狄拉克锥的能隙，从而产生所需的手性铰链通道（chiral hinge channels）。此外，作者识别出了可实现该提议的若干有前景的材料类别。

Conclusion: 研究确立了共线交变磁性作为实现本征手性高阶拓扑绝缘体的可行途径，为在真实材料中发现$C_4\mathcal{T}$保护的高阶拓扑绝缘体开辟了新的路径。

Abstract: Despite significant progress in the study of higher-order topological insulators (HOTIs), the chiral $C_4\mathcal{T}$-protected HOTI has remained elusive in electronic materials, where $C_4\mathcal{T}$ denotes the product of a four-fold rotation and time-reversal symmetry. We show that altermagnetism, a recently discovered form of collinear magnetism, provides a new route to realize this elusive phase. Specifically, we construct a microscopic model that combines a three-dimensional topological insulator with a collinear $d$-wave altermagnet on a Lieb lattice. Through analytical and numerical calculations, we show that the magnetism shifts and gaps the surface Dirac cones to produce the desired chiral hinge channels. Finally, we identify promising material classes to realise our proposal. Our results establish collinear altermagnetism as a route to intrinsic chiral higher-order topology and open a new path toward the discovery of $C_4\mathcal{T}$-protected HOTIs in real materials.

</details>


### [142] [Topological signatures in the quench dynamics of periodically driven quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27588)
*Ashutosh Dubey,Hironmoy Pratihar,Diptiman Sen,Arijit Kundu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了石墨烯（graphene）在外加圆偏振光（circularly polarized light）瞬时开启并考虑费米子热库（fermionic bath）耦合下的淬火动力学（quench dynamics）。通过扶手椅纳米带（armchair nanoribbon）计算周期平均键电流，发现孤立体系中直流电流等同于Floquet能带电流按投影占据数加权；耦合到有限强度的热库后电流趋于饱和，表明非平衡稳态（nonequilibrium steady state）的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解光驱动（光泵浦）下拓扑材料中的瞬态动力学行为，特别是淬火过程中直流响应如何反映由Floquet工程诱导的拓扑相变，以及耗散环境对该响应的影响。

Method: 采用扶手椅纳米带几何结构，在圆偏振光突然开启的淬火协议下，于连续时间序列（stroboscopic times）计算周期平均的边缘键电流；对孤立体系进行解析推导，将直流电流表达为Floquet能带电流按其投影占据数的加权和；对耦合热库体系在极弱耦合极限下利用求和规则对库化学势进行频移求和，量化跨零准能隙与Floquet区边界隙的边缘模数，并通过有限尺寸紧束缚模型的三步驱动协议计算两端电导（two-terminal conductance）加以验证。

Result: 孤立体系中边缘电流围绕一个直流值振荡，该直流值等于Floquet能带电流按投影占据数的加权，非零直流电流标志着诱导的拓扑相；耦合到有限强度热库后电流与电导先增加后饱和，指示非平衡稳态；在耦合趋于零时，经频移求和规则后周期平均电导量子化，揭示了跨零准能隙和Floquet区边界隙的边缘模数目。

Conclusion: 淬火动力学中的直流电流可作为Floquet诱导拓扑相的直接探针，弱耗散极限下周期平均电导的量子化提供了一种识别光驱动拓扑体系的边模计数方案，有限尺寸紧束缚计算与解析结果一致。

Abstract: We study the quench dynamics of graphene, without and with a staggered mass, following the sudden switch-on of circularly polarized light where coupling to a fermionic bath is also considered. Using an armchair nanoribbon, we compute the period-averaged bond current near the edge at successive stroboscopic times. For the isolated system, the current oscillates around a dc value which, we analytically show, equals the Floquet band currents weighted by their projected occupations; a nonzero dc current signals an induced topological phase. When coupled to a bath with a finite coupling, the current and conductance initially increase and then saturate, indicating a nonequilibrium steady state. In the limit of vanishingly small coupling, the period-averaged conductance becomes quantized after summing over bath chemical potentials shifted by integer multiples of the driving frequency, revealing the number of edge modes crossing the zero-quasienergy gap and the Floquet zone boundary gap. We support these results by computing the two-terminal conductance of a finite-size tight-binding model under a three-step driving protocol; the bond conductance after applying the sum rule corroborates our findings.

</details>


### [143] [Turning Zeeman splitting into switchable charge polarization in a double quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27625)
*Chi Sun,Jacob Linder*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过对称性保持的格林函数运动方程方法证明，均匀塞曼场（Zeeman field）可以强烈重构甚至反转非对称开放双量子点（double quantum dot）的单电子电荷极化，且系统始终保持在单电子电荷扇区内，建立了在固定静电失谐下通过磁场控制电荷极化的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为均匀磁场对两个量子点作用相同，不会引起电荷在它们之间移动。作者旨在打破这一直觉预期，探索均匀塞曼场能否在非对称开放双量子点中实现电荷极化的可控反转。

Method: 采用对称性保持的格林函数运动方程方法（Green's-function equation-of-motion approach），分析了非对称开放双量子点系统在均匀塞曼场作用下的单电子电荷分布，并识别出电荷占据偏好反转的参数区域。

Result: 发现两种截然不同的物理机制可实现电荷极化反转：其一，不均匀的门电平使塞曼移动的共振峰相对储层化学势（chemical potential）位置不同，产生不同的占据响应；其二，不均匀的局域相互作用通过多体添加谱（many-body addition spectra）区分两个量子点。库仑阻塞（Coulomb blockade）稳定单电子扇区，使储层介导的响应表现为空间电荷重分布而非总占据数变化。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在固定静电失谐（detuning）条件下通过磁场控制电荷极化的机制，为量子点器件中基于磁场的电荷操控提供了新的理论途径。

Abstract: A magnetic field that acts identically on two quantum dots is not expected to move charge between them. Nevertheless, we show that a uniform Zeeman field can strongly reconfigure and even reverse the single-electron charge polarization of an asymmetric open double quantum dot. Using a symmetry-preserving Green's-function equation-of-motion approach, we identify regimes where the preferred dot occupation reverses while the system remains in the single-electron charge sector. Two distinct mechanisms produce this behavior. Unequal gate levels produce different occupation responses because the Zeeman-shifted resonances lie at different positions relative to the reservoir chemical potential, whereas unequal onsite interactions distinguish the dots through their many-body addition spectra. Coulomb blockade stabilizes the single-electron sector, causing the reservoir-mediated response to appear as spatial charge redistribution rather than a change in total occupation. Our results establish a mechanism for magnetic control of charge polarization at fixed electrostatic detuning.

</details>


### [144] [Quantum transport and unified scaling law in graphene with polyadic Cantor electrostatic barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28398)
*Sudhanshu Shekhar,Mohammad Umar,Bhabani Prasad Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了石墨烯中狄拉克电子在多元康托尔结构静电势作用下的量子输运行为。利用超周期势形式（superperiodic potential formalism）获得了透射概率的闭合表达式，揭示了从超晶格共振到近透明态的谱演化规律，并建立了以双对数标度律（double-logarithmic scaling law）为核心的多参数统一标度关系，表明势场的层次自相似性（hierarchical self-similarity）主导了系统输运性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究具有分形几何结构的静电势场对石墨烯中狄拉克费米子量子输运的影响，特别是理解层级自相似性如何在输运特性中体现。

Method: 采用超周期势形式（superperiodic potential formalism）对多元康托尔结构的静电势进行建模，推导出透射概率的闭合解析表达式，并系统分析透射谱随康托尔级数（Cantor stage）、掺杂构型和入射角度的演化。

Result: 透射谱随康托尔级数增加从稀疏的超晶格共振演化为近透明态（near-transparent regime），演化速率由多元阶数决定；角向响应依赖掺杂构型（n–n–n、n–p–n 和狄拉克点情况表现出不同行为）；在近透明区透射服从关于四个独立控制参数的双对数标度律，并可组合为统一标度律。

Conclusion: 势场的层级自相似性（hierarchical self-similarity）从根本上决定此类系统的量子输运性质，统一的标度律为理解分形势构型下的电子输运提供了普适框架。

Abstract: We study the quantum transport of Dirac electrons in graphene subjected to a polyadic Cantor-structured electrostatic potential. Using the superperiodic potential formalism, we obtain a closed-form expression for the transmission probability. As the Cantor stage increases, the transmission spectrum evolves from a sparse set of superlattice resonances to a near-transparent regime, with the polyadic order setting the rate of this evolution. The angular response depends on the doping configuration, showing distinct behavior in the $n$--$n$--$n$, $n$--$p$--$n$, and Dirac-point cases. In the near-transparent regime, the transmission follows double-logarithmic scaling laws with respect to four independent control parameters: the Cantor stage, the potential height, the initiator length, and the angle of incidence. By combining these individual scaling relations, we establish a unified scaling law governing quantum transport. These results show that the hierarchical self-similarity of the potential governs the transport properties of such systems.

</details>


### [145] [Subcritical bifurcation and on-off bistability in ballistic polariton condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27823)
*Oleg I. Utesov,Soohong Choi,Pavel Kozhevin,Min Park,Daegwang Choi,Hyungdo Lee,Alexey N. Osipov,Alexey V. Yulin,Se Kwon Kim,Yong-Hoon Cho,Igor S. Aranson,Hyoungsoon Choi,Anton V. Nalitov,Sergei V. Koniakhin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续波非相干高斯光泵浦下激子-极化激元凝聚体（exciton-polariton condensate）的动力学行为，发现常规超临界斯图尔特-兰道（Stuart-Landau）图像在参数空间某些区域内失效，强极化激元排斥与较小泵浦光斑条件下由五次斯图尔特-兰道方程描述，表现为亚临界分岔、双稳性及一位记忆效应，并给出实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超临界斯图尔特-兰道模型在描述激子-极化激元凝聚体动力学时存在参数域局限性，需要发展更精确的理论框架以解释和预测强排斥与大光斑限制下的非线性行为。

Method: 采用理论分析结合实验验证的方法，通过考虑储层（reservoir）引起的强极化激元排斥相互作用，对连续波非相干高斯光泵浦下的凝聚体动力学进行摄动分析，推导出五次斯图尔特-兰道方程并考察其分岔结构。

Result: 在强排斥与相对较小泵浦光斑条件下，动力学由五次斯图尔特-兰道方程描述，亚临界音叉分岔（subcritical pitchfork bifurcation）导致凝聚体形成，并在有限泵浦功率范围内出现平凡态与非平凡态之间的双稳性及一位记忆效应；进一步增大排斥参数或缩小光斑则导致摄动方法失效并出现具有复杂动力学的自陷（self-trapping）行为。

Conclusion: 研究结果可为设计利用所预测记忆效应的极化激元器件提供理论指导，并通过实验证据支持了所提出行为的产生。

Abstract: Dynamics of exciton-polariton condensates under continuous-wave incoherent Gaussian optical pumping is considered. It is shown that the conventional supercritical Stuart-Landau picture is invalid in a certain domain of the parameter space. For strong polariton repulsion from the reservoir and relatively small pump spots, the dynamics is adequately described by the quintic Stuart-Landau equation. The corresponding subcritical pitchfork bifurcation leads to condensate formation, accompanied by bistability between the trivial and nontrivial states over a finite pump-power range and a one-bit memory. Further increase of the repulsion parameter or decrease of the spot size breaks down the perturbative approach and leads to a peculiar self-trapping regime with complex dynamics. Experimental evidence of the emergence of the proposed behavior is provided. Our findings can be used to design polaritonic setups that exploit the predicted memory effect.

</details>


### [146] [Quantum geometric bounds at finite temperature for one-dimensional chiral systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27944)
*Peng He,Hai-Tao Ding,Yu-Guo Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在 Uhlmann 混合态框架下，针对一维手性对称系统建立了有限温度下量子态几何量的严格下界：Bures 长度受连续几何相位角约束，并推导出随温度变化的插值界限，在零温与高温极限之间过渡。作者通过 SSH 模型和无自旋 Kitaev 链模型进行了解析与数值验证，并讨论了利用量子虚时演化（QITE）方法在量子电路中探测密度矩阵几何的潜在途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零温下量子态的几何与拓扑通过精确界密切相关（几何量被拓扑不变量从下方约束），但有限温度下的类似关系仍不清楚。作者旨在填补这一空白，在混合态框架下建立有限温度的几何下界。

Method: 作者在 Uhlmann 框架下分析一维手性对称系统在有限温度下的混合态几何，证明 Bures 长度受连续几何相位角的下界约束，并推导出依赖温度的插值界限。理论结果通过 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）模型和无自旋 Kitaev 链模型进行解析与数值验证，并讨论了基于量子虚时演化（QITE）方法的量子电路探测方案。

Result: 作者建立了有限温度下 Bures 长度与连续几何相位角之间的严格下界关系，并获得了在零温极限和高温平庸极限之间插值的温度依赖界。上述结果在 SSH 模型和 Kitaev 链模型中均得到解析与数值验证。

Conclusion: 该工作将零温下几何与拓扑的联系推广到了有限温度混合态情形，为一维手性对称系统提供了严格的有限温度几何下界，并提出了在量子电路中通过 QITE 方法探测密度矩阵几何的可行路径。

Abstract: The geometry and topology of quantum states are intimately related at zero temperature through exact bounds that constrain geometric quantities from below by topological invariants. At finite-temperature, however, the analogous relations remain unclear. Here we establish rigorous geometric lower bounds for one-dimensional (1D) chiral-symmetric systems at finite temperature within the Uhlmann's framework for mixed states. We show that the Bures length is bounded by a continuous geometric phase angle. We further derive a temperature-dependent bound that interpolates between the zero-temperature limit and a trivial high-temperature regime. Our results are verified analytically and numerically using the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model and the spinless Kitaev chain model. Finally, we discuss potential ways to detect the geometry of the density matrix in quantum circuits, with the quantum imaginary time evolution (QITE) method.

</details>


### [147] [Multi-dimensional spectroscopy of mobile excitons in two-dimensional semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27951)
*Ned Wheaton,Jeffrey A. Davis,Jesper Levinsen,Meera M. Parish*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个微观理论框架用于解释二维半导体中激子（exciton）在多维相干光谱（MDCS）中的行为，该理论考虑了激子的离域特性与连续动量自由度，能精确再现掺杂单层MoSe₂的实验光谱，并揭示了激子极化子（exciton polaron）间的相互作用受相空间填充效应（phase-space filling）强烈调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准线性响应探针不足以揭示准粒子间的关联，而此前建模MDCS的少能级唯象方法无法捕捉二维半导体中激子的离域本质，因此需要建立微观理论以准确描述激子动量连续谱及其在光谱中的效应。

Method: 构建了一种考虑可移动激子（具有连续动量自由度）的MDCS微观理论，与通常采用的唯象少能级模型不同，该理论自然包含了由激子动量能连续带（energy continuum）所带来的相互作用诱导退相干，并可用于刻画电荷掺杂半导体中不同激子-极化子准粒子间的干涉。

Result: 计算得到的MDCS光谱与Hao等人（Nature Communications 2017）在掺杂单层MoSe₂上的实验数据吻合良好；结果表明激子极化子之间的相互作用强烈依赖于相空间填充效应，即激子极化子会竞争有限的电子自由度。

Conclusion: 微观理论方法能够从二维半导体MDCS光谱的精细结构中提取新的物理洞察，并展示了微观方法在更普遍意义上建模MDCS实验的实用价值。

Abstract: Multi-dimensional coherent spectroscopy (MDCS) goes beyond standard linear-response probes and provides a powerful tool for investigating correlations between quasiparticles such as excitons (bound electron-hole pairs). Here we present a microscopic theory of MDCS that accounts for the delocalized nature of excitons in two-dimensional semiconductors. In contrast to the more phenomenological few-level approaches typically employed for modelling MDCS, our theory features mobile excitons with continuous momentum degrees of freedom. We find that the energy continuum associated with exciton momenta is crucial for producing interaction-induced decoherence, as well as capturing the interference between different exciton-polaron quasiparticles in the case of charge-doped semiconductors. Crucially, our calculated MDCS spectra agree well with recent experiments on doped monolayer MoSe$_2$ [Hao et al., Nature Communications 8, 15552 (2017)], and they suggest that the interactions between exciton polarons depend strongly on phase-space filling effects, where exciton polarons compete for electrons. Our results demonstrate that microscopic approaches allow one to gain new insights from the fine structure of MDCS on two-dimensional semiconductors, and they illustrate the utility of microscopic approaches to modelling MDCS experiments more generally.

</details>


### [148] [Significant modulation of acoustoelectric current associated with charge density wave transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27959)
*Natsumi Nikaido,Takuya Kawada,Koji Fujiwara,Jihoon Park,Nan Jiang,Kouta Kondou,Shintaro Takada,Yasuhiro Niimi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了发生电荷密度波（charge density wave, CDW）跃迁的材料中，由压电衬底上的表面声波（surface acoustic wave, SAW）诱导的声电（acoustoelectric, AE）电流，发现其极性和大小可被CDW跃迁调制，并建立了应变修正电导模型加以解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索CDW跃迁材料中SAW与电子相互作用的新物理机制，并评估声电效应作为探测范德华（van der Waals）材料中SAW-电子相互作用工具的可能性。

Method: 在压电衬底上通过SAW诱导AE电流，并测量NbSe3和2H-TaSe2在CDW跃迁前后的AE电流极性与大小变化；同时考察SAW传播方向与衬底晶轴相对取向对AE电流符号的影响，并提出一个基于应变修正电导率的唯象模型进行解释。

Result: NbSe3和2H-TaSe2的AE电流极性和大小随CDW跃迁而发生显著调制；AE电流的符号依赖于SAW传播方向与衬底晶轴间的相对取向；唯象模型能够定性解释上述两种效应。

Conclusion: 声电效应为研究范德华材料中的SAW-电子相互作用提供了有力探测手段，并对应变电子学（straintronics）这一新兴领域具有潜在推动作用。

Abstract: We studied acoustoelectric (AE) currents in materials that undergo charge density wave (CDW) transitions, induced by a surface acoustic wave (SAW) on a piezoelectric substrate. The polarity and magnitude of the AE current in NbSe$_3$ and 2H-TaSe$_2$ were modulated due to their CDW transitions. We also found that the sign of the AE current depends on the SAW propagation direction with respect to the crystalline axis of the substrate.A phenomenological model assuming strain-modified conductivity can qualitatively account for the significant modulation of the AE current associated with the CDW transition, as well as the SAW propagation orientation dependence. The present results offer a powerful probe for exploring SAW-electron interactions in van der Waals materials, thereby highlighting their potential for advancing the emerging field of straintronics.

</details>


### [149] [Topological signatures in the curvature-induced energy response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28146)
*Jaehyeok Lee,Iuegyun Hong,Jinhong Park*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本论文通过非相对论Haldane模型的微观晶格形变表述，研究了引力场诱导的拓扑能量响应：第一阶能量响应是非普适的且依赖形变的键分辨结构，而第三阶响应的非连续性具有普适性，与相对论引力Chern–Simons预测定量一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论有效场论预测引力场会产生出现在空间梯度三阶的拓扑能量响应，但这一响应如何在非相对论晶格模型中具体涌现尚不清楚，需要微观层面的验证。

Method: 采用Haldane模型的微观晶格表述来构建曲率诱导的形变，分析曲率下的能量响应，并比较不同键分辨（bond-resolved）形变结构下的各阶响应行为。

Result: 第一阶能量响应非普适，依赖形变的键分辨结构，且在三个最近邻跃迁对称调制时为零；第三阶响应在拓扑转变处出现非连续性，其大小与相对论引力Chern–Simons预测一致。

Conclusion: 尽管绝对能量响应非普适，其第三阶非连续性是普适的，保留了超越相对论极限的特征性拓扑指纹。

Abstract: Relativistic effective field theory predicts a topological energy response to a gravitational field that appears at third order in spatial gradients. Here, we investigate how this response emerges in the nonrelativistic Haldane model using a microscopic lattice formulation of curvature-induced deformations. We find that the leading first-order energy response is nonuniversal and depends on the bond-resolved structure of the deformation; in particular, it vanishes for a symmetric modulation of the three nearest-neighbor hoppings. In contrast, the third-order response exhibits a discontinuity across the topological transition whose magnitude agrees with the relativistic gravitational Chern--Simons prediction. Thus, although the absolute response is nonuniversal, its third-order discontinuity is universal and retains a characteristic topological fingerprint beyond the relativistic limit.

</details>


### [150] [Quantum Geometric Origin of Nonlinear Current Induced Orbital Magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28231)
*Xue-Jin Zhang,Yue-Xin Huang,Wei Du,Xiaolong Feng,Shen Lai,Cong Xiao,Qian Niu,Shengyuan A. Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文发展了非线性电流诱导轨道磁化（nonlinear current-induced orbital magnetization）的完整理论，基于布洛赫电子场修正轨道磁矩的微观推导，揭示了量子几何来源为反常轨道极化率（anomalous orbital polarizability）与Berry联络极化率（Berry-connection polarizability）的竞争，并通过第一性原理计算预测该效应在应变双层石墨烯、单层1T' MoS₂和MoTe₂中显著且实验可测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电流诱导磁化是凝聚态物理研究的焦点，且已推进到非线性领域。然而，由于轨道磁性的非局域性质，如何正确构建非线性电流诱导轨道磁化的理论表征仍是一个根本性挑战。

Method: 论文基于布洛赫电子场修正轨道磁矩（field-corrected orbital magnetic moment）的微观推导，构建了非线性电流诱导轨道磁化的完整理论框架，并与第一性原理计算相结合进行预测。

Result: 理论表明该现象的量子几何根源在于反常轨道极化率与Berry联络极化率的竞争效应。第一性原理预测在应变双层石墨烯、单层1T' MoS₂和MoTe₂中产生显著且实验可观测的非线性轨道磁化。值得注意的是，在具有拓扑能带特征的材料中，非线性轨道磁化可超过其对应的自旋磁化，且与自旋轨道耦合强度无关。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了现有理论中布洛赫电子场修正轨道磁矩这一关键缺失环节，为非线性电流诱导磁化的理解提供了完整理论基础，并揭示了拓扑能带材料中轨道磁化相对于自旋磁化的主导地位。

Abstract: Electric generation of magnetization is a focus of condensed matter research, and has recently been advanced into the nonlinear regime. However, due to the nonlocal nature of orbital magnetism, how to properly formulate nonlinear current-induced orbital magnetization remains a fundamental challenge. Here, we develop the proper theory for this effect. This is based on the microscopic derivation of field-corrected orbital magnetic moment of a Bloch electron, a critical missing piece in the present theory. We show that the quantum geometric origin of this phenomenon lies in both the anomalous orbital polarizability and the Berry-connection polarizability, which often provide competing contributions. Combining our theory with first-principles calculations, we predict significant, experimentally accessible nonlinear orbital magnetization generated in strained bilayer graphene, monolayer 1T' $\mathrm{MoS_2}$ and $\mathrm{MoTe_2}$. Remarkably, nonlinear orbital magnetization can dominate over its spin counterpart in materials with topological band features, irrespective of the spin-orbit coupling strength.

</details>


### [151] [Rapid Charge Stability Diagram Generation from Device-level Modeling of Semiconductor Quantum Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28263)
*Ron Nodel,David W. Kanaar,Connor Nasseraddin,Tim J. Wilson,Hong-Wen Jiang,Jason R. Petta,Chris Anderson,Mark F. Gyure*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合多域多模型（MDMM）方法与自动调谐例程的高效建模流程，通过少量自洽薛定谔-泊松（Schrödinger-Poisson）计算与全组态相互作用（FCI）能量计算，直接参数化Hubbard模型，从而在极低计算成本下生成电荷稳定性图（charge stability diagram），并在Intel Tunnel Falls Si/SiGe器件上验证了其与实验数据的定性一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于全自洽薛定谔-泊松计算的电荷稳定性图刻画在计算上非常昂贵，难以用于多器件快速预测与设计优化，亟需一种低成本且能保持物理准确性的替代建模方法。

Method: 采用多域多模型（MDMM）方法结合自动调谐例程，仅对选定的电荷构型获取门电压；再利用全组态相互作用（FCI）能量计算提取充电能量（charging energy）、杠杆臂（lever arm）和点间库仑相互作用参数，直接构建Hubbard模型以快速生成电荷稳定性图。

Result: 在Intel Tunnel Falls Si/SiGe器件中，Hubbard模型以远低于电压偏置扫描的计算成本复现电荷稳定性图，并与实验数据达到定性一致；该工作流还被成功应用于另一器件的杠杆臂工程，表明其可扩展至多种架构。

Conclusion: 该工作代表了向半导体量子点器件可预测数字孪生（digital twin）模型迈出的重要一步，为量子点器件的设计与表征提供了一种低成本、可扩展的计算框架。

Abstract: Self-consistent Schrödinger-Poisson calculations are a powerful tool for predicting the behavior of layered semiconductor quantum dot devices. However, characterization of charge stability diagrams through fully simulated gate-voltage sweeps is computationally expensive. Combining a Multi-Domain Multi-Model (MDMM) approach with an automated tuning routine, we identify gate voltages associated with selected charge configurations. This small set of self-consistent simulations can be augmented with Full Configuration Interaction (FCI) energy calculations to extract charging energies, lever arms, and interdot Coulomb interactions to directly parameterize a Hubbard model for rapid charge stability diagram generation. For an Intel Tunnel Falls Si/SiGe device, we demonstrate the Hubbard model's ability to reproduce charge stability diagrams at a fraction of the computational cost in comparison to voltage bias sweeps. We further compare the simulated diagrams to experimental data and demonstrate qualitative agreement. Our result represents a step towards predictive digital twin models for semiconductor quantum dot devices. Finally, we apply this workflow towards lever arm engineering in a second device, demonstrating that the method extends to multiple architectures.

</details>


### [152] [Evidence for Three-component Interlayer Coherent Exciton Condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28338)
*Subi Du,Xiaohan Zhang,Hongxi Song,Siyu Fan,Yiduo Wang,Zhenyu Wang,Song Liu,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jiangping Hu,Yang Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在双层石墨烯（triple-layer graphene）体系中报道了三组分层间相干激子凝聚（three-component interlayer coherent exciton condensation）的实验证据。利用相邻WSe2单层中的里德堡激子（Rydberg excitons）作为层敏感光学探针，研究者解析出所有三对层组合的相互作用诱导不可压缩态，并在三层朗道能级趋于简并时观察到单一三组分相干态的涌现，为更高分量的量子霍尔序（quantum Hall order）探索提供了可编程平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统（quantum many-body system）内部组分数目的增加可以承载更简单的体系无法实现的新奇集体有序态。尽管量子霍尔双层体系是实现层间激子凝聚（interlayer exciton condensation）的经典平台，但将这种相干性扩展到三个独立可寻址的电子流体之前仍未被实现。

Method: 研究采用三双层石墨烯体系与相邻WSe2单层的里德堡激子作为层敏感的（layer-sensitive）光学探针，通过解析零朗道能级交叉（zeroth-Landau-level crossings）处的相互作用诱导不可压缩性，检测所有三对层组合中的激子凝聚通道。同时独立调控位移场（displacement field）和层间偏压（interlayer bias），实现三层朗道能级（Landau levels）的连续可调与接近同时简并。

Result: 实验在单个器件中分别建立了顶-中、中-底和顶-底的成对激子凝聚通道，并在三层朗道能级趋近于同时简并的收敛区域中，不可压缩性持续存在，同时激子能量和谱权重在成对极限之间平滑演化，表明三个层在单一三组分态中相干参与。

Conclusion: 独立调控层电势与工程化层间相互作用的能力使多层石墨烯成为探索更高分量量子霍尔序和模拟强关联量子物质的可编程合成维度（synthetic dimension），拓展了激子凝聚现象向多组分复杂体系的推广。

Abstract: Increasing the number of internal components in a quantum many-body system can host collective orders inaccessible to simpler settings. Quantum Hall bilayers provide a canonical realization of interlayer exciton condensation, yet extending such coherence across three independently addressable electronic fluids has remained elusive. Here we report evidence for three-component interlayer coherent exciton condensation in triple-layer graphene system. Using Rydberg excitons in an adjacent WSe2 monolayer as a layer-sensitive optical probe, we resolve interaction-induced incompressibility at zeroth-Landau-level crossings for all three pairwise layer combinations, establishing top-middle, middle-bottom and top-bottom exciton condensate channels within the same device. Independent control of displacement field and interlayer bias continuously tunes these pairwise states towards a regime where Landau levels from all three layers approach simultaneous degeneracy. At their convergence, incompressibility persists while the exciton energy and spectral weight evolve smoothly between the pairwise limits, suggesting coherent participation of all three layers in a single three-component state. More broadly, the ability to independently control layer potentials and engineer interlayer interactions establishes multilayer graphene as a programmable synthetic dimension for exploring higher-component quantum Hall order and simulating strongly correlated quantum matter.

</details>


### [153] [Crystal-phase quantum dots in AlGaAs nanowires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28353)
*Rohan Radhakrishnan,Rodion Reznik,Gilles Patriarche,Lorenzo Leandro,Igor Ilkiv,Anna Andreeva,Artem Khrebtov,George Cirlin,Nika Akopian*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究首次在AlGaAs纳米线中实现晶相量子点（crystal-phase quantum dots, CPQDs），获得线宽仅为61微电子伏特（μeV）的明亮单光子发射，光学质量远超典型的晶相量子点，归因于I型（type-I）能带排列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶相量子点是目前唯一能以单原子层精度制备量子点阵列的平台，但其此前仅在二元半导体（如InP和GaAs）中被研究，发射线宽通常在毫电子伏特（meV）量级，限制了其光子量子技术潜力。

Method: 在AlGaAs纳米线中首次制备晶相量子点，并通过测量单光子发射线宽、背景发射、激子寿命（exciton lifetime）、激子精细结构劈裂（fine-structure splitting）和塞曼劈裂（Zeeman splitting）来表征其光学性质与能带排列类型。

Result: 实现了线宽低至61 μeV的明亮单光子发射，背景发射低；激子寿命为1纳秒（ns），显著短于典型的II型（type-II）晶相量子点，同时观察到了标准I型自组装量子点中常见的激子精细结构劈裂和塞曼劈裂现象。

Conclusion: AlGaAs晶相量子点展现出远优于典型晶相量子点的光学质量，性能源自I型能带排列，为可扩展的量子点光子量子技术提供了新的材料平台。

Abstract: Crystal-phase quantum dots (CPQDs)$\unicode{x2014}$quantum dots in nanowires defined by crystal structure rather than material composition$\unicode{x2014}$constitute the only platform capable of fabricating quantum-dot arrays with the ultimate precision of a single atomic layer. This intrinsic control yields perfectly aligned quantum dots with atomically sharp interfaces, providing a unique pathway toward scalable quantum-dot-based photonic quantum technologies. To date, CPQDs have been studied primarily in binary semiconductors, such as InP and GaAs, where their emission linewidths are typically in the meV range, thereby limiting their technological potential. Here, we report, for the first time, CPQDs in AlGaAs nanowires and show bright single-photon emission with linewidths as narrow as 61 $μeV$ and low background emission, demonstrating optical quality well beyond typical CPQDs. We attribute this performance to a type-I band alignment, as suggested by an exciton lifetime of 1 ns, significantly shorter than that typically observed in type-II CPQDs. Additionally, we observe an exciton fine-structure splitting and a Zeeman splitting, as commonly observed in standard type-I self-assembled quantum dots.

</details>


### [154] [Electrically Tunable Two-Component Exciton Condensate in a Coulomb-Coupled Graphene Trilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28381)
*Bo Zou,A. Okounkova,Shuaiqing Zhang,Jian Liao,J. Pack,K. Watanabe,T. Taniguchi,Yihang Zeng*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究在双层间隧穿可忽略的三层石墨烯中，通过静电门控连续调控各层载流子密度，实验和理论共同证实了一种具有可连续调控内部结构的两分量激子凝聚体（two-component exciton condensate）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组分凝聚体拥有单组分凝聚体所不具备的内部相位自由度，但凝聚体中各组分的调控在固体材料中通常难以实现，因此需要在固体体系中实现并调控多组分凝聚体。

Method: 实验中采用层间隧穿可忽略的三层石墨烯，通过静电门控（electrostatic gating）连续调节各层载流子密度，并利用量子电容（quantum-capacitance）测量表征电荷不可压缩的量子霍尔态；理论上使用静态Hartree-Fock和含时Hartree-Fock计算分析多层的相位刚度本征模和Goldstone模式。

Result: 总填充因子1和2的电荷不可压缩量子霍尔态在全部层填充构型范围内持续存在，并连续连接三个双层激子凝聚极限，表明三层激子态的存在。计算显示，当三层均部分填充时存在两个独立的有限相位刚度本征模和两个线性色散的Goldstone模；当一层未被填充时仅剩一个本征模和一个Goldstone模。刚度本征模随层间电荷转移在相邻层激子基之间连续旋转。

Conclusion: 实验与理论结果共同支持识别出一种两分量激子凝聚体，其内部结构可通过电场连续调控，为实现固体中可电控的多组分凝聚体提供了新途径。

Abstract: Multicomponent condensates possess internal phase degrees of freedom unavailable to a single-component condensate, yet their components are rarely controllable in solids. Here we realize a graphene trilayer with negligible interlayer tunnelling in which the layer-specific carrier densities are continuously tuned by electrostatic gating. Quantum-capacitance measurements demonstrate that charge-incompressible quantum Hall states at total filling factors 1 and 2 persist across the full range of layer-filling configurations and continuously connect the three bilayer exciton-condensate limits. This persistence provides evidence for a trilayer excitonic state. Static Hartree-Fock and time-dependent Hartree-Fock calculations yield two independent finite phase-stiffness eigenmodes and two linearly dispersing Goldstone modes, respectively, when all three layers are partially filled, whereas only one phase-stiffness eigenmode and one linear Goldstone mode remain when one layer is unfilled. The stiffness eigenmodes rotate continuously between the two adjacent-layer exciton bases as charge is transferred among the layers, revealing electrical control of the condensate-mode composition. Together, the experimental and theoretical results support the identification of a two-component exciton condensate with a continuously tunable internal structure.

</details>


### [155] [ScatterWorks: A Python package for building and solving scattering network models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28479)
*R. Johanna Zijderveld,Hélène Spring,Anton R. Akhmerov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本论文介绍 ScatterWorks，一个用于构建和求解网络模型（network models）的开源 Python 包，可研究无序和拓扑系统中的非相互作用输运和波传播现象。该包通过紧凑的网络表示和可组合的变换操作支持周期性及有限网络的灵活构建，并演示了其在 Fabry-Perot 干涉仪和 Chalker-Coddington 模型上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络模型是研究无序和拓扑系统中非相互作用输运和波传播现象的高效框架，但缺乏一个灵活、开源且便于扩展的数值工具来构建和求解这些模型。

Method: 引入 ScatterWorks 包，采用紧凑网络表示，支持平铺（tile）、切割（cut）、重连（relink）和并集（union）等可组合变换操作以灵活构建网络；节点上的局部散射矩阵（scattering matrix）可通过节点标签或显式索引赋值，组装为全局散射方程后用于计算准能量（quasienergy）和输运可观测量；支持稀疏散射矩阵并采用 Schur 求解器评估输运观测量。

Result: 在符号化的 Fabry-Perot 干涉仪上恢复了解析表达式，在数值化的 Chalker-Coddington 模型上恢复了量子霍尔相变预期的近临界输运行为；内置的标签感知绘图工具简化了大型自定义网络几何的调试和可复现搭建过程。

Conclusion: ScatterWorks 提供了一套完整且灵活的开放源码工作流，可高效构建和求解网络模型，为研究复杂无序及拓扑系统中的输运与波传播现象提供有力工具支持。

Abstract: Network models provide an efficient framework for studying non-interacting transport and wave-propagation phenomena in disordered and topological systems. We introduce ScatterWorks, an open-source Python package for building and solving network models. The package uses a compact network representation with composable transformation operations (tile, cut, relink, and union) to enable flexible construction of periodic and finite networks. Local scattering matrices on nodes can be assigned by node labels or explicit indexing, assembled into global scattering equations, and used to compute quasienergies and transport observables. The package supports sparse scattering matrices, allowing transport observables to be evaluated with Schur-based solvers. We demonstrate the package workflow on a symbolic Fabry-Perot interferometer and a numerical Chalker-Coddington model, recovering analytical expressions and the expected near-critical transport behavior of the quantum Hall transition. Plotting utilities with label-aware rendering are included to streamline debugging and reproducible setup for larger, custom network geometries.

</details>


### [156] [Super-resolution Control of Two-dimensional Quantum Emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28574)
*Bosai Lyu,Valeria Vento,Ludivine Fausten,Daniel Suarez-Forero,Klevis Domi,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Alberto Morpurgo,Iaroslav Gaponenko,Patrycja Paruch,Ajit Srivastava*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究将低温光学光谱与扫描探针显微镜相结合，在WSe₂/MoSe₂双层异质结中实现了对局域层间激子的超分辨定位与确定性电荷态控制，并展示了通过尖端调节电磁真空来调控单个发射体的辐射发射，为研究半导体分数化陈绝缘体中的任意子动力学提供了多点点电荷传感平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体过渡金属二硫属化物（transition-metal dichalcogenide）异质双层中的局域层间激子（interlayer exciton）是具有静态电偶极矩的量子发射体，可用作纳米尺度电荷传感器以探测邻近层中的关联量子相。然而，受亚波长约束的限制，此类发射体的确定性空间控制仍面临挑战。

Method: 本平台将低温光学光谱（cryogenic optical spectroscopy）与扫描探针显微镜（scanning probe microscopy）结合，用于研究WSe₂/MoSe₂双层中的束缚层间激子。利用基于原子力显微镜（AFM）的局域斯塔克位移（local Stark shift）技术，实现对相距仅数十纳米发射体的超分辨定位，以及对单个电荷态（包括三子/trion形成）的确定性控制。

Result: 实现了相隔仅几十纳米的发射体的超分辨定位，并展示了对单个电荷态的确定性控制，包括三子的形成。时间分辨测量表明，针尖能够诱导单个发射体周围电磁真空的修正，从而控制其辐射发射（radiative emission）。

Conclusion: 该多点点电荷传感平台具备光学读出能力，特别适用于研究半导体分数化陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator, FCI）中的分数化现象和任意子（anyon）动力学，为深入理解关联量子相提供了新的实验途径。

Abstract: Localized interlayer excitons in semiconducting transition-metal dichalcogenide heterobilayers are quantum emitters with a static electric dipole moment, making them excellent nanoscale charge sensors to probe correlated quantum phases in a proximal layer. These emitters are electrically tunable and inherit spin-valley selection rules, yet their deterministic spatial control remains challenging due to subwavelength confinement. Here, we present a platform that combines cryogenic optical spectroscopy with scanning probe microscopy to investigate trapped interlayer excitons in WSe$_2$/MoSe$_2$ bilayers. By exploiting AFM-based local Stark shift, we achieve super-resolution localization of emitters separated by only a few tens of nanometers and demonstrate deterministic control of individual charge states, including trion formation, opening a path towards coherent inter-dot coupling. Time-resolved measurements reveal tip-induced modification of the electromagnetic vacuum around individual emitters, thus controlling their radiative emission. Our multi-point charge sensing platform with optical readout is particularly well-suited to study fractionalization and anyon dynamics in semiconducting FCIs.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Quantum speed limit time of a uniformly accelerated Unruh-DeWitt detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27478)
*Debasish Ghosh,Bibhas Ranjan Majhi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在开放量子系统（open quantum system）框架下，利用马尔可夫过程（Markovian process）研究了一致加速的Unruh-DeWitt探测器与无质量实标量场相互作用时的量子速度极限时间（quantum speed limit time, QSLT）。研究发现，QSLT在特定加速度值附近出现显著行为变化，且该行为可用于诊断Unruh效应（Unruh effect）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Unruh效应是量子场论与相对论交汇处的重要预言，但至今缺乏直接的实验验证。作者试图寻找一种新的、可观测的诊断工具——量子速度极限时间——来识别和表征Unruh效应。

Method: 采用开放量子系统形式主义，基于马尔可夫过程框架，研究一致加速的Unruh-DeWitt探测器与无质量实标量场的相互作用。考察三种具体场景：场位于闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）且（i）无反射边界但处于有限温度，（ii）存在单个边界且探测器沿平行方向加速，（iii）存在双边界且探测器在边界之间平行运动。

Result: 第一场景中，QSLT在特定加速度值附近发生明显变化，且该临界加速度随场温度升高而降低。另外两种场景中，QSLT在超过临界加速度值后开始波动，且增大加速度会使波动更加剧烈；双边界情形所需临界加速度远小于单边界情形。在对边界间距和探测器频率取特定值时，双边界情形的临界加速度可低至约10^5 m/s²。

Conclusion: QSLT在不同边界条件下的独特行为可以被用作一种有效的诊断工具，以探测和表征Unruh效应。

Abstract: Within the open quantum system formalism, using the Markovian process, we investigate the {\it quantum speed limit time} (QSLT) of a uniformly accelerated Unruh-DeWitt detector, interacting with the massless real scaler field. Three specific scenarios are being studied -- the field is in Minkowski spacetime (i) without any reflecting boundary but at a finite temperature, (ii) presence of single boundary with the detector is accelerating parallel to the plane of it, and (iii) presence of two boundaries with the detector is moving between them along the parallel direction. Interestingly, the behavior of QSLT appears to be very distinctive around a particular value of $a$. For the first scenario QSLT shows very noticeable change around a specific $a$ and the required value of $a$ corresponding to this change decreases with the increase of field temperature. In the other two scenarios, QSLT starts to fluctuate after a critical value of $a$. If we further increase $a$, more vigorous fluctuation emerges. The value of $a$ required for the starting of the oscillation is much less for the double boundary case compared to the single boundary. Taking into account specific values of the distance between the boundaries and the detector's frequency, we estimate that the critical value of $a$ can be as low as $\sim 10^5 \text{m}/\text{s}^2$ for the double boundary situation. We argue that such a distinctive feature in QSLT can be a fruitful tool to diagnose the Unruh effect.

</details>


### [158] [Creation of an inflationary epoch from an Emergent Universe through quantum tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27597)
*Eduardo I. Guendelman,Ramon Herrera,Pedro Labraña*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出在两种测度理论（Two Measures Theories, TMT）框架下，极早期宇宙可由静态且经典稳定的爱因斯坦宇宙（Einstein Universe）描述。通过量子隧穿效应，宇宙从该静态解跃迁进入超暴胀（superinflation）阶段，并最终过渡到慢滚暴胀（slow-roll inflation）阶段。隧穿概率因势垒的对数贡献呈幂律依赖而非指数依赖，且该过程不涉及时空本身的量子创生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准大爆炸宇宙学模型在奇点处失效，无法描述极早期宇宙的起源。Emergent Universe方案通过引入无奇点的静态爱因斯坦宇宙作为初始几何来回避这一问题，而TMT框架为构建此类静态且经典稳定的解提供了理论可能性。

Method: 在自发破缺尺度不变性的TMT框架下引入膨胀子（dilaton）场，并在爱因斯坦框架下研究其有效势。利用膨胀子对应的守恒量，将宇宙学演化重构为以有效势V(φ̇)描述的形式，该势呈现一个发散势垒。对微超空间（minisuperspace）哈密顿量进行量子化，并采用WKB近似计算隧穿概率，通过固定平方根分支来保证隧穿概率的半经典解释一致性。

Result: 隧穿作用量在选定平方根分支下是有限且良定义的。势垒对隧穿指数产生对数贡献，使隧穿概率呈幂律（power-law）而非通常指数依赖，且在所考虑的参数值下隧穿概率接近1。宇宙以有限尺度因子进入超暴胀阶段，随后过渡到慢滚暴胀。

Conclusion: 该量子创生过程产生了一个暴胀宇宙，但并非时空本身的量子创生——时空在隧穿之前就已存在。此机制为大爆炸奇点问题提供了一种替代方案，同时保持可观测的暴胀演化。

Abstract: We consider that the very early Universe was well described by an Emergent Universe, whose geometry was that of a static and classically stable Einstein Universe, which is possible in certain Two Measures Theories (TMT). These solutions do not have a big bang, but rather they extend to arbitrarily early times. The TMT we consider are theories with spontaneously broken scale invariance and contain a dilaton field, crucial for the implementation of the scale invariance, that after the symmetry breaking acquires a nontrivial effective potential, which we study in the Einstein frame. Using the conserved quantity associated with the dilaton, we recast the cosmological evolution in terms of an effective potential $V(\dotφ)$, which exhibits a divergent barrier separating the static solution from the subsequent expanding evolution. Quantizing the corresponding minisuperspace Hamiltonian, we evaluate the tunneling probability in the WKB approximation. The tunneling action is well defined and finite once the branch of the square root is fixed by requiring a consistent semiclassical interpretation of the tunneling probability. We find that the barrier gives rise to a logarithmic contribution to the tunneling exponent, yielding a power-law rather than the usual exponential dependence of the tunneling probability, which for the parameter values considered is close to unity. The Universe thus emerges with a finite scale factor into a superinflationary phase that develops into slow-roll inflation. This quantum creation of an inflationary Universe does not represent a quantum creation of spacetime, which exists before the tunneling.

</details>


### [159] [Thermodynamic Criticality in FLRW Cosmology with Non-extensive Loop Quantum Gravity Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27721)
*Miguel Cruz,Joel Saavedra,Juan Rodriguez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在具有非广延圈量子引力（非广延Loop Quantum Gravity）启发熵的Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker（FLRW）宇宙视界上，基于完整的Kodama-Hayward温度和统一第一定律（unified first law），推导出修正的Friedmann动力学和视界状态方程，建立了一个自洽的热力学临界结构，并证明了其属于标准平均场（mean-field）普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在研究非广延圈量子引力启发熵在宇宙学表观视界上诱导的热力学临界性，探索该修正熵形式下是否存在有限物理临界点，以及其临界行为是否偏离标准平均场普适类。此为在量子引力修正热力学框架下检验宇宙视界相变结构的重要理论问题。

Method: 论文在空间平坦的FLRW宇宙表观视界上引入非广延圈量子引力启发熵，使用完整的Kodama-Hayward温度和统一第一定律推导修正的Friedmann动力学方程，并构造相应的视界状态方程（horizon equation of state）。随后通过分析旋节线（spinodal curve）、热容、等温压缩率、Gibbs自由能和Ruppeiner曲率等热力学量来刻画临界结构，并计算临界指数（critical exponents）以确定普适类。

Result: 研究发现有限物理临界点仅出现在非广延分支q>1中，而Bekenstein-Hawking极限q→1下临界点被推至v_c→∞和T_c→0。旋节线上定压热容和等温压缩率均发散，旋节线极值点即临界点。临界温度以下Gibbs自由能出现多个热力学分支，不同视界态通过温度、压力和Gibbs自由能相等实现相共存。临界指数为(0,1/2,1,3)，属于标准平均场普适类。归一化Ruppeiner曲率在旋节线上精确发散，临界等温线和临界等容线上分别呈R_N~-|v-v_c|^{-4}和R_N~-|t|^{-2}的临界标度律。临界膨胀尺度为H_c^2=2(√5-2)|β|，表明临界点出现在熵变形量级为1时。

Conclusion: 尽管状态方程具有非代数形式，但该系统的临界性质仍属于标准平均场普适类，这为宇宙学表观视界的有效热力学态空间建立了自洽的临界结构。研究结果说明量子引力熵修正的引入不会改变临界普适性，但会对临界点的具体位置和热力学几何性质产生重要影响。

Abstract: We investigate thermodynamic criticality in a spatially flat Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker universe with a non-extensive Loop Quantum Gravity inspired entropy on its apparent horizon. Using the full Kodama-Hayward temperature and the unified first law, we derive the modified Friedmann dynamics and construct the corresponding horizon equation of state. We see that a finite physical critical point only appears in the non-extensive branch $q>1$, while in the Bekenstein-Hawking limit $q\to1$, the critical point is continuously pushed to $v_c\to\infty$ and $T_c\to0$. Along the same spinodal curve, both the constant-pressure heat capacity and the isothermal compressibility become unbounded, and the extremum of this curve is located at the critical point. Below the critical temperature, the Gibbs free energy develops several thermodynamic branches, and the phase coexistence for different horizon states is verified by the equality of temperature, pressure, and Gibbs free energy. The critical exponents are $(α_{cr},β_{cr},γ_{cr},δ_{cr}) =(0,1/2,1,3)$, indicating that the system belongs to the standard mean-field universality class despite the non-algebraic form of the equation of state. The normalized Ruppeiner curvature diverges precisely on the spinodal curve and shows critical scaling $R_N\sim-|v-v_c|^{-4}$ on the critical isotherm, and $R_N\sim-|t|^{-2}$ on the critical isochore. Finally, the critical expansion scale is given by $H_c^2=2(\sqrt{5}-2)|β|$, which implies that the thermodynamic critical point occurs when the entropy deformation is of order unity. These results establish a self-consistent critical structure for the effective thermodynamic state space of the cosmological apparent horizon and retain the mean-field critical universality.

</details>


### [160] [Compact Core--Shell Equilibria in Gravitational Vlasov--Poisson Systems with Positive and Negative Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27900)
*Naoki Sato*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对引力Vlasov–Poisson系统（含正负质量分布）构造了规则、球对称、紧凑的能量截断平衡态。研究发现平衡态内部结构取决于负质量的定义方式：在Bondi约定下，平衡态由中心重叠加核和有限正质量壳层及外部真空构成；在引力电荷约定下，系统空间分离为负质量核、真空间隙、正质量壳层和外部真空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源于Bondi的负质量概念及带符号质能源的爱因斯坦引力弱场极限之间的联系，作者希望通过动力学框架研究负质量在恒星动力学中能否形成稳定构型，以及不同负质量约定如何影响平衡态结构。

Method: 采用引力Vlasov–Poisson系统，在恒星动力学背景下引入正负质量分布，构造球对称、规则、紧凑的能量截断平衡态。对于截断指数n=-1/2的情形给出显式径向匹配解，对规则截断n=1/2的情形则通过Lane–Emden型方程描述物质区域。

Result: 在Bondi约定下，负质量遵循等效原理且与正质量同号翻转，平衡态为中心重叠加核（含两种质量种类）、有限正质量壳层和外部真空；纯Bondi负质量气体无法单独形成紧凑稳态，但可被合适的正质量分布空间约束。在引力电荷约定下，仅被动静质量和主动引力质量改变符号，两种物种遵循相反的自由落体定律且异号质量相互排斥，平衡态为空间分离的负质量核、真空间隙、正质量壳层和外部真空。

Conclusion: 这些结果提供了一个动力学框架，用于研究负质量在天体物理和宇宙学情境中的分布规律。

Abstract: We construct regular, spherically symmetric, compact energy-cutoff equilibria for gravitational Vlasov--Poisson systems sourced by positive and negative mass distributions in the context of stellar dynamics. Motivated by Bondi's notion of negative mass and its relation to the weak field limit of Einstein gravity with a signed mass--energy source, we show that the internal structure of the steady states depends qualitatively on the type of negative mass present. In the Bondi convention, the signs of inertial, passive gravitational, and active gravitational mass are reversed together, preserving the equivalence principle. The resulting equilibria consist of a central overlap core containing both mass species, surrounded by a finite positive mass shell and an exterior vacuum. Thus, although a pure Bondi negative mass gas cannot form a compact steady state, Bondi negative mass can be spatially confined by a suitable positive mass distribution. In the gravitational-charge convention, only the passive and active gravitational masses change sign, so that the two species obey opposite free-fall laws and unlike masses repel. The resulting equilibria are spatially segregated into a negative mass core, a vacuum gap, a positive mass shell, and an exterior vacuum. For cutoff exponent $n=-1/2$, the radial matching is explicit, while for the regular cutoff $n=1/2$ the matter regions satisfy Lane--Emden-type equations. These results provide a kinetic framework for investigating the distribution of negative mass in astrophysical and cosmological settings.

</details>


### [161] [Dynamical friction on black holes in scalar field environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28029)
*Adith Praveen,Dawood Kothawala*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟研究黑洞在具有质量 $m$ 的复标量场中运动时所受到的阻力，覆盖匀速与匀加速两种情形。研究发现匀速情形下阻力在后期饱和，且其速度依赖关系不同于已知形式；匀加速情形下阻力先增大至最大值 $F_\star \approx 0.3\,ma$ 后急剧减小，且密度尾迹（density wake）出现时间间隔递减的复兴现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天体物理中的黑洞并非处于真空环境，其穿越周围介质时通过动力学摩擦（dynamical friction）和邦迪吸积（Bondi accretion）等过程受到作用力。现有文献主要研究匀速运动情形，而匀加速运动下的黑洞受力尚缺乏系统性数值探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用数值模拟方法。对于匀加速情形，使用 C 度量（C-metric）建模黑洞运动；对于匀速情形，采用带附加无散度、无涡度速度场的 Painlevé-Gullstrand 度量模拟。两种情形均针对质量 $m$ 的复标量场中运动的黑洞进行阻力计算。

Result: 匀速运动情形下，阻力在晚期达到饱和，其饱和值对速度的依赖关系与既有文献所知的依赖形式不同；匀加速情形下，阻力先增大到最大值 $F_\star$（约等于 $0.3\,ma$），随后急剧减小；该情形下密度尾迹表现出复兴（revival）现象，且相邻复兴之间的时间间隔逐渐缩短。

Conclusion: 两种运动模式下黑洞所受阻力均呈现此前未被发现的非平凡行为，尤其是匀加速情形下阻力峰值的量级关系及密度尾迹的周期性复兴，暗示黑洞与标量场相互作用中存在更丰富的物理机制，有待进一步理论解释。

Abstract: Astrophysical black holes exist within non-vacuum environments, and their motion through these environments generically result in a force on these black holes through processes such as dynamical friction and Bondi accretion. We explore these forces numerically for black holes moving through a complex scalar field of mass $m$ with constant acceleration $a$, while also revisiting the constant velocity case considered in the existing literature. The former is modeled by the C-metric, while we use Painlevé-Gullstrand metric with an additional divergence and vorticity free velocity field to mimic the constant velocity motion. Our simulations reveal several novel and interesting aspects of the drag force in both cases. For the constant velocity case, the force saturates at late times, with a value that depends on velocity in a manner distinct from known dependence. For the constant acceleration case, the force increases to a maximum value $F_\star$ and then reduces drastically, with $F_\star \approx 0.3~ m a$. Moreover, the density wake in this case shows revivals separated by decreasing time intervals.

</details>


### [162] [Devoured by a Hairy Gargantua: Probing Massive Scalar Charges with Non-minimal Curvature Coupling with Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28037)
*Leif Lui,Adrian Ka-Wai Chung,Alejandro Torres-Orjuela*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究表明，与曲率非最小耦合的超轻标量场（ultralight scalar field）可以赋予旋转黑洞稳态毛发（stationary hair）并改变周围轨道。在极端质量比旋近（extreme-mass-ratio inspiral）系统中，研究发现相对于广义相对论可产生约10弧度的引力波去相位（gravitational-wave dephasing），并利用费舍尔预报显示李萨探测器（LISA）中该参数可被高精度测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非最小耦合的超轻标量场在理论上可为黑洞提供额外毛发结构，这可能为检验广义相对论（general relativity）提供新的途径。作者希望通过研究这种毛发对极端质量比旋近轨道的影响，评估其对引力波信号的观测效应。

Method: 利用最近构建的带毛黑洞时空（hairy black-hole spacetimes），对极端质量比旋近的入轨过渡（transition to plunge）进行建模。设标量质量无量纲参数μM=0.01和0.1，曲率耦合常数ζ=10^-3和10^-4，计算相对广义相对论的引力波去相位；在μM=0.2处进行多模（multimode）费舍尔预报（Fisher forecast），利用相邻的毛解对μ取数值导数。

Result: 在约10^3圈轨道上，相对广义相对论产生约10弧度的引力波去相位。对于信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）约80的LISA源，ζ和μ的测量精度分别可达约0.2%和0.6%–1.1%。

Conclusion: 由非最小耦合标量场引起的黑洞毛发可对极端质量比旋近引力波信号产生显著影响，且该效应有望在LISA观测中高精度地约束标量场相关参数，为探测这类超越广义相对论的物理提供了可行性。

Abstract: Ultralight scalar fields nonminimally coupled to curvature can endow rotating black holes with stationary hair and alter nearby orbits. Using recently constructed hairy black-hole spacetimes, we model the transition to plunge of an extreme-mass-ratio inspiral. For scalar masses $μM=0.01$ and $0.1$ and dimensionless curvature coupling $ζ=10^{-3},10^{-4}$, we find $\mathcal{O}(10)$ rad of gravitational-wave dephasing relative to general relativity over $\mathcal{O}(10^3)$ orbits. A multimode Fisher forecast at $μM=0.2$, where neighbouring hair solutions permit a numerical $μ$-derivative, suggests that $ζ$ and $μ$ could be measured to precisions of $\simeq0.2\%$ and $0.6$--$1.1\%$, respectively, for a source with signal-to-noise ratio of about 80 in LISA.

</details>


### [163] [Photon Spheres and Shadows of Covariant Loop Quantum Black Holes in the general $μ$-scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28186)
*Yu Han,Meng Liu,Yongzhuang Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了两族协变量子修正黑洞度量（BH-I与BH-II）在一般μ-方案（general μ-scheme）下的黑洞阴影与光子球性质，给出了光子球半径、阴影半径及Lyapunov指数（Lyapunov exponent）的闭式表达式与小参数展开，并利用事件视界望远镜（EHT）观测对参数空间（ξ,s）施加了约束，发现BH-II的约束比BH-I严格约2至4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前文献通常将一般μ-方案中的幂律指数s固定为1（即μ̄方案），掩盖了更丰富的现象学。本工作将s推广到一般值（包括非整数），作为μ₀方案（s=0）与μ̄方案（s=1）之间的唯象插值，以揭示更完整的物理图景。

Method: 对BH-I与BH-II两类协变量子修正黑洞度量，系统推导了光子球半径、阴影半径与Lyapunov指数的闭式表达式及小参数解析展开；在（ξ,s）二维参数平面内进行系统扫描，分析阴影半径随参数的单调性行为，并结合EHT观测数据推导参数约束。

Result: BH-I的光子球对所有参数值存在，且在s=1处存在精确抵消使坐标位置恢复为r_ph=3M（与ξ无关）；BH-II的视界与光子球在（ξ,s）平面内均存在临界曲线，当ξ超过闭式临界值ξ_c^PS(s)时光子球消失。两类度量的光子球在所有物理参数空间内均解析证明为不稳定的（λ_ph>0）。阴影半径随s的变化对BH-II单调递减、对BH-I非单调。EHT约束下BH-II比BH-I严格约2至4倍。

Conclusion: 单一阴影测量在二维（ξ,s）参数空间中存在观测简并（observational degeneracy），可通过同时测量Lyapunov指数来打破该简并。一般μ-方案中的参数推广揭示了以往被遮蔽的丰富现象学，并为未来更高精度的黑洞阴影观测提供了理论预测基础。

Abstract: We investigate black hole shadows and photon sphere properties for two families of covariant quantum-corrected black-hole metrics (hereafter called BH-I and BH-II) formulated within a general $μ$-scheme, parameterised by a power-law exponent $s$ and an amplitude $ξ$. The extension to general (including non-integer) $s$ is a phenomenological interpolation between the $μ_0$-scheme ($s=0$) and $\barμ$-scheme ($s=1$) and reveals a rich phenomenology masked when $s$ is usually fixed to 1 in previous literature. For BH-I, the photon sphere exists for all parameter values and exhibits an exact cancellation at $s=1$ where the coordinate location $r_{\rm ph}=3M$ is restored for any $ξ$. For BH-II, both the horizon and the photon sphere exhibit critical curves in the $(ξ,s)$ parameter plane; the photon sphere disappears when $ξ$ exceeds a closed-form critical value $ξ_c^{\rm PS}(s)$. We prove analytically that the photon sphere is always unstable ($λ_{\rm ph}>0$) throughout the physical parameter space of both metrics, and derive closed-form expressions for the Lyapunov exponent. Systematic parameter scans reveal that for fixed $ξ$ the shadow radius decreases monotonically with $s$ for BH-II and non-monotonically for BH-I. We derive small-parameter analytic expansions for the photon-sphere radius, shadow radius, and Lyapunov exponent, and demonstrate that a single shadow measurement suffers an observational degeneracy in the two-dimensional $(ξ,s)$ space; the degeneracy can be broken by a simultaneous measurement of the Lyapunov exponent. Using Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) measurements, we derive constraints on the $(ξ,s)$ parameter space and find that BH-II is constrained roughly $2$--$4$ times more tightly than BH-I.

</details>


### [164] [Global structure of Kerr-Newman-BR spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28265)
*Hryhorii Ovcharenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kerr-Newman-BR时空及其子情形（如Schwarzschild-BR时空）的全局结构，通过引入倒数坐标 y=1/r 证明整个Kerr-Newman-BR时空族可通过 r→∞ 表面连续延拓，并指出延拓后的共形结构取决于时空是否旋转以及所选取的极角方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前研究发现 r→∞ 并非真正的共形无穷远（conformal infinity），因此需要进一步探索该时空族在 r→∞ 表面的全局几何性质，以理解其整体结构。

Method: 采用倒数坐标（reciprocal coordinate）y=1/r，对Kerr-Newman-BR时空族及其子情形（包括Schwarzschild-BR时空）进行坐标变换，考察穿过 r→∞ 表面的连续延拓性质，并结合是否存在旋转参数以及极角 θ 的选取（一般极角、赤道面附近或两极附近）进行分类讨论。

Result: 整个Kerr-Newman-BR时空族可以通过 r→∞ 表面进行连续延拓；但延拓后的共形结构依赖时空类型（是否存在旋转）以及所固定的极角方向（一般极角 θ、赤道面 θ=π/2 附近或极点 θ=0, π 附近）。

Conclusion: 讨论了这种连续延拓的普适性（universality），表明其可能适用于更广泛的时空类别。

Abstract: We conduct an investigation of the global structure of Kerr-Newman-BR spacetime and its various subcases such as Schwarzschild-BR spacetime. In this work, we extend the result of our previous studies, where we found that $r\to \infty$ is not the proper conformal infinity. By using the reciprocal coordinate $y=1/r$, we show that the whole family of Kerr-Newman-BR spacetimes can be continuously extended through the surface $r\to \infty$. However, the conformal structure after passing $r\to\infty$ strongly depends on the type of spacetime one is considering (either rotation is present, or not) and on the polar direction one chooses to be fixed (namely, whether we consider generic polar angle $θ$, or we focus on the behaviour near the equatorial plane $θ=π/2$, or near the poles $θ=0,π$). Finally, we discuss the universality of such a continuous extension.

</details>
